The surrogate is designed so that the expected value of the next frontier depends on the offspring generative model only through finite-dimensional summaries that are amortized offline, and so that the resulting per-round objective is monotone with diminishing returns. Together, these two properties make planning tractable: the deterministic backup eliminates Monte-Carlo sampling, and the diminishing-returns structure lets a marginal greedy allocation achieve a \((1-1/e)\)-approximation for the per-round problem. On a simulation environment calibrated to a real respondent-driven sampling dataset, GFP outperforms random, reinforcement-learning, and i.i.d.\ dynamic-programming baselines across four discount factors.

</details>


### [128] [Pre-Intervention Prediction of Sparse Autoencoder Steering Side Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08365)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预干预筛选框架，通过SAE特征统计量预测特征干预的副作用（稳定性和附带影响），在多个模型和字典上验证了预测有效性，但最优预测因子和效果因模型/字典设置而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE特征干预通常不干净，同一干预在不同上下文中表现不一致且会扰动无关特征，缺乏提前预测副作用的方法。

Method: 提出预干预筛选框架，从特征统计量（解码器几何、激活统计量、共激活结构、直接logit足迹等）预测特征干预的模块化程度（效果稳定性和附带扩散两个维度），在GPT-2-small、Pythia-70M、Gemma-2-2B、Llama-3.1-8B和ReLU/JumpReLU/TopK SAE字典上进行评估。

Result: 解码器几何、激活统计量、共激活结构和直接logit足迹能比频率和激活幅度基线更好地预测模块化性；信号在GPT-2-small、Pythia-70M和Llama-3.1-8B中最强（残差化后仍存在），在Gemma-2-2B中较弱。筛选可选出更干净的未见特征，但最优提升维度因模型而异。

Conclusion: SAE特征干预的副作用可以提前预测，但有效预测因子和可迁移的模块化维度依赖于模型和字典设置。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features are increasingly used to steer language models, but feature steering is rarely clean: the same intervention can behave inconsistently across contexts and perturb unrelated features. We introduce a pre-intervention screening framework for forecasting SAE steering side effects from feature statistics computed before steering. We operationalize side effects along two axes of steering modularity, effect stability and collateral spread, and evaluate GPT-2-small, Pythia-70M-deduped, Gemma-2-2B, and Llama-3.1-8B across ReLU, JumpReLU, and TopK SAE dictionaries. Across these settings, decoder geometry, activation statistics, co-activation structure, and direct-logit footprint predict steering modularity better than frequency-only and activation-magnitude baselines. The signal is strongest in GPT-2-small, Pythia-70M, and Llama-3.1-8B, where it survives residualization against magnitude-related confounds, and weaker in Gemma-2-2B. Held-out screening shows that ranking unseen features by predicted cleanliness can select features that steer more cleanly on fresh contexts, but the successful axis varies by setting: GPT-2 improves most cleanly, Pythia improves mainly on stability, Llama mainly on collateral, and Gemma only partially. A controlled Llama Scope width comparison shows that the predictive signal persists under a 32K-to-128K dictionary-width change, although the screening payoff becomes less stable. Overall, SAE steering side effects are predictable in advance, but the useful predictor signature and transferred modularity axis are model- and dictionary-setting dependent.

</details>


### [129] [An Information-Theoretic Definition for Open-Ended Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08369)
*Wanqiao Xu,Yifan Zhu,Benjamin Van Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的开放学习定义，通过"比特等价"概念量化达到不同预期奖励水平所需的信息，并证明经典赌博机环境不具备开放性，同时提出了一种能在开放环境中实现持续学习的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管开放学习在人工智能领域展现出巨大潜力，但缺乏对开放性的统一定义以及代理应如何探索开放环境的相关理论。

Method: 引入"比特等价"这一信息论概念，量化达到各预期奖励水平所需的信息量，将环境开放性定义为代理能实现线性比特等价增长；分析经典赌博机环境的局限性，并构造一个具有开放性的赌博机环境；提出相应的开放学习算法。

Result: 经典赌博机环境不具备开放性；所提出的赌博机环境具有开放性；所提算法能在该环境中实现开放学习。

Conclusion: 本文为开放学习提供了信息论基础定义和理论框架，并展示了在简单环境中实现开放学习的可行性。

Abstract: A growing body of work points to the great promise of AI systems that can continually expand their capabilities as they operate in an open-ended environment. But yet there is no coherent definition of open-endedness or theory about how an agent ought to explore an open-ended environment. We introduce an information-theoretic definition based on a new concept -- the ${\textit bit-equivalent}$ -- which quantifies the information required to attain each level of expected reward. We consider an environment to be open-ended if an agent can attain linear growth in the bit-equivalent. We establish that classical bandit environments are not open-ended and formulate a bandit environment that is. We also introduce an algorithm that achieves open-ended learning in this environment.

</details>


### [130] [Few-step Cofolding with All-Atom Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08375)
*Gianluca Scarpellini,Ron Shprints,Peter Holderrieth,Juno Nam,Pranav Murugan,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli,Tommi Jaakola,Maruan Al-Shedivat,Nicholas Matthew Boffi,Avishek Joey Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeCAF框架，通过将最先进的共折叠扩散模型蒸馏为流映射（flow map），在仅需少量推理步数的情况下生成高质量的3D生物分子复合物结构。该框架结合基于去噪器的流映射公式、端点损失和SE(3)刚性对齐，并引入推理时奖励引导搜索，在蛋白-配体对接任务上显著优于现有方法，且速度提升5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全原子水平的3D生物分子复合物生成通常需要昂贵的迭代扩散过程，导致部署和推理时搜索计算成本过高。现有方法难以在保持高质量的同时实现高效推理。

Method: 1) 基于去噪器的流映射公式，结合端点损失并自然支持SE(3)刚性对齐；2) 推导变量变换使DeCAF能在EDM风格架构的σ-space噪声调度中运行，实现直接从预训练共折叠扩散模型蒸馏；3) 引入基于奖励引导搜索的推理时框架，利用流映射的前瞻能力改进采样。

Result: DeCAF-Boltz在RMSD和物理有效性指标上优于Boltz-1x；DeCAF-Pearl在成功率上与其教师模型持平，但仅需1/5的NFE。在PoseBusters上展示出更优的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: DeCAF成功将扩散模型蒸馏为高效流映射，大幅降低推理成本的同时保持或提升性能，为生物分子结构预测的实用化部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: All-atom generative modeling of 3D biomolecular complexes has emerged as the dominant paradigm for predicting the structure of proteins and protein-ligand systems. Generating structures at the atomic level of fidelity, however, typically requires expensive iterative diffusion rollouts, making both conventional deployment and inference-time search techniques computationally costly. In this paper, we introduce the Denoiser Cofolding All-Atom Flowmap (DeCAF) framework for distilling state-of-the-art all-atom cofolding models into all-atom flow maps that produce high-quality samples in only a few inference steps. We build DeCAF on a denoiser-based formulation of flow maps with endpoint losses that naturally support SE(3) rigid alignment, which we show is critical for training accurate models. We further derive a simple change of variables that lets DeCAF operate in the σ-space noise schedule of EDM-style architectures, enabling direct distillation from pretrained cofolding diffusion models. Equipped with DeCAF's flowmap lookahead, we introduce a purpose-built inference-time framework that improves sampling through reward-guided search. Empirically, DeCAF-Boltz statistically improves over Boltz-1x in both accuracy (RMSD) and physical validity scores of protein-ligand poses at strict NFE budgets on the challenging Runs N' Poses, while also showing a more optimal Pareto frontier across all inference compute budgets on PoseBusters. Distilling the state-of-the-art Pearl cofolding model, DeCAF-Pearl outperforms diffusion-based cofolding models and matches its teacher on success rate while using 5x fewer NFEs. We release our code at https://github.com/genesistherapeutics/decaf.

</details>


### [131] [RiskNet: A large-scale dataset of AI risk incidents from news with alignment and multi-dimensional annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08376)
*Leihan Zhang,Wecheng Ye,Xianlong Ma,Haochuan Liu,Yang Li,Qianyu Zhang,Jinliang Chen,Qiang Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RiskNet，一个从大规模多语言新闻来源构建的AI风险事件大规模数据集。该数据集通过结构化流水线进行AI风险新闻识别、事件级报告筛选、事件对齐和多维分类，最终形成以事件为中心的结构化记录，包含事件分类、事件对齐和风险标签标注的基准数据集，并支持在线平台浏览和探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理框架多停留在高层原则层面，缺乏大规模、可计算的实证资源来持续追踪和分析真实世界中的AI风险事件。现有的事件集合多为人工整理、规模较小，难以支撑数据驱动的持续监控和下游计算分析。

Method: 构建了一个结构化流水线，包括：1）大规模多语言新闻来源的AI风险新闻识别；2）事件级报告筛选；3）事件对齐（incident alignment）；4）多维事件分类。最终产出包含事件簇（incident clusters）和标注基准子集（annotated benchmark subsets）。

Result: 覆盖数亿条源记录，生成了大规模的AI风险相关报告集合，包括对齐的事件簇和标注基准子集，并提供在线平台供浏览和探索。

Conclusion: RiskNet通过提供结构化、可复用的实证资源，弥合了高层治理原则与AI风险事件文档化现实之间的鸿沟，可支持AI安全、治理、风险分析和基准测试等下游研究，以及纵向和跨来源的AI危害分析。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed across socially consequential domains, reports of AI-related harms and failures have grown in frequency and diversity. Although existing governance frameworks articulate high-level principles for responsible AI, large-scale empirical resources for tracking and analyzing real-world AI risk incidents remain limited. Existing incident collections are often manually curated, relatively small in scale, and insufficient for continuous, data-driven monitoring and downstream computational analysis. To address this need, we present RiskNet, a large-scale dataset of AI risk incidents constructed from large-scale multilingual news sources. RiskNet applies a structured pipeline for AI risk news identification, event-level report screening, incident alignment, and multi-dimensional incident classification. The resulting resource organizes dispersed news reports into incident-centered records and provides benchmark datasets for event classification, incident alignment, and incident-level risk labeling. In its current release, RiskNet covers hundreds of millions of source records and yields a large-scale collection of AI risk-related reports, including aligned incident clusters and annotated benchmark subsets. The dataset is also accessible through an online platform for browsing and exploration. We describe the data sources, processing workflow, taxonomy design, and technical validation of the resource. RiskNet is intended to support downstream research on AI safety, governance, risk analysis, and benchmarking, as well as longitudinal and cross-source analyses of AI-related harms. By providing a structured and reusable empirical resource, RiskNet helps bridge the gap between high-level governance principles and the documented realities of AI risk incidents.

</details>


### [132] [STAR-KV: Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Soft Thresholding for Adaptive Rank Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08382)
*Priyansh Bhatnagar,Ashkan Moradifirouzabadi,Se-Hyun Yang,SeungJae Lee,Jungwook Choi,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAR-KV 提出了一种自适应低秩 KV 缓存压缩框架，通过可微分阈值机制实现细粒度秩选择、混合分解策略以及低秩感知混合精度量化，在多种 LLM 上达到 75% 压缩率，结合量化可实现 20 倍 KV 缓存缩减，并提供 6.9 倍注意力模块加速和 3.1 倍端到端生成吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩投影利用隐藏维度冗余压缩 KV 缓存具有潜力，但现有方法依赖固定或启发式秩选择，难以在激进压缩下保持低精度损失。

Method: 1) 可微分阈值机制，在注意力头和块级别实现最优秩选择；2) 混合分解策略，根据键/值投影的敏感性应用不同的低秩分解；3) 低秩感知混合精度量化，利用数据统计实现近无损低比特量化。

Result: 最高 75% KV 缓存压缩率；结合量化实现 20 倍整体 KV 缓存缩减；基于 Triton GPU 内核实现注意力模块 6.9 倍加速和 3.1 倍端到端生成吞吐提升。

Conclusion: STAR-KV 通过自适应细粒度秩控制、混合分解和低秩感知量化，实现了高效且精度损失极小的 KV 缓存压缩，显著提升了 LLM 推理效率。

Abstract: Low-rank projection has emerged as a promising approach for compressing the KV cache by exploiting hidden-dimension redundancy. However, prior methods rely on fixed or heuristic rank selection and struggle to achieve aggressive compression with minimal accuracy degradation. We propose STAR-KV, an adaptive low-rank KV cache compression framework with fine-grained rank control. STAR-KV encompasses 1) a differentiable thresholding mechanism that enables optimal rank selection at both attention-head and block levels, 2) a hybrid decomposition strategy that applies different low-rank factorizations according to the sensitivity of key and value projections, and 3) a low-rank-aware mixed precision quantization that leverages data statistics for near lossless low-bit quantization. Evaluated across multiple LLMs and benchmarks, STAR-KV achieves up to 75% KV cache compression and up to 20x overall KV cache reduction when combined with quantization. Enabled by custom Triton-based GPU kernels, STAR-KV delivers up to 6.9x speedup for the attention module and 3.1x end-to-end generation throughput. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/PriyanshBhatnagar/STAR-KV.

</details>


### [133] [The Spectral Dynamics and Noise Geometry of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08388)
*Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Mahmoud Abdelmoneum,Tomaso Poggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon通过将矩阵梯度替换为极因子UV^T来扁平化更新谱，在特定条件下具有熵最大化偏置，在欠定回归中趋于平谱，在矩阵感知实验中与梯度下降不同，在NanoGPT中优于AdamW但在小ViT中相反，表明其平谱偏置在需要保持多谱方向活跃时有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Muon优化器（将梯度替换为极因子UV^T）所带来的优化偏置，理解这种谱扁平化操作如何影响学习动态和最终模型性能。

Method: 理论分析（显式对齐假设下的熵最大化证明、欠定回归连续时间动态推导）、矩阵感知实验（对比归一化梯度下降）、小规模NanoGPT和ViT预训练实验。

Result: 1) 极更新在显式对齐假设下是单步熵最大化更新；2) 欠定回归中归一化谱趋于等非零奇异值；3) 固定Frobenius范数下Muon的区分态为平谱，而非核范数最小化的谱集中态；4) 实验排除简单梯度缩放解释，Muon不可被归一化梯度下降复现；5) NanoGPT中Muon优于AdamW，小ViT中相反。

Conclusion: Muon并非普遍优越，其平谱偏置在需要保持多个谱方向活跃时有益，性能高度依赖具体任务和模型结构。

Abstract: Muon replaces a matrix gradient $G=UΣV^\top$ by its polar factor $UV^\top$. This keeps the singular directions selected by the gradient, but makes the update spectrum flat. We study the optimization bias created by this operation. Under explicit alignment assumptions, we prove that the polar update is the one-step entropy-maximizing choice among bounded updates that use the gradient singular directions and do not adapt to the current weight spectrum. In an underdetermined regression model, we derive exact singular-value dynamics for continuous-time Muon and identify a measurement-dependent condition under which the normalized spectrum moves toward equal nonzero singular values. This geometry also rules out a common low-rank interpretation: at fixed Frobenius norm, Muon's distinguished state has a flat spectrum, whereas nuclear-norm minimization favors spectral concentration. Controlled matrix-sensing experiments separate the effect from simple gradient rescaling, show that norm-matched gradient descent does not reproduce Muon, and recover the predicted flattening trend across broad ablations. In small NanoGPT pretraining, Muon preserves stable rank, has a broad learning-rate plateau, and improves validation loss relative to AdamW; in a matched small-ViT control, the ranking reverses. The resulting picture is regime-dependent: Muon is not universally superior, but its flat-spectrum bias can help when many spectral directions need to remain active.

</details>


### [134] [When Are Neural Interaction Discoveries Real? Identifiability, Recoverability, and a Pre-Fit Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08390)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经时间序列模型中交互作用的可识别性问题，指出交互作用的可恢复性取决于输入支持集的几何结构（联合滞后块协方差的有效秩），而非模型架构。通过乘法门控神经加法向量自回归模型（GNAVAR），作者证明了依赖输入会导致边特定交互项之间的泄漏，低维支持集允许不同的交互分解。理论给出了可识别性条件，并提出有效秩作为拟合前的实用诊断工具，以及两种子稳定性检查作为操作测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经时间序列模型报告一个变量调节另一个变量对目标的影响时，发现的交互作用是数据本身的属性还是模型灵活性的伪影？这是一个可识别性问题，现有研究缺乏对交互作用可恢复性前提条件的理论理解。

Method: 在乘法门控扩展的神经加法向量自回归模型（GNAVAR）框架下进行理论分析。证明了表示能力不等于可识别性，推导了归一化最小GNAVAR分解在显式支持条件下的总体可识别性定理，提出了联合滞后块协方差的有效秩作为拟合前诊断工具，以及两种子稳定性检查作为操作测试。

Result: 交互作用的可恢复性取决于支持集的几何结构，而非具体神经架构。有效秩提供了实用的拟合前诊断指标，跨独立拟合的不稳定性是不可识别交互发现的典型特征。支持条件将经验结果组织成理论预测的三种状态。

Conclusion: 可识别性现象、支持条件和失稳特征是模型无关的，GNAVAR只是使它们可证明的载体。交互作用可恢复性由支持几何决定，有效秩是实用的预拟合诊断，跨拟合的不稳定性是不可识别性的标志性信号。

Abstract: When a neural time-series model reports that one variable modulates another's effect on a target, is the discovered interaction a property of the data or an artifact of model flexibility? We argue that this is fundamentally a question of identifiability, governed by the geometry of the observed input support rather than by the specific neural architecture. We study the problem in a multiplicative-gating extension of neural additive vector autoregression (GNAVAR), in which source contributions are modulated by other lagged variables. We show that representational capacity is not identifiability: dependent inputs induce leakage between edge-specific interaction terms, and low-dimensional support permits distinct interaction decompositions that agree on the observed data while differing elsewhere. We then prove a population identifiability theorem for normalized minimal GNAVAR decompositions under explicit support conditions, including settings with shared modulators. The theory yields a simple practitioner-facing diagnostic: the effective rank of the joint lag-block covariance predicts, before fitting, whether interaction recovery is feasible for a given candidate set. When the candidate set is unknown, a two-seed stability check provides a practical operational test. The same support condition organizes empirical outcomes into the three states predicted by the theory. Our results show that interaction recoverability depends on support geometry, that effective rank provides a practical pre-fit diagnostic, and that instability across independent fits is a characteristic signature of non-identifiable interaction discovery. The identifiability phenomenon, the support condition, and the instability signature are model-agnostic; GNAVAR is the vehicle that makes them provable.

</details>


### [135] [Provably Efficient Personalized Multi-Objective Bandits with Proactive Conversational Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08410)
*Linfeng Cao,Ming Shi,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MO-PQUCB算法，通过融合用户主动查询（如“便宜又干净的酒店”）提供的结构化偏好信号与多目标摇臂赌博机的反馈，利用Plackett-Luce子集选择模型建模查询，并设计移位不变正则化与双探索UCB机制，克服仅依赖查询学习的移位不变性障碍，证明主动查询能加速偏好估计并改善遗憾界，同时在查询被污染时给出鲁棒估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅从效用反馈中推断用户偏好，将偏好学习与奖励探索纠缠；然而实际中用户常通过主动查询（如“便宜又干净的酒店”）提示其偏好，这种结构化信号未被利用。

Method: 形式化基于主动查询的框架，用Plackett-Luce子集选择模型建模查询信号；提出MO-PQUCB混合算法，结合查询偏好锚定与赌博机反馈，通过移位不变正则化与双探索UCB实现；针对查询被污染的情况设计鲁棒估计器。

Result: 证明仅查询学习因移位不变性障碍不充分；主动查询加速偏好估计，改善偏好感知MO-MAB方法的遗憾界；在稀疏污染下鲁棒估计器达到近最优性能；实验验证理论与实践增益。

Conclusion: 主动查询信号有效提升了多目标摇臂赌博机中个性化决策的学习效率，且对查询污染具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Personalized decision-making in multi-objective bandits requires learning user-specific trade-offs among competing objectives. Since arm utility depends on both unknown rewards and unknown preferences, existing methods infer preferences only from utility feedback, entangling preference learning with reward exploration. In practice, however, users often reveal their priorities through proactive conversational queries (e.g., "cheap and clean hotel"), yet this structured signal is not leveraged. We formalize a proactive query-based framework in which user queries provide structured preference signals. Modeling these signals via a Plackett-Luce subset choice model, we show that query-only learning is insufficient due to a fundamental shift-invariance barrier. To resolve this, we introduce MO-PQUCB, a hybrid algorithm that integrates query-based preference anchoring with bandit feedback through shift-invariant regularization and dual-exploration UCB. We prove that proactive queries accelerate preference estimation and yield improved regret scaling over prior preference-aware MO-MAB methods. Under corrupted queries, we further characterize statistical limits and design a robust estimator achieving near-optimal performance when the corruption is sparse. Experiments validate both theoretical and practical gains.

</details>


### [136] [Sparrow: Sparse Rollout for Stable and Efficient Long-context RL of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08446)
*Yang Zhou,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Zhaofeng Sun,Zhuoming Chen,Souvik Kundu,Saket Dingliwal,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Aram Galstyan,Haizhong Zheng,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RLVR中稀疏注意力加速长序列生成时的稳定性-效率权衡问题，提出基于动态稀疏度调度（保持每token策略分布尾部统计量恒定）的方法，在Qwen3系列模型上实现2.0-2.4倍加速，并引入DistillSparse蒸馏机制进一步提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽强大但导致极长COT计算成本高昂，而稀疏注意力虽能加速但存在稳定性-效率权衡：过于激进会导致训练崩溃，过于保守则加速不足。本文旨在找到平衡点并实现稳定高效训练。

Method: (1) 观察到稀疏rollout崩溃并非由所有token均匀退化导致，大多数稀疏token即使在激进稀疏下也能与dense对齐；(2) 提出假设：若轨迹中每token actor-policy mismatch的下尾统计量保持高于临界阈值，稀疏rollout训练即稳定；(3) 引入动态稀疏度调度，在生成过程中保持该尾部统计量恒定；(4) 使用成本模型在该阈值下寻找最大加速比的稀疏度调度；(5) 提出DistillSparse：基于轻量LoRA的蒸馏方法，使更激进的稀疏达到相同mismatch阈值，提升加速比。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上分别实现2.2x、2.4x、2.0x的rollout加速；阈值泛化至Qwen3-14B及编程领域；DistillSparse进一步提升加速效果。

Conclusion: 保持每token mismatch尾部统计量恒定可稳定训练稀疏rollout，结合成本模型可找到最优加速调度，DistillSparse进一步释放更激进稀疏的潜力。

Abstract: Despite being powerful, reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) induces extremely long COT, making it computationally expensive. Since RLVR per-step cost is dominated by long-context rollout generation, sparse attention offers a promising way to accelerate dense rollout. However, sparse rollouts require a delicate stability-efficiency tradeoff: overly aggressive sparsity causes collapse, while overly lenient sparsity gives insufficient speedup. In this work, we study this tradeoff through sparse-to-dense actor-policy mismatch. We first observe that sparse rollout collapse is not driven by uniform degradation across tokens: most sparse tokens align perfectly with dense even under aggressive sparsity. Motivated by this, we hypothesize that sparse rollout training remains stable if the lower tail of per-token actor-policy mismatch stays above a critical threshold throughout the trajectory. We introduce a dynamic sparsity schedule that keeps this tail statistic constant during generation and validate our hypothesis. Across Qwen3 thinking-family models, keeping the tail mismatch statistic near a consistent threshold generally enables stable training. We then use a cost model to find the sparsity schedule for maximum speedup under this mismatch threshold, achieving 2.2x, 2.4x, and 2.0x rollout speedups when training Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B. Empirically, we show the thresholds generalize to a larger model (Qwen3-14B) and another RL domain (coding). Finally, our analysis naturally motivates DistillSparse: lightweight LoRA-based distillation on sparse rollout lets more aggressive sparsity reach the same sparse-to-dense mismatch threshold, yielding higher speedup.

</details>


### [137] [Not Just After One: Sleep-Inspired Replay Prevents Catastrophic Forgetting After Sequential Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08447)
*Anthony Bazhenov,Jean Erik Delanois,Giri P. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受生物启发的持续学习方法，允许神经网络在多个新任务上顺序训练，然后通过无监督的类睡眠回放阶段部分恢复所有先前学习任务的性能，解决了传统神经网络连续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络在连续学习时面临灾难性遗忘问题，即训练新任务会干扰并覆盖先前学到的知识。现有算法通常在每轮新训练期间或之后立即应用保护机制，而人类和动物则能够先主动学习多个新记忆，再通过睡眠等机制将其巩固到长期存储中。作者希望弥合这一差距，探索更接近生物学习的持续学习范式。

Method: 顺序训练多个新任务，然后在训练完成后应用一个无监督的类睡眠回放阶段（sleep-like replay phase），该阶段不依赖任务标签或额外监督信号，通过回放机制部分恢复所有先前学习任务的性能。

Result: 1) 无监督的类睡眠回放可以部分恢复多个先前学习任务的性能；2) 任务特定信息对新训练的干扰具有一定弹性，但会随着网络在新任务上的持续训练而逐渐衰减。

Conclusion: 该研究为开发更广泛的持续学习AI解决方案提供了新的原理性指导，表明将主动学习与后续的巩固阶段分离是一种可行的持续学习策略。

Abstract: One of the critical limitations of artificial neural networks is their lack of ability to continually learn: training on new tasks often leads to interference and forgetting of the previous ones. While several algorithms have been proposed to protect old memories from interference, they are typically applied during or immediately after each new episode of training. In contrast, humans and animals can learn continuously, acquiring multiple new memories during active learning before consolidating all of them into long-term storage. Here we show that multiple new tasks can be trained sequentially before an unsupervised sleep-like replay phase is applied to partially restore performance across all previously learned tasks. Our study further suggests that task-specific information remains resilient to new training but decays gradually as network is trained on new tasks. These findings point to novel principles for developing a broad range of continual learning AI solutions.

</details>


### [138] [Theoretical Foundations of Continual Learning via Drift-Plus-Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08452)
*Nazreen Shah,Govinda Arya,Bharath B. N.,Ranjitha Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于漂移-惩罚（DPP）原则的持续学习框架COLD，通过控制理论视角显式调节遗忘演化，利用虚拟队列追踪长期稳定性偏差，在标准基准上优于现有方法并提供可调控的稳定性-可塑性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流非平稳且顺序到达，持续学习需在整合新任务的同时缓解灾难性遗忘，现有方法缺乏对遗忘演化的显式调控机制。

Method: 基于随机优化的漂移-惩罚（DPP）原则，在回放式持续学习中引入虚拟队列追踪先前任务的长期稳定性偏差，通过可调控制参数实现稳定性-可塑性权衡的显式调控。

Result: 在标准基准上，COLD持续优于多种最先进的持续学习方法，同时通过参数调节实现竞争性且可控的遗忘行为。

Conclusion: 控制理论视角为持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡提供了显式调控框架，COLD通过虚拟队列和DPP原则实现了遗忘过程的可控演化与理论保证。

Abstract: In many real-world settings, data streams are nonstationary and arrive sequentially, requiring learning systems to adapt continuously without retraining from scratch. Continual learning (CL) addresses this challenge by incorporating new tasks while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, where learning new information degrades performance on previously acquired knowledge. We introduce a control-theoretic perspective on CL that explicitly regulates the evolution of forgetting, framing adaptation as a controlled process subject to long-term stability constraints. We focus on replay-based CL, where a finite memory buffer stores representative samples from prior tasks. We propose COntinual Learning with Drift-Plus-Penalty (COLD), a continual learning framework based on the Drift-Plus-Penalty (DPP) principle from stochastic optimization. To facilitate analysis, we also consider an oracle variant, COLD-ORACLE, as a reference benchmark. At each task, both methods minimize the current task loss while maintaining a virtual queue that tracks deviations from long-term stability on previously learned tasks, capturing the stability-plasticity trade-off as a regulated dynamical process. We establish stability and convergence guarantees that characterize this trade-off through a tunable control parameter. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that COLD consistently outperforms a broad range of state-of-the-art CL methods while providing competitive and controllable forgetting behavior through explicit regulation of stability and plasticity.

</details>


### [139] [Beyond Linear Activation Steering: Invertible Latent Transformations for Controlling LLM Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08454)
*Tuc Nguyen,Thai Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INNSteer提出了一种基于可逆神经网络的非线性激活引导框架，通过学习从原始激活空间到潜空间的非线性映射，在潜空间中进行线性控制后再逆变换回原始空间，从而实现对大型语言模型行为的输入依赖式非线性干预，在多个模型和行为基准上优于传统线性引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法将行为控制视为全局线性加性偏移，忽略了行为特征在激活空间中可能呈非线性变化或位于弯曲各向异性流形上，导致最优干预依赖于输入，存在局限性。

Method: 学习轻量级可逆神经网络φ将LLM激活映射到潜空间，在该空间中进行线性干预，再通过精确逆变换φ⁻¹将修改后的激活映射回原始空间，实现原始激活空间中的非线性、输入依赖式干预。

Result: 在多个LLM系列、规模、行为特质和安全基准实验上，INNSteer一致优于线性、基于传输和非线性引导基线，同时较好地保持了生成流畅性。

Conclusion: 可逆潜空间变换使简单的线性干预在原始空间中表现为输入相关的非线性操作，为激活引导提供了更灵活有效的控制范式。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight inference-time mechanism for controlling large language models (LLMs) by modifying their internal activation vectors toward desired behaviors. Most existing methods compute a fixed steering direction in the original activation space, typically from pairs of contrastive examples using mean differences, linear probes, or arbitrary separability criteria. While effective to a certain extent, these methods treat behavioral control as a global, linear, additive offset: the same direction is applied across inputs, and behaviors are linearly separable. This can be restrictive when behavioral features vary nonlinearly across the activation space or lie on curved and anisotropic manifolds, where the optimal intervention may be input-dependent. To address this limitation, we propose INNSteer, a nonlinear activation steering framework based on invertible latent transformations. Rather than searching for a better steering vector in the original representation space, INNSteer learns a lightweight invertible neural network $φ$ that maps an LLM's activations into a latent space where behavioral classes are more amenable to linear control. At inference time, activations are mapped through $φ$, steered in the latent space, and mapped back through the exact inverse transformation $φ^{-1}$. This makes a simple latent-space translation become a nonlinear, input-dependent intervention in the original activation space. Across experiment settings on multiple LLM families, scales, behavioral traits, and safety benchmarks, INNSteer consistently improves model control over linear, transport-based, and nonlinear steering baselines while largely preserving generation fluency.

</details>


### [140] [The Confidence Trap: Calibration Attacks for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08467)
*Cuong Dang,Jiahao Zhang,Hieu Ta Quang,Dung Le,Lu Cheng,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一图校准攻击框架（UGCA），用于评估图神经网络在对抗性结构扰动下的校准鲁棒性，通过KL散度损失、重排序机制、混合损失和波束搜索等方法，在白盒场景下显著提升期望校准误差而不影响分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管置信度校准对安全关键应用中的可信决策至关重要，但校准后的GNN对对抗性结构扰动的鲁棒性尚未被充分探索，且图上的校准攻击面临离散结构梯度优化困难、欠置信目标无法驱动均匀分布、以及边扰动易导致标签违反攻击约束等独特挑战。

Method: 提出UGCA框架，包括：(1) KL散度损失鼓励预测分布均匀化；(2) 重排序机制减少标签翻转；(3) 混合损失在标签违反时恢复标签；(4) 波束搜索扩展对抗搜索空间。同时提供理论分析连接模型泛化、数据集复杂度与校准脆弱性。

Result: 大量实验表明UGCA在保持分类精度的同时显著提高了期望校准误差（ECE），且高精度模型或训练于多类别数据集的模型更容易受到此类攻击。

Conclusion: UGCA框架有效揭示了GNN校准鲁棒性中的脆弱点，为安全关键场景下的可信图学习提供了评估工具。代码已开源。

Abstract: While confidence calibration is essential for trustworthy decision-making in safety-critical applications, the robustness of calibrated GNNs to adversarial structural perturbations remains largely unexplored. However, studying calibration attacks on graphs presents unique technical challenges: (1) the discrete nature of graph structures complicates gradient-based optimization, (2) existing underconfidence objectives fail to drive predictions toward uniform distributions, and (3) GNNs are highly sensitive to edge perturbations, often causing unintended label changes that violate attack constraints. To address these challenges, we propose a \textbf{Unified Graph Calibration Attack (UGCA)} framework designed for \textbf{worst-case (white-box) analysis} of GNN calibration robustness. UGCA introduces a KL-divergence loss to encourage uniform predictive distributions, a reranking mechanism to reduce label flipping, a hybrid loss to recover labels when violations occur, and beam search to explore a broader adversarial search space. We further provide theoretical insights linking model generalization, dataset complexity, and calibration vulnerability, showing that models with higher accuracy or trained on datasets with more classes are more susceptible under this threat model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UGCA substantially increases Expected Calibration Error while preserving classification accuracy. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/CaptainCuong/Graph-Calibration-Attack.git.

</details>


### [141] [Physically Consistent Null Space Alignment for Detection of Low-Magnitude False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08473)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理一致零空间对齐（PCNSA）框架，通过预处理保留物理零空间与测量导出伪零空间之间的几何对应关系，从而检测隐蔽的虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）。核心步骤是伪零空间守恒数据预处理（PSCP），它重新将测量表达在物理坐标框架中，使得SVD导出的伪零子空间与物理残差空间对齐，无需显式系统模型H。在IEEE 14-118总线系统上，该方法在逃逸传统检测器（XTM、LSTM、AE、Isolation Forest）的隐蔽攻击下仍能实现高F1分数和检测精度，且对部分可观测性和PMU噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型驱动和数据驱动检测器无法识别低幅值高影响的隐蔽FDIA：残差检验忽略伪零空间中的变化，子空间学习方法虽能捕获相关模式但未强制物理一致性。

Method: 提出PCNSA框架，核心为PSCP预处理步骤：将测量数据重新表达在物理坐标框架中后再进行子空间提取。证明PSCP能保持行空间与其正交补的分离性，使得SVD导出的伪零子空间与物理残差空间自然对齐。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、57、118总线系统上，PCNSA在逃逸XTM、LSTM、AE、Isolation Forest的隐蔽攻击下，仍获得更高的F1分数和检测准确率，且在部分可观测性和PMU噪声条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过保持物理零空间与测量伪零空间的几何对齐，PCNSA能有效检测传统方法无法识别的隐蔽FDIA，且无需显式系统模型知识。

Abstract: False data injection attacks (FDIAs) introducing small measurement perturbations can still cause large deviations in power system state estimation when the injected signals align with the pseudo-null space of the system model. Existing model- and data-driven detectors may fail to identify such low-magnitude but high-impact attacks because residual tests ignore changes hidden in the pseudo-null space, while subspace learning methods capture correlation patterns without enforcing physical consistency. This paper proposes Physically Consistent Null Space Alignment (PCNSA), a framework that detects stealthy FDIAs by preserving, through preprocessing, the geometric correspondence between the physical null space and the measurement-derived pseudo-null space. The key point is a Pseudo-null Space Conserved data Preprocessing (PSCP) step that re-expresses measurements in the physical coordinate frame before subspace extraction. We prove that PSCP preserves the separation between row space and its orthogonal complement, a property that conventional per-feature standardization violates. This keeps the singular value decomposition (SVD)-derived pseudo-null subspace aligned with the physical residual space without explicit knowledge of H. Experiments on IEEE 14-, 30-, 57-, and 118-bus systems confirm this principle in practice: stealthy attacks that evade XTM, LSTM, AE and Isolation Forest baselines appear as clear deviations in the aligned subspace, yielding higher F1-score and detection accuracy while remaining robust under partial observability and realistic PMU noise.

</details>


### [142] [Inferring hidden forcing in a biological oscillator using Kolmogorov-Arnold networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08479)
*Julian Szereszewski,Facundo Fainstein,Leandro E. Fernandez,Gabriel B. Mindlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络从鸟类气囊压力数据中重建了驱动呼吸动力学的有效肌肉力，揭示了压力信号中不明显的两相激活模式，并通过肌电图记录独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分观测数据推断驱动动力系统的力是物理学中的基本挑战，尤其是当不同机制产生相似的可观测动力学时。

Method: 基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络的可解释学习框架，直接从数据中推断系统控制方程。

Result: 重建的动力学预测了每个呼吸周期内的两相激活模式，并通过呼气肌的肌电图记录独立验证。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的动力学定律重建可以揭示隐藏的物理结构，并为部分观测动力系统中潜在力的推断提供通用途径。

Abstract: Inferring the forces that drive a dynamical system from partial observations is a fundamental challenge across physics, particularly when distinct underlying mechanisms produce similar observable dynamics. Here we show that the effective muscular forcing underlying avian respiratory dynamics can be reconstructed from measurements of air-sac pressure alone. Using an interpretable learning framework based on Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, we infer the governing equations of the system directly from data and uncover a nontrivial structure in the underlying forcing that is not apparent from the pressure signal, which instead suggests a relaxation-like oscillation. The reconstructed dynamics predict a two-phase activation pattern within each respiratory cycle, which we independently validate through electromyographic recordings of expiratory muscles. These results demonstrate that data-driven reconstruction of dynamical laws can reveal hidden physical structure and provide access to unobserved driving variables, establishing a general route to infer latent forces in partially observed dynamical systems.

</details>


### [143] [Adaptive Loss Balancing for Noise-Robust GRPO in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08480)
*Kewei Xu,Junbo Qi,Yanyan Zou,Pengfei Zhang,Xingzhi Yao,Shengjie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaGRPO框架，通过选择性策略梯度优化（而非统一应用）解决强化学习在生成式推荐中因奖励模型不可靠导致的噪声放大问题。该方法以监督负对数似然为主干，利用策略难度和奖励可区分性两个诊断指标对GRPO目标进行逐样本门控，在大型电商数据集上提升了召回率并抑制了幻觉，A/B测试中点击率和停留时间均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL用于生成式推荐时，通常假设奖励模型足够可信。但生产环境中的排序器（reward model）在曝光偏差日志上训练，导致样本依赖性的评估偏差。统一应用RL优化会对某些样本产生噪声放大甚至损害性能，缺乏对何时应当启用奖励引导的判别机制。

Method: 提出AdaGRPO框架：1) 以监督负对数似然（NLL）为训练主干；2) 引入两个逐样本诊断指标——策略侧难度（policy-side difficulty）和奖励可区分性（reward discriminability）；3) 根据诊断结果对GRPO目标进行二值门控，仅当两个指标均满足条件时才启用RL目标，否则退化为纯监督学习。

Result: 在大型电商数据集上，最佳中间检查点将HR@10从11.01%提升至12.18%，幻觉率控制在0.22%以下；最终检查点保持HR@10 11.63%、幻觉率0.27%，优于固定NLL-GRPO混合策略。在线A/B测试中点击率和停留时间均取得统计显著提升。

Conclusion: AdaGRPO验证了将奖励引导优化视为选择性准入而非统一压力的有效性，通过动态门控机制在检索性能与生成有效性之间取得更优权衡，为RL在推荐系统中的应用提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) presents a promising avenue for enhancing generative recommendation beyond supervised imitation, leveraging reward signals to guide policy improvement. However, its efficacy is critically contingent on the trustworthiness of the reward model for the samples it evaluates. In practice, production rankers, the widely adopted reward models, are trained on exposure-biased logs, leading to sample-dependent inaccuracies that violate this assumption. Our stratified analysis uncovers a consistent pattern: reward guidance is most beneficial when the policy exhibits uncertainty and the ranker can effectively discriminate the ground-truth item from rollout negatives. On other samples, the reward signal is either negligible or detrimental, highlighting the risk of uniform RL application. To address such an issue, we introduce AdaGRPO, a novel framework that treats reward-guided optimization as selective admission rather than uniform pressure. Training is anchored in supervised negative log-likelihood, while the GRPO objective is gated by a binary, per-sample clip determined by two rollout diagnostics: policy-side difficulty and reward discriminability. Instances failing either diagnostic default to pure supervision, ensuring stability and mitigating the amplification of noisy gradients. We validate AdaGRPO on a large-scale e-commerce dataset. At the best intermediate checkpoint, it elevates HR@10 from 11.01% to 12.18% while constraining hallucination below 0.22%, and maintains robustness at the final checkpoint (HR@10 11.63%, hallucination 0.27%), outperforming fixed NLL--GRPO mixtures across the retrieval--validity frontier. In production A/B tests, AdaGRPO achieves statistically significant gains in click-through rate and dwell time, confirming its practical utility.

</details>


### [144] [PIPE-Cypher: Automatic Enterprise Benchmark Generation for Text-to-Cypher Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08481)
*Suraj Ranganath,Anish Raghavendra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIPE-Cypher是一个能从实时属性图和种子查询中自动生成平衡、可执行、多样化的NL-to-Cypher基准测试的流水线，使用本地Qwen3.5-9B进行生成和评估，验证了3000个示例，并证明零样本迁移效果差而模式特定示例库有助于兼容模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业属性图的模式、术语、治理约束和交互模式差异极大，而现有Text2Cypher基准测试难以反映特定部署场景下的真实用户查询模式，因为每个图的模式和值是独一无二的，且图结构随时间变化。

Method: PIPE-Cypher流水线包含：模式分析、逆向查询接地、约束生成、确定性Cypher治理、执行验证、脱敏、多样性控制，以及基于本地Qwen3.5-9B的校准LLM评判器。

Result: 在FinBench/SNB上生成了3000个可接受示例，完成了三个消融套件审计，用人工标签校准了评判器行为，评估了11个本地下游模型。实验表明零样本迁移效果弱，而模式特定示例库有助于兼容模型族。

Conclusion: PIPE-Cypher使Text2Cypher基准测试成为一个可重复的过程，能够随图结构、用户和目标工作负载一同演化，解决了企业级部署中基准测试难以维护和适配的核心问题。

Abstract: Enterprise property graphs vary widely in schema structure, internal terminology, domain assumptions, governance constraints, and user interaction patterns. A deployment-relevant Text2Cypher benchmark therefore reflects the questions users and agents actually ask of that graph. Creating such a benchmark is difficult because schemas and values are unique, and graph structure changes over time. Each NL-query pair must also be executable, use real graph entities, preserve diversity, and remain balanced across query types and difficulty levels. We present PIPE-Cypher, a local benchmark-generation pipeline that turns a live property graph and optional seed queries from customer questions, analyst logs, or agent tool calls into balanced NL-to-Cypher benchmarks. PIPE-Cypher combines schema profiling, reverse-query grounding, constrained generation, deterministic Cypher governance, execution validation, redaction, diversity controls, and a calibrated local LLM judge. Using local Qwen3.5-9B generation and judging, PIPE-Cypher exports 3,000 accepted FinBench/SNB examples, completes three audited ablation suites, calibrates judge behavior with human labels, and evaluates 11 local downstream models. The resulting benchmark is deliberately discriminative: zero-shot transfer is weak, while a few-shot control shows that schema-specific example banks can help compatible model families. Together, PIPE-Cypher makes Text2Cypher benchmarking a repeatable process that evolves with the graph, its users, and its target workloads.

</details>


### [145] [STELLAR: Spatio-Temporal Environmental Learning with Latent Alignment and Refinement for Long-Tailed Species Distribution Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08484)
*Shufeng Kong,Tao Yu,Yuanyuan Wei,Caihua Liu,Junwen Bai,Yingheng Wang,Marc Grimson,Daniel Fink,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STELLAR是一个联合物种分布建模框架，通过图-时序编码器、上下文锚定潜在对齐和感知不平衡的解耦解码三个模块，联合优化动态生境上下文与群落结构，在eBird数据集上显著优于现有方法，尤其在稀有物种预测和可解释物种相互作用方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JSDM方法存在两大耦合挑战：环境驱动因素与物种分布固有的时空特性，以及物种共现模式中复杂的非线性群落结构和由稀有物种导致的严重长尾不平衡。现有方法通常孤立处理这些因素，如仅从静态协变量学习或忽略动态群落结构的历史轨迹。

Method: STELLAR框架包含三个互补组件：(1) 图-时序编码器，利用图注意力和循环单元聚合空间邻域效应并捕捉环境上下文与群落结构的共演历史动态；(2) 上下文锚定潜在对齐机制，通过标签激活的混合先验和监督对比学习构建潜在空间，基于共享环境偏好主动聚类物种；(3) 感知不平衡的解耦解码模块，利用非对称损失聚焦于困难稀有样本的学习，防止长尾中的模式崩溃。

Result: 在大规模eBird数据集上的实验表明，STELLAR显著优于最先进的基线方法，尤其在稀有物种预测方面表现突出，并能揭示可解释的物种相互作用。

Conclusion: STELLAR通过联合建模时空环境动态与群落结构，有效解决了JSDM中的长尾不平衡和复杂非线性共现模式问题，为生物多样性监测和保育规划提供了更准确的建模工具。

Abstract: Joint Species Distribution Modeling (JSDM) is a key enabler for biodiversity monitoring and conservation planning. However, accurate JSDM faces two coupled challenges: environmental drivers and species distributions are inherently spatio-temporal, while species co-occurrence patterns exhibit complex non-linear community structure and severe long-tail imbalance driven by rare species. Existing approaches often address these factors in isolation, learning from static covariates or neglecting the historical trajectories of dynamic community structure. To overcome these limitations, we propose STELLAR (Spatio-Temporal Environmental Learning with Latent Alignment and Refinement), a novel framework that learns a shared latent space where dynamic habitat context and community structure are optimized jointly. Our approach integrates three complementary components: (1) a Graph-Temporal Encoder that employs graph attention and recurrent units to aggregate spatial neighborhood effects and capture the co-evolving historical dynamics of environmental context and community structure; (2) a Context-Anchored Latent Alignment mechanism that structures the latent space using a label-activated mixture prior and supervised contrastive learning, actively clustering species based on shared environmental preferences; and (3) an Imbalance-Aware Decoupled Decoding module that utilizes Asymmetric Loss to focus learning on hard, rare species samples, preventing mode collapse in the long tail. Experiments on the large-scale eBird dataset, curated with domain experts, demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, particularly in predicting rare species and revealing interpretable species interactions.

</details>


### [146] [A Joint Finite-Sample Certificate for Adaptive Selective Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08517)
*Xiaoli Yu,Jiamiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对选择性预测器的有限样本联合认证方法，能同时上界选定风险、下界接受概率和下界部署效用，且支持自适应阈值选择。通过将选定风险直接视为比率并使用方差自适应的经验Bernstein界，相比Hoeffding方法将接受概率依赖从1/pmin优化到1/√pmin，在ImageNet和COCO上实现了+22pp的认证接受前沿和约10倍紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全部署选择性预测器需要一个单一的有限样本证书，能同时上界选定风险（selected risk）、下界接受概率（保证高于最小阈值pmin）以及下界部署效用（utility）。现有方法如Hoeffding风格的证书在样本效率上不够紧致，且缺乏对自适应阈值选择的联合控制。

Method: 将选定风险直接建模为比率而非通过Hoeffding范围界，构建三个置信界：方差自适应经验Bernstein界（用于比率风险）、Clopper-Pearson界（用于接受概率）、双边接近性界（用于效用）。支持从m个阈值对组成的有限网格中进行自适应选择，基于n_cert个样本进行联合认证。

Result: 相比基于范围的Hoeffding比率构造，将接受概率的依赖从1/pmin优化到1/√pmin。在ImageNet（三个ResNet）和COCO val 2017全景分割任务上，相比Hoeffding-CRC方法实现了+22pp的认证接受前沿，且比非空洞的匹配基线紧致约10倍。认证器运行时间为O(n_cert m)。

Conclusion: 所提证书在选择性预测器的安全部署中提供了更紧的有限样本保证，但增益具有场景特定性（在ADE20K上无提升），并非普适。论文还给出了一个闭式推论，识别了风险界优于Hoeffding conformal risk control (Hoeffding-CRC) 的每对阈值场景。

Abstract: Selective predictors answer on confident inputs and abstain elsewhere; deploying one safely needs a single finite-sample certificate that simultaneously upper-bounds the selected risk, lower-bounds the acceptance probability $\pacc$ above a floor $\pmin$, and lower-bounds the deployment utility. This certificate must be valid under adaptive threshold selection from a finite grid of $m$ pairs on $\ncert$ samples. We give such a certificate for bounded, possibly non-monotone losses by treating the selected risk directly as a ratio rather than through a Hoeffding-style range bound. The construction couples three confidence bounds: a variance-adaptive empirical-Bernstein bound on the ratio risk, a Clopper--Pearson bound on acceptance, and a two-sided closeness bound on utility. Together they lower-bound the certified policy's utility absolutely and to within $2\gammau$ of the best over the \emph{certified set}, both non-vacuous whenever feasible; a regime-scoped third leg matches an external oracle, informative only where the risk margin $\gammar < α$ and vacuous at the headline operating points. Relative to the range-only Hoeffding-ratio construction this sharpens the acceptance-floor dependence from $1/\pmin$ to $1/\sqrt{\pmin}$, and a closed-form corollary identifies a per-pair regime in which our risk bound dominates a Hoeffding conformal risk control (Hoeffding--CRC) selective bound. Empirically, on ImageNet (three ResNets) and COCO val 2017 panoptic, the certificate opens a $+22$ pp certified-acceptance frontier over Hoeffding--CRC and is ${\approx}10{\times}$ tighter than a non-vacuous matched-valid baseline; these gains are regime-scoped, not universal, and absent on ADE20K. The certifier runs in $O(\ncert m)$ time.

</details>


### [147] [Autonomous Aerial Manipulation via Contextual Contrastive Meta Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08533)
*Lixuan Jin,Bingxuan Lan,Xinyi Bao,Xiangyuan Xie,Chunjie Zhang,Zheng Chen,Tianshuo Liu,Ruijie Tian,Jinyu Ru,Gang Wang,Lei Yuan,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Aco2框架，通过上下文对比元强化学习实现四旋翼无人机自主抓取、运输和投递多样负载，无需人工校准或系统辨识，从仿真直接迁移到真实环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机自主操作假设预装负载或依赖专用夹爪，缺乏能在线适应不同负载动力学变化的端到端通用方案。

Method: 设计上下文观测编码器从交互历史推断紧凑隐变量以适应负载动力学；引入对比学习目标优化上下文嵌入，增强跨负载泛化；结合域随机化在仿真中训练。

Result: 完全在仿真中训练的策略可直接部署到物理四旋翼，无需真实世界微调。

Conclusion: Aco2实现了无需人工干预的自主空中操作，通过上下文对比元学习有效解决了负载变化带来的动力学适应问题。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly being deployed in logistics, service robotics, and other real-world applications, creating a growing demand for autonomous payload acquisition and delivery. Existing approaches typically assume pre-attached payloads or rely on specialized grippers, leaving versatile end-to-end aerial delivery largely unresolved, where different payloads induce highly variable flight dynamics, requiring a single policy to adapt online without manual calibration or explicit system identification. To this end, we study \textbf{A}utonomous \textbf{A}erial Manipulation via \textbf{Co}ntextual \textbf{Co}ntrastive Meta Reinforcement Learning (\textbf{\textit{Aco2}}), a fully autonomous aerial delivery setting in which a quadrotor equipped with a lightweight hook continuously picks up, transports, and delivers diverse handle-equipped objects between randomized locations, all without human intervention. First, we design a contextual observation encoder that infers a compact latent context from recent interaction history, enabling the policy to adapt online to payload-dependent dynamics. To further improve the quality of this context, we introduce a contrastive objective that structures the context embedding around task-relevant variations, improving generalization across diverse payloads without requiring explicit system identification. Trained entirely in simulation with extensive domain randomization, \textit{Aco2} can be directly deployed on a physical quadrotor without real-world fine-tuning.

</details>


### [148] [Routine laboratory trajectories encode the onset of organ-level complications in cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08538)
*Jannik Lübberstedt,Krischan Braitsch,Jacqueline Lammert,Christof Winter,Florian Gabriel,Tristan Lemke,Christopher Zirn,Markus Graf,Friedrich Puttkammer,Hartmut Häntze,Johannes Moll,Anirudh Narayanan,Andrei Zhukov,Fabian Drexel,Zeineb Ben Chaaben,Sebastian Ziegelmayer,Su Hwan Kim,Marion Högner,Jan Kirschke,Florian Bassermann,Marcus Makowski,Christian Wachinger,Lisa Adams,Keno Bressem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 基于Transformer的模型利用25万余患者、277万余次常规实验室检测数据，预测多发性骨髓瘤和卵巢癌患者两年内162种治疗相关并发症（含治疗相关骨髓增生异常综合征等8大类别），在群体层面实现1.5-6.1倍富集，AUROC提升最高达0.11，外部验证AUROC达0.85，证明纵向实验室轨迹可提前数周至数月预警器官功能恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预后工具仅利用单时间点实验室数据，丢弃了纵向检测中蕴含的时序结构信息，无法捕捉并发症发生的动态生理演变过程。

Method: 使用Transformer模型，对3,905名患者的2,777,595次实验室测量序列进行训练，预测8大临床类别共162种治疗相关并发症的两年内发生风险，并通过生物标志物掩码分析和外部验证（MIMIC-IV、MMRF CoMMpass）评估模型泛化能力。

Result: 模型在群体层面实现1.5-6.1倍富集，AUROC提升最高0.11，外部验证AUROC达0.85；预测能力跨癌种泛化，差异集中在疾病特异性并发症；生物标志物掩码恢复的征象与已知病理生理机制一致。

Conclusion: 常规肿瘤实验室数据编码了临床发作前数周至数月的器官功能恶化信息，无需额外检测基础设施即可实现并发症特异性监测。

Abstract: Routine laboratory panels drawn during cancer treatment constitute longitudinal physiological recordings of organ function, yet their temporal structure is discarded by single-timepoint prognostic tools. A transformer trained on 2,777,595 laboratory measurements from 3,905 patients with multiple myeloma or ovarian cancer predicted the two-year onset of 162 treatment-associated complications, including therapy-related myelodysplastic syndromes, spanning eight clinical categories, achieving 1.5- to 6.1-fold enrichment above prevalence at the group level. It matched or outperformed non-sequential baselines across grouped endpoints (AUROC gains up to +0.11), demonstrating that longitudinal laboratory trajectories capture evolving complication-specific physiology inaccessible from isolated measurements. Predictions generalised across both cancers, divergence concentrating in disease-specific complications, and biomarker masking recovered signatures consistent with established pathophysiology. External validation on MIMIC-IV and MMRF CoMMpass confirmed transferability across independent healthcare systems (AUROC up to 0.85). Routine oncological laboratory data encode organ deterioration weeks to months before clinical onset, enabling complication-specific surveillance without additional testing infrastructure.

</details>


### [149] [A Theoretical Analysis of Memory and Overfitting Phenomena in Stochastic Interpolation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08554)
*Yunchen Li,Shaohui Lin,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了随机插值模型（如扩散模型）在连续时间条件下的记忆效应，即生成过程会恢复训练样本；在欧拉离散化下，生成样本围绕训练样本分布且偏差受步长控制；存在估计误差时，累积误差控制生成样本与训练集的偏离。生成样本可表示为训练样本加上离散化误差、估计误差和高斯噪声三个受控项，并基于此给出了生成模型中过拟合与欠拟合的理论定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为随机插值模型中的记忆（memorization）现象提供严格的理论解释，明确生成模型何时会记住训练样本、何时会产生泛化行为，并给出过拟合与欠拟合的理论定义。

Method: 利用最优速度场和对应分数函数的闭式表达式，分别分析了连续时间（确定性/随机性）生成过程、欧拉离散化生成过程以及存在估计误差时的生成过程，通过理论推导得到生成样本与训练样本之间的偏差界。

Result: 连续时间下，确定性和随机性生成过程都能恢复训练样本；欧拉离散化下，生成样本围绕训练样本分布，偏差由步长控制；存在估计误差时，累积估计误差控制端点偏离；生成样本可分解为训练样本+三个受控扰动项（离散化、估计误差、高斯噪声）。基于此给出了过拟合与欠拟合的理论定义。

Conclusion: 随机插值模型在连续时间下必然记忆训练样本，离散化和估计误差会引入可控的偏差；该理论框架为理解和控制生成模型的记忆与泛化行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper provides a theoretical account of memorization in stochastic interpolation models. By leveraging closed-form expressions for the optimal velocity field and the associated score function, we show that, in the continuous-time oracle setting, both deterministic and stochastic generation processes recover training samples. Under Euler discretization, generated samples remain centered around training samples, with deviations controlled by the step size. We further analyze generation in the presence of estimation errors and show that accumulated estimation errors control the endpoint deviation from the training set. These results imply that the generated sample admits a representation as a training sample perturbed by three controlled terms: a discretization-induced bound, an estimation-error-induced bound, and stochastic Gaussian noise. Based on this characterization, we provide theoretical definitions of overfitting and underfitting in generative models. Synthetic simulations support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [150] [Physics-Guided Dual Decoding and Spectral Supervision for Global 3D Hydrometeor Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08563)
*Dandan Chen,Yaqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PredHydro-Net提出了一种物理引导的双解码框架，通过解耦架构、小波频率分解、频谱幅度匹配和对抗训练，有效缓解了三维水凝物预测中的过度平滑问题，在极端事件检测和频谱表征上优于Earthformer、PredRNNv2和GFS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数据驱动模型在连续大气变量预测上表现出色，但三维水凝物预测因存在零膨胀、长尾分布而面临挑战；标准深度学习优化常产生过度平滑的预测，削弱极端事件和空间纹理。

Method: 提出PredHydro-Net，采用物理引导的双解码框架：解耦架构让宏观热力学/动力学场单向调控水凝物生成；集成小波频率解耦、频谱幅度匹配和对抗训练，以平衡定量精度与空间保真度。

Result: 在72小时全球评估中，PredHydro-Net在极端事件检测和频谱表征上优于Earthformer、PredRNNv2和GFS；与GPM卫星反演具有良好的气候一致性，能合理再现极端天气事件（如飓风Ian）的三维云结构。

Conclusion: 特征归因证实模型依赖相对湿度、风辐合等物理前兆量，为长尾大气预测提供了一种鲁棒的、物理信息驱动的解决方案。

Abstract: While global data-driven models excel at predicting continuous atmospheric variables, three-dimensional hydrometeor forecasting remains challenging due to the zero-inflated, long-tailed distributions of these variables. Standard deep learning optimization often yields overly smooth forecasts, attenuating extreme events and spatial textures. We propose PredHydro-Net, a physics-guided dual-decoding framework that mitigates this smoothing. To resolve multi-variable optimization conflicts, it employs a decoupled architecture where macroscopic thermodynamic and dynamic fields unidirectionally modulate hydrometeor generation. By integrating wavelet-based frequency decoupling, spectral amplitude matching, and adversarial training, the model achieves a favorable trade-off between quantitative accuracy and spatial fidelity. In a 72-h global evaluation, PredHydro-Net outperforms both spatiotemporal deep learning baselines (Earthformer and PredRNNv2) and the operational Global Forecast System (GFS) in extreme-event detection and spectral representation. Furthermore, it demonstrates strong climatological consistency with Global Precipitation Measurement (GPM) satellite retrievals. The model reasonably reproduces the three-dimensional cloud structures in extreme weather events, such as Hurricane Ian. Feature attribution confirms its dependence on physical precursors such as relative humidity and wind convergence, offering a robust, physics-informed approach to long-tailed atmospheric prediction.

</details>


### [151] [EinSort: Sorting is All We Need for Tensorizing LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08565)
*Toshiaki Koike-Akino,Jing Liu,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应张量化方法，通过索引排序发现目标张量中的固有低秩结构，在权重和KV缓存压缩上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型基础模型规模巨大且权重分布非结构化，难以识别其中的隐式低秩结构，现有方法无法有效应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出自适应张量化方法，通过索引排序（index ordering）自动发现目标张量中的固有低秩结构，从而设计更高效的张量网络压缩表示。

Result: 在权重压缩和KV缓存压缩任务上，该方法相比基线方法取得了更好的重建质量。

Conclusion: 自适应张量化能够有效发现大型模型中的低秩结构，为压缩大型基础模型提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Tensor networks provide efficient representations for compressing large neural networks. By carefully designing shapes and topologies, they can significantly reduce memory and computational costs. However, identifying implicit low-rank structures in large foundation models remains challenging due to their enormous scale and un-structured weight distributions. We propose an adaptive tensorization method that discovers inherent low-rank structure in a target tensor by index ordering. Experiments on weight and KV-cache compression demonstrate improved reconstruction quality compared to baselines.

</details>


### [152] [Titans-as-a-Layer: Test-Time Memory for Conversational Speech Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08573)
*Daniel Chen,Qicong Hu,Yang Xiao,Ting Dang,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory-as-a-Layer (MAL)适配器，在测试时为大型音频语言模型提供对话历史记忆，在不修改骨干模型的情况下提升语音情感识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SER方法通常将对话情感识别表述为话语级分类，忽略了说话人的惯用音域和先前话语建立的情感语境。虽然语音语言模型通过微调可以适应SER标签，但仍缺少对话级别的状态信息。

Method: 基于Titans架构，提出即插即用的Memory-as-a-Layer (MAL)适配器，将对话历史写入小型神经记忆，并以音频令牌对齐的残差更新方式读回，避免修改宿主模型的令牌位置。

Result: 在多种音频LLM和情感识别数据集上的评估表明，MAL设计在不同评估指标上均提升了SER性能。

Conclusion: 测试时记忆可以作为对话式SER的残差上下文机制，支持在不改动大型模型的情况下引入对话级上下文信息。

Abstract: Speech emotion recognition (SER) is commonly formulated as utterance-level classification, although conversational emotion depends on a speaker's usual vocal range and the emotional context established by previous utterances. Speech-language models provide strong pretrained acoustic and semantic representations, and can adapts them to SER labels via finetune, but this mechanism still missing per-dialogue state. We study whether test-time neural memory can supply this missing context while leaving the large audio language models (LALMs) backbone intact. Building on Titans, we introduce a plug-and-play Memory-as-a-Layer (MAL) adapter that writes dialogue history into a small neural memory and reads it back as an audio-token-aligned residual update, avoiding changes to the host model's token positions. Across different audio LLMs and emotion recognition datasets evaluations, our design improves SER performs across different evaluation metrics, supporting test-time memory as a residual contextual mechanism for conversational SER.

</details>


### [153] [OrderDP: A Theoretically Guaranteed Lossless Dynamic Data Pruning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08574)
*Chenhan Jin,Shengze Xu,Qingsong Wang,Fan Jia,Dingshuo Chen,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OrderDP框架，通过先随机选择子集再选择top-q样本的方式，在替代损失函数上实现无偏梯度估计，提供理论收敛性和泛化保证，在CIFAR和ImageNet上降低40%以上训练成本且保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据剪枝方法倾向于选择高信息量样本，导致梯度估计有偏，且该偏差对最终性能的影响尚不明确。

Method: OrderDP框架：先随机选择子集，再从中选择top-q样本，基于替代损失函数建立无偏性，并提供收敛性和泛化分析的理论保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和ImageNet-1K上，与多种基线方法相比，实现竞争性精度、稳定收敛和精确控制，训练成本降低超40%。

Conclusion: OrderDP作为即插即用框架，兼具强性能和计算效率，是数据高效学习的鲁棒且易适配的工具。

Abstract: Data pruning (DP), as an oft-stated strategy to alleviate heavy training burdens, reduces the volume of training samples according to a well-defined pruning method while striving for near-lossless performance. However, existing approaches, which commonly select highly informative samples, can lead to biased gradient estimation compared to full-dataset training. Furthermore, the analysis of this bias and its impact on final performance remains ambiguous. To address these challenges, we propose OrderDP, a plug-and-play framework that aims to obtain stable, unbiased, and near-lossless training acceleration with theoretical guarantees. Specifically, OrderDP first randomly selects a subset and then chooses the top-$q$ samples, where unbiasedness is established with respect to a surrogate loss. This ensures that OrderDP conducts unbiased training in terms of the surrogate objective. We further establish convergence and generalization analyses, elucidating how OrderDP affects optimal performance and enables well-controlled acceleration while ensuring guaranteed final performance. Empirically, we evaluate OrderDP against comprehensive baselines on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet-1K, demonstrating competitive accuracy, stable convergence, and exact control -- all with a simpler design and faster runtime, while reducing training cost by over 40%. Delivering both strong performance and computational efficiency, our method serves as a robust and easily adaptable tool for data-efficient learning. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/shengze-xu/OrderDP.

</details>


### [154] [Lost in the Non-convex Loss Landscape: How to Fine-tune the Large Time Series Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08578)
*Xu Zhang,Peang Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出平滑全微调（SFF）技术，通过随机初始化辅助LTSM并与预训练模型权重线性插值来平滑损失景观，解决大时间序列模型微调时因非凸损失景观病态导致的过拟合和次优性能问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练的LTSM存在病态条件的非凸损失景观，导致可训练性受限，直接微调容易过拟合且性能次优，甚至不如从头训练，严重削弱了预训练的优势。

Method: SFF方法：1) 通过随机初始化构建一个辅助LTSM以获得更平滑的损失景观；2) 将辅助模型的权重与预训练模型权重进行线性插值，平滑原始损失景观；3) 在此基础上进行下游任务微调。

Result: 在Timer、TimesFM、MOMENT、UniTS、MOIRAI、Chronos、TTMs、Sundial等8种代表性LTSM上，跨多样下游任务的基准数据集实验均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: SFF通过扰动尖锐最小值而不显著伤害平坦区域，帮助模型逃离不良局部盆地，导向更平滑、泛化性更好的解，有效提升了预训练LTSM在下游任务上的微调效果。

Abstract: Recently, large time series models (LTSMs) have gained increasing attention due to their similarities to large language models, including flexible context length, scalability, and task generality, outperforming advanced task-specific models. However, prior studies indicate that pre-trained LTSMs may exhibit a poorly conditioned non-convex loss landscape, leading to limited trainability. As a result, direct fine-tuning tends to cause overfitting and suboptimal performance, sometimes even worse than training from scratch, substantially diminishing the benefits of pre-training. To overcome this limitation, we propose Smoothed Full Fine-tuning (SFF), a novel fine-tuning technology. Specifically, we construct an auxiliary LTSM via random initialization to obtain a smoother loss landscape, and then linearly interpolate its weights with those of the pre-trained model to smooth the original landscape. This process improves trainability while preserving pre-trained knowledge, thereby enabling more effective downstream fine-tuning. From an optimization perspective, SFF perturbs sharp minima without significantly harming flat regions, facilitating escape from poor local basins toward smoother and more generalizable solutions. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across eight representative LTSMs, including Timer, TimesFM, MOMENT, UniTS, MOIRAI, Chronos, TTMs, and Sundial, on diverse downstream tasks. The code is available at the link: https://github.com/Meteor-Stars/SFF.

</details>


### [155] [A spectral audit framework reveals task-dependent aperiodic reliance across EEG and ECG deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08583)
*Jasmeet Singh Bindra,Siddharth Panwar,Shubhajit Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频谱审计框架，发现深度学习模型对生理时间序列（EEG/ECG）中宽带非周期1/f成分存在显著且架构通用的依赖。在睡眠-觉醒分类中，去除非周期成分导致准确率下降超过0.42；临床异常检测下降0.07-0.13；运动想象任务影响最小。该混淆效应在6/7的EEG基础模型及PTB-XL ECG数据集中均显著，且不能被人口学匹配完全消除。建议将非周期控制作为生理时间序列深度学习可解释分析的标准流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在生理时间序列（如EEG、ECG）上的可解释性通常依赖领域特定特征（如振荡节律、形态复合波），但这些信号同时包含宽带非周期1/f分量，该分量与唤醒、年龄和病理状态共变。现有研究忽略了这种非周期成分作为潜在混淆因素对深度学习模型决策的影响。

Method: 提出频谱审计框架，结合周期/非周期分解、相位保持的傅里叶干预、假对照以及仿真验证。在6种神经架构、7个EEG基础模型以及PTB-XL ECG数据集上评估非周期成分对模型性能的贡献，并通过年龄/性别和记录时期控制分析混淆效应。

Result: 非周期依赖具有任务依赖性和架构通用性：睡眠-觉醒分类中非周期平坦化导致平衡准确率下降超过0.42；临床异常检测下降0.07-0.13；运动想象任务影响最小。7个EEG基础模型中有6个显示FDR显著的非周期依赖；年龄/性别和记录时期控制减少了但未消除该效应。ECG中神经准确率下降0.32-0.36，且人口学匹配后仍存在。

Conclusion: 非周期控制应成为生理时间序列深度学习可解释分析的标准规范。研究揭示了广泛存在的混淆效应，必须加以控制才能确保深度学习在生理信号分析中的可信可解释性。

Abstract: Deep learning on physiological time series is interpreted through domain-specific features -- oscillatory rhythms in EEG, morphological complexes in ECG -- yet these signals sit atop a broadband aperiodic 1/f-like envelope that covaries with arousal, age, and pathology. We introduce a spectral audit framework combining aperiodic/periodic decomposition, phase-preserving Fourier interventions, sham controls, and simulation validation. Aperiodic reliance was task-dependent and architecture-general: across six neural architectures, flattening drops exceeded 0.42 balanced-accuracy points for sleep-wake classification, reached 0.07-0.13 for clinical abnormality detection, and remained minimal for motor imagery. Six of seven EEG foundation models showed FDR-significant aperiodic reliance on clinical EEG; age/sex and recording-era controls reduced but did not eliminate the effect. Applying the audit to PTB-XL ECG revealed neural drops of 0.32--0.36 persisting after demographic matching, confirming this confound class extends beyond EEG. Aperiodic controls should become standard for interpretable physiological time-series deep learning.

</details>


### [156] [Convolutional Sparse Coding via the Locally Competitive Algorithm on Loihi 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08584)
*Geoffrey Kasenbacher,Daniel Ruepp,Gerrit A. Ecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Loihi 2神经形态硬件上首次实现了卷积稀疏编码的局部竞争算法（LCA），并与GPU基线进行了对比评估，揭示了该算法在何种运行条件下对神经形态硬件具有吸引力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积稀疏编码引入了空间结构、权重共享、重叠感受野和规模化行为，比非卷积版本更贴近实际稀疏推理负载，但目前缺乏在神经形态硬件（如Loihi 2）上的实现与评估。

Method: 采用单层递归LCA公式，扩展至卷积特征图，并使用基于成对滤波器交互的局部抑制核；在Loihi 2上实现，并与传统GPU基线进行相同推理问题的对比。

Result: 首次完成了卷积LCA在Loihi 2上的实现与基准测试，明确了在何种运行条件下卷积稀疏推理在神经形态硬件上具有吸引力。

Conclusion: 将卷积LCA定位为新兴神经形态系统上结构化稀疏推理的有用基准，为后续研究提供了参考。

Abstract: Sparse coding provides a principled framework for signal representation by expressing an input as a linear combination of only a small number of basis functions. The Locally Competitive Algorithm (LCA) is particularly attractive in the context of neuromorphic computing because its dynamics, leaky integration, thresholding, and lateral inhibition map naturally to neuromorphic hardware. While prior work has studied non-convolutional LCA on Loihi 2, the convolutional setting is of particular interest because it introduces spatial structure, weight sharing, overlapping receptive fields, and scaling behavior that are more representative of practical sparse inference workloads. In this work, we present a Loihi 2 implementation of convolutional sparse coding via the LCA and evaluate it against a conventional GPU baseline on the same inference problems. The implementation follows a one-layer recurrent LCA formulation and extends it to convolutional feature maps with local inhibitory kernels derived from pairwise filter interactions. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first implementation and benchmark of convolutional LCA on Loihi 2. Our goal is not only to demonstrate feasibility, but also to clarify in which operating regimes convolutional sparse inference becomes attractive on neuromorphic hardware. The resulting study positions convolutional LCA as a useful benchmark for structured sparse inference on emerging neuromorphic systems.

</details>


### [157] [Quantum Global Variational Learning for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08592)
*Shun Ryuzaki,Hideo Mukai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种全局结构的量子神经网络，通过减少量子电路中所需的酉矩阵数量，实现97%的训练时间缩减和最高25%的训练完成率提升，最终达到100%训练成功率，并超越以往研究的纠错性能。同时增强了对内部网络噪声的鲁棒性，保真度提升最高15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效量子纠错对量子计算的发展至关重要，现有方法在训练效率和纠错性能上存在不足。

Method: 设计一种具有全局结构的量子神经网络，通过减少量子电路中酉矩阵的数量来降低计算负载。

Result: 训练时间减少97%，训练完成率提升最高25%，达到100%训练成功率；纠错性能超越以往研究；内部网络噪声下的保真度提升最高15%。

Conclusion: 所提全局结构量子神经网络在训练效率和纠错性能上均有显著提升，并增强了对抗内部噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Efficient quantum error correction is essential for the advancement of quantum computing. We propose a quantum neural network with a global structure that reduces the number of unitary matrices required in quantum circuits. This approach resulted in a 97\% reduction in training time and up to a 25\% improvement in the training completion rate, ultimately achieving a 100\% success rate in training while surpassing the error correction performance reported in previous studies. In addition, we demonstrated the enhanced robustness of quantum error correction against internal network noise. Moreover, the fidelity of quantum error correction under internal network noise increased by up to 15\% due to the reduced computational load.

</details>


### [158] [How Much Capacity Does EEG Denoising Need? Ultra-Compact Networks reveal Benchmark Saturation and Metric-Utility Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08594)
*Jasmeet Singh Bindra,Siddharth Panwar,Shubhajit Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制模型容量（1.05K-40.26K参数）在深度可分卷积U-Net上进行实验，发现EEG去噪的重构性能在3-6.5K参数时已饱和，更大参数（如8.46M）无优势；重构指标无法预测下游BCI任务性能，重建优化的去噪反而显著降低CSP+LDA分类准确率。建议采用容量控制的评估、更难的Task-aware基准和强制性的下游验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG去噪架构参数规模从数万到数千万不等，但尚无研究将模型容量作为独立实验变量，也未验证重构指标能否预测下游神经信号的实际可用性。作者填补了这一空白。

Method: 固定架构、损失函数、数据划分和训练策略，仅改变通道宽度（参数从1.05K到40.26K）在最小深度可分卷积U-Net上进行扫描；在EEGDenoiseNet基准、跨数据集BCI迁移测试、受控基线重训练以及所有9个BCI Competition IV-2a受试者的运动想象分类（5种解码器家族）上进行评估。

Result: 重构性能在3-6.5K参数时饱和，后续每log10参数单位仅提升至多0.015相关系数；8.46M参数基线与40.26K紧凑模型在EOG上效果相当（200倍参数差距无优势）；重构优化的去噪显著降低CSP+LDA分类（最优去噪准确率0.547 vs 噪声基线0.612），该现象在自然记录试次上持续存在（Δ=-0.047）。

Conclusion: 标准EEG去噪基准在当前模型容量下已饱和，重构指标不能预测BCI实用性；超紧凑模型（33-46 KB，1.27-2.61M FLOPs/段）可用于边缘部署；建议容量控制的评估、更难的Task-aware基准以及强制性的下游验证。

Abstract: Deep learning EEG denoising architectures have scaled from tens of thousands to tens of millions of parameters, yet no prior study has isolated model capacity as the experimental variable or tested whether reconstruction metrics predict downstream neural-signal utility. We address both gaps by fixing architecture, loss, data split, and training recipe while sweeping only channel width from 1.05K to 40.26K parameters in a minimal depthwise-separable convolutional U-Net. Models were evaluated on the EEGDenoiseNet benchmark, cross-dataset BCI transfer tests, controlled baseline retraining, and downstream motor-imagery classification with five decoder families across all nine BCI Competition IV-2a subjects. Reconstruction performance saturated by 3-6.5K parameters, with post-elbow gains of at most 0.015 correlation coefficient per log10-parameter unit. An 8.46M-parameter baseline retrained under the same pipeline matched the 40.26K compact variant on EOG--a 200x parameter gap yielding no advantage--while a Patch-Transformer control reproduced the same diminishing-return shape. Downstream evaluation exposed a classifier-dependent metric-utility gap: reconstruction-optimized denoising significantly degraded CSP+LDA classification across all nine subjects and three artifact types (best denoised accuracy 0.547 vs. 0.612 noisy baseline; Bonferroni p=0.0488), persisting on naturally recorded trials (Delta=-0.047; BH-FDR q=0.0049). End-to-end neural decoders showed variable or neutral effects. Standard EEG denoising benchmarks are saturated far below current model capacity, and reconstruction metrics do not predict BCI utility. Ultra-compact models at 33-46 KB and 1.27-2.61M FLOPs/segment are practical for edge deployment. These findings argue for capacity-controlled evaluation, harder task-aware benchmarks, and mandatory downstream validation.

</details>


### [159] [Reinforcement Learning for Flow-Matching Policies with Density Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08602)
*Boshu Lei,Kostas Daniilidis,Antonio Loquercio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RLDT的在线强化学习算法，通过将基于密度的流匹配策略与SVGD导出的传输场对齐，实现了在连续控制问题中高效微调流匹配策略，无需反向传播时间或蒸馏，在密集/稀疏奖励及视觉/状态基的机器人操作任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在强化学习中微调流匹配策略时，要么近似当前或最优策略分布引入偏置梯度，要么采用蒸馏损失多模态建模能力，缺乏直接且高效的对齐方法。

Method: 利用最大熵强化学习目标结合SVGD构建传输场，将策略改进视为动作密度向高奖励区域的传输；通过期望目标估计从中间去噪步骤近似策略动作，避免不稳定的反向传播时间，实现传输场更新向网络参数的稳定传播。

Result: 在多种连续控制任务（包括密集/稀疏奖励、状态/视觉基长程机器人操作）中，RLDT在奖励质量和收敛速度上均优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: RLDT成功将强化学习中的策略改进与流匹配的传输公式对齐，解决了梯度不稳定和蒸馏偏差问题，为基于流的生成模型在RL中的高效微调提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We present an online reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm for fine-tuning flow-matching policies in continuous-control problems. Our key insight is to view RL-based policy improvement as a transport of action densities towards regions of high reward, which naturally aligns with the transport formulation of flow matching models. Prior methods either approximate the current or optimal policy distribution or resort to distillation, which introduces biased gradients or sacrifices multimodal modeling capacity. In contrast, our approach for RL with Density Transport, which we name \emph{RLDT}, constructs a transport field from a maximum-entropy RL objective using Stein Variational Gradient Descent (SVGD). Then, it finetunes a pretrained flow matching policy to align with this field. Training with this alignment objective is nontrivial because flow-matching policies generate actions via a multi-step process, making direct gradient-based optimization challenging. To overcome this challenge and stabilize training, we approximate policy actions from intermediate denoising steps via expected-target estimation. This allows the transport-field update to propagate into the network parameters without unstable backpropagation through time. Experimental results demonstrate that RLDT outperforms competitive baselines in reward quality and convergence speed. This performance holds across diverse continuous-control tasks, encompassing both dense and sparse rewards, as well as state- and vision-based long-horizon robot manipulation. The project webpage is \href{https://rpfey.github.io/rldt/}{https://rpfey.github.io/rldt/}.

</details>


### [160] [Tyan-WP: A Wind Power Foundation Model for Ultra-Short-Term Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08630)
*Jiahui Huang,Ao Luo,Lei Liu,Hongwei Zhao,Tengyuan Liu,Ruibo Guo,Bo Wang,Zhao Wang,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tyan-WP，首个面向超短期概率预测的风电基础模型。该模型在大规模风电数据集（覆盖美国12.6万站点、7年数据）上预训练，通过静态站点嵌入（坐标、地形、生态区）和功率感知气象融合模块，实现了零样本预测。在域内127个站点上，相比11种通用LSTM，MAE降低19.9%、RMSE降低16.6%、CRPS降低22.2%、AQL降低21.7%、R²提升16.7%，并在英国6个真实站点上验证了跨地理泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球风电装机容量快速增长，行业亟需准确的风电基础模型以缩短调试周期、加速并网。现有站点特定时间序列模型在数据稀缺场景下泛化能力差，而通用大型时间序列模型多限于单变量输入，无法充分利用静态站点属性及功率与气象协变量间的依赖关系，导致精度不足。

Method: 提出Tyan-WP风电基础模型，包含两大领域专用模块：(1) 静态站点嵌入——利用坐标、地形和生态区元数据编码站点静态特征；(2) 功率感知气象融合模块——建模历史功率与气象协变量间的交互作用。模型在美国12.6万站点、7年数据上预训练，实现零样本超短期概率预测。

Result: 在10个域内站点上超越8种站点特定监督TSM；在127个域内站点上超越11种通用LTSM，MAE降低19.9%、RMSE降低16.6%、CRPS降低22.2%、AQL降低21.7%、R²提升16.7%。在英国6个真实站点上验证了跨地理泛化能力。

Conclusion: 风电基础模型可在无需目标站点训练的情况下实现准确的零样本预测，为新风电机组快速入网和概率风险管理提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Global wind power capacity, especially in China, is booming, with new farms spanning diverse terrains and climates. The industry urgently needs accurate wind power foundation models to shorten commissioning and accelerate grid connection. This is because site-specific time series models (TSMs) are not well suited to data-scarce scenarios and generalize poorly, while generic large time series models (LTSMs) are mostly limited to univariate inputs and cannot fully exploit static site attributes or the dependencies between power and meteorological covariates, leading to insufficient accuracy. To fill this gap, we propose \textbf{Tyan-WP}, the first wind power foundation model for ultra-short-term probabilistic forecasting. Pretrained on a large-scale wind power dataset covering more than 126,000 U.S. sites over seven years, Tyan-WP further improves zero-shot forecasting through two domain-specific module designs: static site embedding using coordinate, terrain, and ecoregion metadata, and a power-aware meteorological fusion (PAMF) module that models interactions between historical power and meteorological covariates. Under a unified evaluation protocol, Tyan-WP surpasses eight site-specific supervised TSMs on 10 in-domain sites and outperforms eleven generic LTSMs on 127 in-domain sites, reducing MAE by 19.9%, RMSE by 16.6%, CRPS by 22.2%, and AQL by 21.7%, while raising R^2 by 16.7%. It further demonstrates strong cross-geography generalization on six real U.K. sites. These results show that the wind power foundation model can achieve accurate zero-shot forecasting without target-site training, providing a practical pathway for rapid turbine onboarding and probabilistic risk management at new wind farms.

</details>


### [161] [Operator learning for the 2D incompressible Navier-Stokes equations: a conformal prediction approach in the data-scarce regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08654)
*Weinan Wang,Bowen Gang,Hao Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扰动的共形预测框架，用于神经算子（特别是FNO）在2D Navier-Stokes方程中的不确定性量化。该方法通过比较两个在近相同数据集上训练的算子（一个用原始标签，一个用加高斯噪声的标签）构建局部不确定性尺度，在数据稀缺场景下比需要单独不确定性网络的方法产生更窄的共形带，同时维持目标覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子作为昂贵PDE求解器的快速替代品，但自身无法提供时空场预测的校准不确定性。现有方法需要额外的不确定性网络，在数据稀缺时会进一步分割有限标签预算。因此需要一种样本高效的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 将训练好的FNO与分裂共形预测结合，通过训练两个近相同数据集上的算子（一个原始标签，一个加高斯噪声扰动标签），比较两者预测差异来构建局部不确定性尺度。在固定总标签预算下，该方法无需额外网络分配数据。

Result: 在2D Navier-Stokes基准测试上，在相同总数据预算下，基于扰动的方法比现有方法产生更窄的共形带，同时保持目标同时覆盖率。

Conclusion: 扰动敏感性是共形化神经算子的一种实用且样本高效的不确定性代理。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a perturbation-based conformal prediction framework for uncertainty quantification in operator learning, with a focus on the 2D Navier--Stokes equations. While neural operators provide fast surrogates for expensive PDE solvers, they do not by themselves provide calibrated uncertainty for spatiotemporal field predictions. Our approach wraps a trained Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) with split conformal prediction and constructs the local uncertainty scale by comparing the predictions of two operators trained on nearly identical datasets: one on the original labels and one on labels perturbed by small Gaussian noise. We consider this procedure in the data-scarce regime, where the total label budget is fixed and methods that require a separate uncertainty network must divide training data between multiple models. On the 2D Navier--Stokes benchmark, the perturbation-based method produces substantially narrower conformal bands than existing methods under matched total data budgets while maintaining the target simultaneous coverage. These results suggest that perturbation sensitivity is a practical and sample-efficient uncertainty proxy for conformalized neural operators.

</details>


### [162] [SkillHone: A Harness for Continual Agent Skill Evolution Through Persistent Decision History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08671)
*Zhiwei Li,Yong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillHone提出了一种基于持久决策历史的持续智能体技能演化方法，通过记录诊断、修订、证据和结果的结构化历史，使角色分离的子智能体能够在跨会话中精炼技能而无需重新发现过去的推理。在深度研究基准测试中，使用Qwen3.6-35B-A3B作为评估骨干，生成的技能在GAIA上超过商用检索服务的深度研究智能体15.8分，在WebWalkerQA-EN上超过3.2分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅在有限运行中改进技能，只保留最终产物，丢弃了后续智能体解释先前修订、评估和被拒绝替代方案所需的决策历史。

Method: SkillHone通过将技能修订与评估侧证据配对以提供实践反馈，记录诊断、修订、证据和结果的结构化历史。角色分离的子智能体在带有脱敏报告的实践探针上运行候选技能，并根据先前决策提出修订，实现跨会话精炼。

Result: 在原始开放网络环境中，使用Qwen3.6-35B-A3B作为评估骨干，生成的技能在GAIA上超越商用检索服务的深度研究智能体15.8分，在WebWalkerQA-EN上超越3.2分，同时也超过了先前的技能演化方法。

Conclusion: SkillHone通过持久化决策历史并支持跨会话的持续技能演化，显著提升了语言模型智能体在深度研究任务中的表现。

Abstract: Agent skills extend language-model agents with task-specific procedures, scripts, and references, but the tasks and environments they target continually change. Existing methods improve skills in bounded runs and retain only the final artifact, discarding the decision history that later agents need to interpret prior revisions, evaluations, and rejected alternatives. We introduce SkillHone, a harness for continual agent skill evolution grounded in persistent decision history. SkillHone pairs skill revisions with evaluation-side evidence that supplies practice feedback, recording structured histories of diagnoses, revisions, evidence, and outcomes. Role-separated subagents run candidate skills on practice probes with redacted reporting and propose revisions informed by prior decisions, enabling cross-session refinement without rediscovering past rationale. We evaluate SkillHone on deep-research benchmarks in a raw open-web setting, where agents are not given an integrated search stack and must organize retrieval through portable skills. We compare against a deep-research agent backed by commercial retrieval services. With Qwen3.6-35B-A3B as the evaluation-time backbone, the resulting skills outperform the deep-research agent by 15.8 points on GAIA and 3.2 points on WebWalkerQA-EN, while also exceeding prior skill-evolution methods.

</details>


### [163] [Activation Steering Induces Emergent Misalignment: A More Comprehensive Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08682)
*Qi Cao,Jian Lou,Meiting Liu,Wenjie Feng,Dan Li,See-Kiong Ng,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了激活引导（Activation Steering）这一推理时技术是否能够引发涌现性对齐失败（Emergent Misalignment, EM）。研究发现，激活引导可以在Qwen-3.5等模型中诱发广泛的对齐失败，且其产生的有害响应比微调版本语义关联性更强、连贯性更高；同时分析了引导强度、低秩子空间结构、训练轮次等关键因素对EM的影响，揭示了激活引导是尚未被充分重视的EM来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管微调引发的涌现性对齐失败已被广泛研究，但激活引导作为一种日益流行的推理时模型控制技术，是否也能诱发EM尚未被充分探索。
