Our results show that distribution shifts strongly influence privacy vulnerability: the closer the adaptation data is to the pretraining distribution, the higher the practical privacy risk at the same theoretical guarantee, even without direct data overlap. We find that parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods, such as LoRA, achieve the highest empirical privacy protection for OOD data. Our benchmark identifies key factors for achieving practical privacy in DP LLM adaptation, providing actionable insights for deploying customized models in sensitive settings. Looking forward, we propose a structured framework for holistic privacy assessment beyond adaptation privacy, to identify and evaluate risks across the full pretrain-adapt pipeline of LLMs.

</details>


### [234] [LargeMonitor: Monitoring Online Task-Free Continual Learning via Large Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09430)
*Mingqi Yuan,Xiaoquan Sun,Shihao Luo,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LargeMonitor利用预训练的大模型（LVM用于零样本漂移检测，LMM用于语义诊断）在在线无任务持续学习中实现解耦的、训练无关的分布漂移检测与诊断，动态触发自适应的优化策略，提升现有TFCL算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线TFCL方法（如prompt tuning、动态结构扩展）依赖训练耦合的优化动态（如经验损失波动、潜在距离演化）来驱动适应，无法区分分布漂移的结构性来源（如新类出现 vs. 环境域偏移），导致对本质上不同的流变化机械地使用固定策略。

Method: LargeMonitor包含两个解耦模块：(1) 基于冻结的大视觉模型（LVM）稳定表示空间的零样本漂移检测模块，无需训练依赖或阈值调优；(2) 基于大多模态模型（LMM）的上下文感知诊断模块，用于解释流变化的具体语义病因（如新类涌现、环境域偏移）。之后动态部署偏移特定的自适应优化策略。

Result: 在多个TFCL设置和基准上的大量实验表明，LargeMonitor实现了对复杂数据流的精确、鲁棒检测与诊断，并持续提升现有在线TFCL算法的性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于大模型解耦检测与诊断的框架，使连续学习器能动态采用漂移类型特化的优化策略，弥补了现有训练耦合方法对漂移结构来源的忽视。

Abstract: Online task-free continual learning (TFCL) requires intelligent agents to sequentially accumulate knowledge from an unbounded, non-stationary data stream under strict single-pass constraints and without any explicit task identifiers. Existing online TFCL paradigms primarily rely on parameter-efficient prompt tuning or dynamic structure expansion driven by training-coupled optimization dynamics, such as empirical loss fluctuations or evolving latent distances. As a result, these training-coupled solvers remain agnostic to the structural origins of distribution drift, mechanically enforcing a fixed strategy across fundamentally distinct streaming variations. To address this gap, we propose LargeMonitor, a framework that leverages large pretrained foundation models to autonomously orchestrate task-free continuous adaptation. Specifically, LargeMonitor introduces a decoupled detection module utilizing the frozen, stable representation space of large vision models (LVMs) to achieve robust, zero-shot drift detection without training-dependent interference or brittle threshold tuning. Upon a confirmed drift, the framework activates a context-aware diagnostic module driven by large multimodal models (LMMs) to interpret the precise semantic etiologies of the stream variation (e.g., novel class emergence vs. environmental domain shift). This dual-stage capability empowers the continuous learner to dynamically deploy adaptive and shift-specific optimization strategies. Extensive experiments across multiple TFCL settings and benchmarks demonstrate that LargeMonitor achieves precise, robust detection and diagnosis of complex data streams while consistently improving the performance of existing online TFCL algorithms.

</details>


### [235] [Graph Mamba Operator: A Latent Simulator for Interacting Particle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09432)
*Karn Tiwari,Niladri Dutta,N M Anoop Krishnan,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图曼巴算子(GraMO)，一种将状态空间模型与图交互学习结合的潜在空间模拟器，通过单一循环耦合图交互和时间状态更新，在N体系统、动作捕捉和机器人数据集上实现最优的长时域预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络处理交互动力系统时存在两个问题：1）依赖自回归滚动且时空分离处理，导致长时域误差累积；2）仅关注局部交互和短时间上下文，无法捕捉多跳依赖和全局结构。

Method: 提出GraMO，在潜在空间中通过单一循环同时耦合图交互和时间状态更新，更新过程在潜在状态上是线性的，但系数是输入依赖的，可跨不同动态模式自适应调整。

Result: 在N体系统、动作捕捉和机器人数据集上，GraMO在所有基准测试中实现了最低误差，并在长时域预测中取得了最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将状态空间模型与图交互学习在单一循环中深度融合，能有效解决长时域预测中的误差累积问题，同时捕捉多跳依赖和全局结构。

Abstract: Modeling interacting dynamical systems requires capturing spatial interactions alongside long-range temporal dependencies. Graph neural networks (GNNs) provide a natural representation but typically rely on autoregressive rollouts and treat spatial and temporal dynamics separately, leading to error accumulation over long horizons. Existing approaches also focus on local interactions and short temporal contexts, limiting their ability to capture multi-hop dependencies and global structure. We introduce the Graph Mamba Operator (GraMO), a latent-space simulator that integrates state-space models with graph-based interaction learning. In contrast to prior work that sequences nodes or applies spatial and temporal updates in separate stages, GraMO couples graph-based interactions and temporal state updates within a single recurrence. The update is linear in the latent state, with input-dependent coefficients that adapt across regimes. We evaluate GraMO on N-body systems, motion capture, and robotics datasets, achieving the lowest error across benchmarks and the largest gains in long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [236] [Operator learning for solving Fokker-Planck equations with various initial conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09434)
*Li Zeng,Xiaoliang Wan,Yaobin Wang,Fabio Nobile,Tao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件归一化流的物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，用于高效近似Fokker-Planck方程在整个初始条件范围内的解算子。通过Chapman-Kolmogorov方程将问题转化为从任意点狄拉克初始分布开始的转移概率密度近似，利用线性化SDE的PDF作为归一化流的基分布，并引入时间加权损失函数以缓解小时间步的数值不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fokker-Planck方程在描述随机动力学系统概率密度函数的时间演化中至关重要，但传统数值方法在处理整个初始条件范围内的解算子时计算成本高昂，需要一种高效且鲁棒的近似方法。

Method: 1) 利用Chapman-Kolmogorov方程将FPE问题重构成从任意点狄拉克初始分布开始的转移概率密度近似问题；2) 采用相关线性化SDE的PDF作为归一化流的基分布，避免狄拉克初始分布带来的奇异性；3) 设计时间加权损失函数，平衡因果关系与训练难度。

Result: 多项数值实验表明所提方法在近似FPE解算子方面具有有效性和鲁棒性，能够处理从不同初始条件开始的概率密度演化。

Conclusion: 所提出的条件归一化流PINN框架能够高效、鲁棒地近似FPE的整个初始条件范围的解算子，为随机动力学系统的概率密度演化提供了新的数值求解范式。

Abstract: The Fokker-Planck equation (FPE) plays a pivotal role in describing the time evolution of probability density functions (PDFs) for systems governed by stochastic dynamics. In this work, we propose a conditional normalizing flow-based physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework for efficiently approximating the solution operator of the FPE for a whole range of initial conditions. Leveraging the Chapman-Kolmogorov equation for Markovian stochastic processes, the problem is reformulated into approximating a transition PDF starting at initial time from a Dirac mass centered at an arbitrary point. The PDF of an associated linearized stochastic differential equation (SDE) is employed as the base distribution for the normalizing flow, providing a good approximation of the target PDF, especially for small times, and thereby avoiding the singularity of the map associated with the Dirac delta initial distribution. Furthermore, a time-weighted loss function is introduced to mitigate numerical instabilities arising at small times, achieving a balance between causality and training difficulty as time progresses. A variety of numerical experiments are presented to illustrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [237] [Breaking the Tokenizer Barrier: On-Policy Distillation across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09456)
*Yifan Niu,Han Xiao,Dongyi Liu,Zelong Wang,Dihong Gong,Yasheng Wang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨分词器的同策略蒸馏方法，通过精确的令牌映射算法，使教师和学生模型即使使用不同分词器也能进行高效的令牌级知识蒸馏，实验证明该方法在多种基准上比基线方法计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略蒸馏要求教师和学生模型共享相同的分词器，这极大限制了其应用范围；而当前跨分词器的做法（对教师生成响应进行SFT）无法捕获教师概率分布中的丰富知识。

Method: 提出一种精确的令牌映射算法，使标准同策略蒸馏方法能够跨模型系列运作，使高保真令牌级信号能在不同分词器之间传播。

Result: 大量实验表明，跨分词器同策略蒸馏在多种基准上比基线方法计算效率显著更高。

Conclusion: 该工作解锁了更广泛的教师-学生配对用于同策略蒸馏，为适配和增强大语言模型之间的交互开辟了新途径。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has become a core technique in the post-training of Large Language Models (LLMs) for transferring knowledge from domain experts to student models. However, existing OPD distillation methods require teacher and student models to share the same tokenizer, restricting the applicability of OPD within the model series. Current mainstream practice typically employs Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) on teacher-generated responses for cross-tokenizer distillation, which fails to capture the rich knowledge embedded in the teacher's probability distribution. In this work, we enable the standard on-policy distillation method to operate across model families, ensuring that high-fidelity token-level signals can propagate across different tokenizers with a precise token-mapping algorithm. Extensive experiments show that cross-tokenizer OPD is significantly more compute-efficient than baselines on various benchmarks. Our results unlock a broader range of teacher-student pairs for OPD, opening up new avenues for adapting and enhancing interactions between LLMs.

</details>


### [238] [Escaping the KL Agreement Trap in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09471)
*Haoran Xin,Anhao Zhao,Ying Sun,Jin Li,Xiaoyu Shen,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对同策略蒸馏（OPD）中存在的低KL一致性陷阱问题，提出了KAT（KL一致性陷阱终止）方法，通过动态自适应阈值检测并终止低质量训练信号，在四个数学基准上显著提升准确率并减少约60%的推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在OPD中，当学生模型漂移到无法恢复的前缀时，教师模型可能对退化状态产生局部一致，导致低反向KL但缺乏有效纠正信号，形成低KL一致性陷阱，影响训练效率。

Method: 提出KAT方法：一种在线OPD终止规则，通过动态训练自适应阈值检测持续的低KL一致性，从而过滤来自退化一致性的弱监督信号。

Result: 在四个数学基准上，avg@k准确率提升2.66%，pass@k提升3.43%，平均推理长度减少59.73%。

Conclusion: KAT有效解决了低KL一致性陷阱问题，通过过滤弱监督信号提升了蒸馏效率与模型性能，同时大幅缩短了推理长度。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision by asking a teacher to score student-generated rollouts. However, when the student drifts into an unrecoverable prefix, the teacher may locally agree with the degraded state, producing low reverse KL but little corrective training signal. We identify this persistent regime as a low-KL agreement trap. Further analyses show that tokens during and after such traps produce less useful supervision signals. We propose KAT (KL Agreement Trap Termination), an online OPD termination rule that detects persistent low-KL agreement with a dynamic training-adaptive threshold. By filtering weak supervision from degenerate agreement, KAT improves avg@k accuracy by 2.66% and pass@k by 3.43% across four mathematical benchmarks, while reducing average rollout length by 59.73%.

</details>


### [239] [Loss-Guided Adaptive Scale Refinement for Molecular Force Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09480)
*Limin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种损失引导的自适应尺度细化框架，用于分子力预测。该框架将预定义尺度作为初始锚点，通过插值、路由、可微分尺度更新和尺度池细化来发现任务有效分辨率。在NaCl水溶液离子系统上验证，相比固定尺度方法（MAE 399.65），连续最优插值降至380.96，最终更新的尺度池{0,0.125,0.25,0.375,0.5,0.75,1}达到MAE 381.23，接近最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子表示学习方法依赖于人工预定义尺度，而任务最优建模尺度可能与这些固定层次不一致，因此需要一种能够自适应发现任务有效尺度的框架。

Method: 提出了损失引导的自适应尺度细化框架，包括四个关键操作：插值（在锚点尺度间连续插值）、路由（硬路由选择最佳尺度）、可微分尺度更新（通过梯度下降优化尺度参数）、尺度池细化（动态扩展与优化尺度集合）。以NaCl水溶液离子系统为测试平台，构建短程和长程力预测分支并分析其互补性。

Result: Oracle硬路由将整体力MAE从399.65降至382.67，连续Oracle插值进一步降至380.96。近接触区域（最近离子距离<0.6 nm）MAE从327.22降至260.51。从端点锚点{0,1}开始，损失引导更新自动生成中间尺度，最终尺度池{0,0.125,0.25,0.375,0.5,0.75,1}实现整体MAE 381.23。

Conclusion: 自适应尺度细化是分子表示学习的一个有前景的方向，尤其在固定尺度建模不足以捕捉多尺度相互作用时，该方法能自动发现任务最优分辨率。

Abstract: Molecular systems involve interactions across multiple spatial scales, from local coordination and short-range perturbations to long-range electrostatic and solvent-mediated effects. However, most molecular representation learning methods rely on manually predefined scales, and the task-optimal modeling scale may not coincide with these fixed levels. This study introduces a loss-guided adaptive scale refinement framework for molecular force prediction, treating predefined scales as initial anchors and discovering task-effective resolutions through interpolation, routing, differentiable scale updates, and scale pool refinement.
  Using a NaCl aqueous ionic system as a minimal testbed, this study constructs short-scale and long-range force prediction branches and analyzes their complementarity. Oracle hard routing reduces the overall force MAE from 399.65 to 382.67, while continuous oracle interpolation further reduces it to 380.96. In close-contact regimes with nearest-ion distance below 0.6 nm, the close-contact MAE decreases from 327.22 to 260.51. A minimal scale pool update experiment shows that starting from endpoint anchors {0,1}, loss-guided updates automatically generate intermediate scales and recover most of the continuous oracle performance. The final updated scale pool {0,0.125,0.25,0.375,0.5,0.75,1} achieves an overall MAE of 381.23.
  These results support adaptive scale refinement as a promising direction for molecular representation learning, especially when fixed-scale modeling is insufficient.

</details>


### [240] [BUDDY: BUdget-Driven DYnamic Depth Routing for Adaptive Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09514)
*Yuhua Zhou,Shaoqi Yu,Shichao Weng,Changhai Zhou,Mingze Yin,Fei Yang,Aimin Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Buddy框架，通过动态深度路由在满足用户指定计算预算的前提下自适应选择Transformer层，支持解码时重路由和单模型多预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度剪枝方法无法在用户指定的计算预算下灵活控制，且在解码过程中路由路径固定不变，无法随上下文增长自适应调整。

Method: Buddy包含轻量级决策模块，根据输入对中间层打分并确定性地执行top-k层以满足预算；复用第一层KV缓存作为全局上下文来源，将其与最新token表示融合后进行路由决策；可选预算预测器在无显式预算时估计输入依赖的计算量。

Result: 在Llama系列和Qwen模型上的实验表明，Buddy与强静态剪枝基线竞争力相当，且通常能改进精度-计算权衡，同时独特地支持严格预算控制、解码时重路由及单模型多预算。

Conclusion: Buddy是一种预算驱动的动态深度路由框架，能够在不牺牲模型质量的前提下满足用户指定的计算预算约束，并具备解码时自适应能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) incur high inference cost due to their depth and parameter scale. Depth pruning can reduce latency by skipping redundant Transformer blocks, but existing methods (i) provide limited control under user-specific compute budgets and (ii) typically fix the routing path, failing to adapt as the context grows during decoding. We propose Buddy, a budget-driven dynamic depth routing framework. Buddy uses a lightweight Decision Module to score intermediate layers conditioned on the input and deterministically executes the top-k layers to satisfy a given budget. To support decode-time adaptation, Buddy reuses the first-layer KV cache as a low-overhead global context source and pools it together with the newest token representation before each routing decision. When no explicit budget is provided, an optional Budget Predictor estimates an input-dependent compute level to balance quality and efficiency. Experiments on Llama-family and Qwen models show that Buddy is competitive with strong static pruning baselines and often improves the accuracy-compute trade-off, while uniquely supporting strict budget control, decode-time rerouting, and multiple budgets within a single trained model.

</details>


### [241] [Investigating Calibration Challenges in Probabilistic Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09517)
*Jan Niklas Lettner,Hadeer El Ashhab,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前概率电价预测中使用的评分规则过于强调锐度而忽视校准，导致模型产生过度自信且统计不可靠的预测，并呼吁未来研究转向校准感知的目标和架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源并网增加市场波动性，概率性电价预测对风险管理至关重要，但现有评分规则牺牲校准性追求锐度，导致不确定性估计不可靠。

Method: 通过理论分析揭示当前评分规则在锐度与校准之间的权衡问题，论证忽视校准会导致模型退化为确定性预测代理。

Result: 证明了仅优化锐度的评分规则会导致模型产生过度自信的概率预测，在统计可靠性上存在显著缺陷。

Conclusion: 未来概率电价预测研究应转向校准感知的目标函数和模型架构，以确保预测的分布完整性。

Abstract: As renewable energy integration increases market volatility, probabilistic electricity price forecasting has become essential for effective risk management. However, current-proper-scoring rules often prioritize forecast sharpness at the expense of calibration, leading to overconfident and statistically unreliable uncertainty estimates. This work highlights the critical gap between theoretical scoring and practical calibration, demonstrating that models can become mere proxies for deterministic forecasts when reliability is neglected. We conclude that future research must shift toward calibration-aware objectives and architectures to ensure the distributional integrity of energy market forecasts.

</details>


### [242] [Efficient Traffic Prediction at Scale: A Systematic Study of STGCN Architectural Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09539)
*Soban Nasir Lone,Mohamed Abouelela,Taeyoung Yu,Jiwon Kim,Constantinos Antoniou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了STGCN（时空图卷积网络）的参数量化问题，发现标准2-block架构存在过度参数化：1-block在大多数数据集和短期预测中表现最优，而增加block数量带来的性能提升微乎其微（<0.5%），却显著增加了计算开销（61%延迟增加、37%吞吐量下降）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作提出了STGNN的高效替代方案，但一个根本问题尚未被探索：这些架构本身是否过度参数化？本文旨在评估STGCN的block数量对性能与效率的影响，为ITS实际部署提供指导。

Method: 以STGCN为研究对象，在四个不同的交通数据集上，系统比较了1-block、2-block（标准配置）和3-block三种变体的预测性能与计算效率，涵盖短期（10分钟）和长期预测任务。

Result: 1-block在三个数据集的短期预测中达到最优；长期预测性能下降极小（相对误差≤1.8%）；2-block相比1-block延迟增加61%、吞吐量下降37%；3-block计算成本翻倍但改进<0.5%。

Conclusion: 默认2-block STGCN在许多应用场景下可能过度参数化，对ITS实际部署和效率导向的基准测试均有重要启示。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) have become the dominant approach for traffic prediction, yet their computational requirements pose challenges for practical deployment in intelligent transportation systems (ITS). While recent work has proposed efficient alternatives to STGNNs, a fundamental question remains unexplored: are these architectures themselves over-parameterised? We examine this question using the Spatio-Temporal Graph Convolutional Network (STGCN), one of the most widely adopted models in this domain. Through systematic experiments across four diverse traffic datasets, we compare 1-block, 2-block (standard), and 3-block STGCN variants. Our findings reveal that the single-block architecture achieves optimal performance for short-term prediction (10 mins) on three of four datasets, while incurring only marginal degradation ($\leq$1.8% relative error) at longer horizons. Crucially, the 2-block variant incurs 61% higher CPU inference latency and 37% lower throughput relative to 1-block -- substantial overhead for resource-constrained ITS deployment. The 3-block architecture offers no favourable tradeoff, more than doubling computational cost for $<$0.5% relative improvement. These results suggest that the default 2-block STGCN may be over-parameterised for many applications, with implications for both practitioners deploying traffic prediction systems and researchers benchmarking efficiency-focused methods.

</details>


### [243] [Safe-RULE: Safe Reinforcement UnLEarning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09559)
*Shixiong Jiang,Taozheng Zhu,Fanxin Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Safe-RULE框架，一种用于离线安全强化学习的防御机制，通过强化卸载（reinforcement unlearning）消除数据投毒攻击的影响，无需重新训练或访问原始环境，实验表明该方法能有效提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线安全强化学习依赖静态数据集，易遭受数据投毒攻击，恶意样本会破坏安全性并导致不安全的策略行为，现有方法缺乏有效的防御机制。

Method: 提出Safe-RULE（safe reinforcement unlearning）学习范式，通过强化卸载过程在考虑任务性能和安全性约束的前提下，消除中毒数据的影响，无需从头重新训练或访问原始训练环境。

Result: 在多个基准安全强化学习任务上的实验表明，该方法能有效提升对抗数据投毒攻击时的安全性。

Conclusion: Safe-RULE为离线安全强化学习中的数据投毒攻击提供了一种有效的防御框架，能够在无需重训的条件下恢复策略安全性。

Abstract: Offline safe reinforcement learning (Safe RL) enables policy learning without online interactions, making it suitable for safety-critical systems such as robotics systems. However, its reliance on static datasets exposes offline Safe RL to data poisoning attacks, where adversaries inject malicious samples that compromise safety and induce unsafe policy behavior. In this work, we propose a new learning paradigm, named safe reinforcement unlearning (Safe-RULE), used as a defense framework to remove the influence of poisoned data without retraining from scratch or requiring access to the original training environment. We further extend reinforcement unlearning to offline Safe RL by explicitly accounting for both task performance and safety constraints during the unlearning process. Experiments across benchmark Safe RL tasks demonstrate that our approach effectively enhances safety performance against data poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [244] [On Choosing the $μ$ Parameter in Gaussian Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09582)
*Bogdan Kulynych,Antti Honkela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从纯-DP ε到GDP μ的原则性映射方法，通过匹配强敌手成员推断攻击在最坏情况下的成功概率（基于三种指标：固定FPR下的乘法优势、固定召回率下的精确度和隐私轮廓），推荐μ≈ε/5作为保守的通用转换公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年研究主张使用高斯差分隐私（GDP）来报告隐私保护机器学习中的隐私保证，但缺乏从纯差分隐私（pure-DP）到GDP的系统性、原则性映射方法。

Method: 通过匹配强敌手成员推断攻击在最坏情况下的成功概率，基于三种指标（固定假阳性率下的乘法优势、固定召回率下的精确度、标准隐私轮廓）建立从纯-DP ε到GDP μ的映射，并在实用参数范围内制表。

Result: 提供了从纯-DP ε到GDP μ的映射表，并推荐μ≈ε/5作为保守的通用转换公式。

Conclusion: 所提出的映射方法为不同隐私度量体系之间的转换提供了原则性基础，推荐的μ≈ε/5可作为实践中的简便转换规则。

Abstract: Recent work argues for using Gaussian differential privacy (GDP) to report the privacy guarantees in privacy-preserving machine learning. We provide principled mappings from pure-DP $\varepsilon$ to GDP $μ$ by matching the worst-case success of a strong-adversary membership inference attack in terms of three metrics: multiplicative advantage at fixed FPR, precision at fixed recall, and the standard privacy profile. We tabulate $μ$ values across a useful range of parameters and recommend $μ\approx \varepsilon/5$ as a conservative general-purpose conversion.

</details>


### [245] [Assessing Sample Quality in Conditional Generation under Compositional Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09601)
*Berker Demirel,Valentino Maiorca,Marco Fumero,Theofanis Karaletsos,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验、每样本的可信度分数，用于评估条件生成模型在样本外推（extrapolative）场景下的生成质量，解决了因缺乏参考目标分布而无法使用标准评估指标的循环问题。该分数结合全局真实性和属性保真度，可在仅使用训练分布的情况下对生成样本进行有效过滤、排序和弃权，并在生物成像和视觉基准任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型常用于探索罕见、昂贵或尚未观测到的条件组合（外推场景），但标准条件质量指标需要参考目标分布，而外推场景下该分布本身不可得，形成了评估的循环困境。作者旨在解决这一评估难题。

Method: 提出一个后验、每样本的可信度分数，由两个可估计的量组成：(1) 全局真实性（global realism），衡量样本与真实数据流形的兼容性；(2) 属性保真度（attribute-wise faithfulness），衡量样本是否更接近请求的属性而非其他合理替代属性。该分数仅在训练分布上计算，且需满足观测属性的温和覆盖条件。

Result: 在生物成像任务中，筛选后的样本更好地保留了真实的形态结构并提升了下游预测性能；在可控视觉基准任务上也观察到类似增益。此外，该分数可在生成过程中应用，实现完全解码前的弃权。

Conclusion: 所提可信度分数能够有效恢复外推生成样本之间的有意义的比较，支持对生成结果的过滤、排序和弃权，可直接用于现成的预训练模型，为条件生成模型的评估提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Conditional generators provide a natural tool for controllable generation, including settings where the desired condition is a new composition of observed attributes or experimental factors. In many applications, especially in scientific domains, such models are attractive to explore conditions for which real samples are rare, expensive, or not yet observed. However, this creates a circularity for evaluation: standard conditional quality metrics require a reference target distribution, but in the extrapolative regime that distribution is unavailable by definition. We address this problem with a post-hoc, per-sample trust score for assessing conditional samples using only the training distribution. The score combines two estimable quantities: global realism, measuring compatibility with the real data manifold, and attribute-wise faithfulness, measuring whether a sample is closer to the requested attributes than to plausible alternatives. We show that the score can recover meaningful comparisons across extrapolated generations, under a mild coverage condition on the observed attributes. These comparisons enable effective filtering, ranking, and abstention of generations and can be used directly on off-the-shelf pretrained models. In biological imaging, selected samples preserve real morphological structure better and improve downstream predictive performance, while similar gains are observed on controlled vision benchmarks. Finally, we show how the score can be applied during generation, enabling abstention before full decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/berkerdemirel/faithful-cond-gen.

</details>


### [246] [Closure-Validated Circuit Discovery in Attention Heads: Co-activation Proposes, Ablation Disposes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09607)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了基于共激活统计的注意力头聚类能否识别出真正的电路。在密集模型上，聚类社区通过了闭合测试（因果消融验证），但在MoE模型中，路由条件聚类虽存在统计信号，却未通过闭合测试。作者提出闭合测试是区分电路提案与已确认电路的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究越来越多地将组件群组（而非单个单元）作为基本分析对象，并通过聚类共激活统计来发现这些群组。作者质疑这种廉价信号是否真的能识别出注意力头电路，需要更严格的因果验证方法。

Method: 借鉴稀疏自编码器的聚类方法到注意力头，但用因果消融替代重构作为验证手段。对聚类发现的社区进行闭合测试：消融发现社区，比较每个样本的损失变化与随机匹配的控制组。在Pythia 1B、OLMo 1B两个密集模型和OLMoE-1B-7B MoE模型上进行实验，并追踪训练过程中闭合测试的变化。

Result: 密集模型（Pythia 1B、OLMo 1B）中聚类社区通过了闭合测试。MoE模型中，路由条件聚类恢复出统计上真实的信号，但未通过闭合测试——消融反而降低了损失（错误方向）。扩展闭合测试到训练过程中，注意力目标选择性和参与比率与功能在两个方向上解耦。

Conclusion: 廉价信号（共激活聚类）只能作为电路提案，而非已确认的电路；闭合测试（因果消融验证）是区分二者的关键。

Abstract: Interpretability increasingly treats groups of components, not individual units, as the basic object, and proposes to find them by clustering co-activation statistics. We ask whether such a cheap signal actually identifies an attention-head circuit. Adapting a sparse-autoencoder clustering recipe to attention heads -- but validating by causal ablation rather than reconstruction -- we cluster heads and then run a closure test: ablate the discovered community and compare per-example damage to matched-random controls. Across two dense 1B-scale models (Pythia 1B, OLMo 1B) and two input distributions, the communities pass closure. In a Mixture-of-Experts model (OLMoE-1B-7B), route-conditional clustering recovers a statistically real signal that nonetheless does not survive closure -- ablation improves loss, the wrong direction. Extending closure across training, attention-target selectivity and participation ratio decouple from function in both directions. We conclude that a cheap signal is a circuit proposal, not a confirmed circuit; closure is what separates them.

</details>


### [247] [Constrained user-item allocation for e-commerce marketing campaigns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09623)
*Maja Lindström,Natalija Glisovic,Jan von Pichowski,Tommy Löfstedt,Martin Rosvall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了自动定位（auto-targeting）方法，通过约束光谱双聚类、贪心局部搜索和强化学习框架三种策略，联合选择用户和商品以构建多个不重叠的营销活动。实验表明，双聚类方法在活动质量、提升度和公平性上表现最优，而基于多臂赌博机的方法在大规模数据上更具可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有营销活动方法要么预设活动结构，要么将商品选择与用户分配解耦，无法从联合交互模式中自动发现活动分组。因此需要一种能同时选择用户和商品、自动构建多个不重叠活动的联合优化方法。

Method: 形式化定义了自动定位问题，提出三种互补策略：(i) 约束光谱双聚类，寻找用户-商品亲和矩阵中的稠密区域；(ii) 带成对交换的贪心局部搜索，进行组合优化；(iii) 多臂赌博机框架，通过探索逃离局部最优。

Result: 在合成数据、Amazon Reviews基准和大规模商业数据上，与模拟退火基线相比，双聚类方法在活动质量、提升度和公平性得分上持续最优。双聚类在小数据集上高效，但在超大规模数据上计算时间显著增加，而基于赌博机的方法提供了可扩展的替代方案。

Conclusion: 双聚类是高质量营销活动构建的首选方法，多臂赌博机方法更适合大规模场景。研究为零售商的联合用户-商品选择与活动分组提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: When running marketing campaigns, retailers must decide which products to promote and which users to target. These decisions are inherently coupled: effective campaigns match users and items with strong mutual affinity into non-overlapping groups of predefined sizes. However, existing approaches assume predefined campaign structure or decouple item selection from user assignment, and cannot discover campaign groupings directly from joint interaction patterns. We therefore formalize this campaign problem as auto-targeting: jointly selecting users and items to construct multiple disjoint campaigns. To solve this combinatorial problem, we propose three complementary strategies: (i) constrained spectral biclustering to find dense regions in the user-item affinity matrix, (ii) greedy local search with pairwise swaps for combinatorial refinement, and (iii) a multi-armed bandit framework to escape local optima through exploration. We evaluate these methods on a synthetic dataset, the Amazon Reviews benchmarks, and large-scale proprietary commercial data, and compare the results to simulated annealing as a baseline. The results show that biclustering consistently achieves the highest campaign quality, lift, and fairness scores. While biclustering runs efficiently on smaller datasets, its runtime increases substantially on very large ones, where bandit-based methods instead offer a scalable alternative.

</details>


### [248] [Data-driven discovery of governing differential equations across physical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09638)
*Siyu Lou,Hao Xu,Wenguan Wang,Lu Lu,Hao Sun,Yang Liu,Linfeng Zhang,Dongxiao Zhang,Yuntian Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了数据驱动微分方程发现领域，提出问题导向视角：通过二维相图（结构复杂度×系数复杂度）组织发现问题，并引入REO（表示-评估-优化）框架抽象发现过程的核心原理。文章指出该领域已从稀疏简单方程向复杂结构和灵活参数化发展，并强调未来挑战在于利用方程修正现有理论、提炼机制、形成新科学概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微分方程对科学发现至关重要，但传统第一性原理方法在底层物理不明时受限。数据驱动发现虽发展迅速，但缺乏统一组织视角，尤其是AI方法涌现后方向多样、体系混乱。

Method: 提出二维相图（结构复杂度×系数复杂度）对发现问题进行分类，并引入REO框架（表示-评估-优化）作为发现过程的通用抽象，将讨论从具体算法提升至基本原理层面。

Result: 二维相图揭示了领域从稀疏简单方程向复杂结构的发展轨迹，并解释了不同方法族在不同问题场景下的成败原因。REO框架识别了超越算法变体的核心问题。

Conclusion: 数据驱动微分方程发现的下一挑战不仅是恢复方程，更是利用方程修正理论、提炼机制及形成新概念。REO框架和相图共同为理解发现过程提供了系统化视角。

Abstract: Differential equations play a critical role in scientific discovery because they provide a mathematical framework to describe the behaviour of physical phenomena. As a promising alternative to traditional first principles, data-driven differential equation discovery has attracted increasing attention for its ability to infer governing laws directly from experimental or simulated data, especially when the underlying physics is unclear. However, the field has expanded rapidly along diverse methodological directions, particularly with the emergence of AI-based approaches, and still lacks a clear organizing perspective. In this Review, we propose a problem-oriented perspective on data-driven differential equation discovery. We first introduce a two-dimensional phase diagram of equation discoverability, where discovery problems are organized according to structural complexity and coefficient complexity. This phase diagram shows how the field has moved from the discovery of sparse equations with simple coefficients toward more complex governing laws with richer structures and more flexible parameterizations. It also clarifies why different methodological families succeed or fail in different problem settings. We then present the representation-evaluation-optimization (REO) framework as a fundamental abstraction of the discovery process. By identifying the core problems of equation discovery that persist across algorithmic variations, REO shifts the discussion from individual algorithms to the fundamental principles that determine discoverability. We connect these perspectives to applications across physics and adjacent sciences, and argue that the next challenge is not merely recovering equations, but using them to revise existing theories, distil mechanisms and form new scientific concepts.

</details>


### [249] [A Unifying Framework for Concept-Based Representational Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09653)
*Grégoire Dhimoïla,Victor Boutin,Agustin Martin Picard,Thomas Fel,Thomas Serre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念对齐的统一框架，沿表示vs概念和实例级vs分布级两个轴分解对齐，引入干预基准InterVenchA和联合稀疏自编码器CoSAE，证明对齐本质上是多目标问题，仅需0.1%配对数据即可恢复实例级对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念对齐方法术语相同但目标各异，对齐的定义模糊不清，缺乏统一的理论框架来明确不同方法实际保证的对齐性质。

Method: 提出两轴对齐框架（表示/概念 × 实例级/分布级），定义四种性质；构建InterVenchA基准分别度量提取质量、翻译质量和概念一致性；提出CoSAE联合优化互补的对齐目标。

Result: 常用对齐目标间的等价假设不成立，优化一个性质无法可靠恢复其他性质；纯无监督目标无法恢复有意义的实例级对齐；CoSAE联合训练才能实现强对齐；仅需0.1%配对数据即可锚定分布目标以恢复实例级对齐。

Conclusion: 概念对齐本质上是多目标优化问题，需要明确定义、度量和优化多个互补的对齐性质。

Abstract: Learned representations across models and modalities often exhibit striking structural similarities, suggesting shared underlying concept decompositions. However, concept alignment remains poorly defined: existing approaches optimize different objectives under the same terminology, obscuring what is actually aligned.
  We propose a unifying framework that decomposes alignment along two axes: what is aligned (representations vs. concepts) and at what level (instance-wise vs. distributional). This induces four corresponding properties -- instance-wise and distributional variants of translation and concept consistency -- and reveals precisely which of these guarantees existing methods provide. We further introduce \InterVenchA, an intervention-based benchmark that separately measures extraction quality, translation quality, and concept consistency. Through theory and experiments, we show that commonly assumed equivalences between alignment objectives fail in practice: optimizing one property does not reliably recover the others, and purely unsupervised objectives fail to recover meaningful instance-level alignment. We then propose the Coupled Sparse Autoencoder (CoSAE), which jointly enforces complementary alignment objectives. Strong alignment emerges only in this regime. Surprisingly, as little as 0.1\% paired data is sufficient to recover instance-level alignment when anchoring distributional objectives.
  Overall, our results show that concept alignment is fundamentally multi-objective: it must be defined, measured, and optimized as such.

</details>


### [250] [Muon Learns More Robust and Transferable Features than Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09658)
*Tianyu Ruan,Fengzhuo Zhang,Shuche Wang,Shihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon优化器在预训练大语言模型和视觉分类器中优于Adam和SGD，本文通过鲁棒性和迁移性实验证明Muon学习到的特征更鲁棒、更可迁移，并在多组件特征分类问题上给出理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon虽在效率上优于Adam和SGD，但其特征学习优势尚不明确，本文旨在从鲁棒性和迁移性角度揭示Muon的特征学习优势。

Method: 1) 在损坏图像和文本上评估预训练模型的鲁棒性，并使用逐层探针分析logit margin；2) 在下游任务上通过线性分类器或全模型微调评估迁移性，用有效秩（effective rank）测量隐藏状态多样性；3) 在多组件特征分类问题上给出理论证明。

Result: Muon学习到的特征在多种架构（Transformer和CNN）上比Adam和SGD更鲁棒，鲁棒性优势体现在更大的逐层logit margin上；Muon特征的迁移性更强，有效秩更高；多组件特征分类问题的理论证明支持了实证发现。

Conclusion: Muon优化器在特征学习的鲁棒性和迁移性上均优于Adam和SGD，理论分析进一步佐证了其更大的margin和更高的有效秩。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a state-of-the-art optimizer for pretraining Large Language Models (LLMs) and vision classifiers. Despite its efficiency advantage over Adam and SGD, the feature-learning advantage of Muon remains unclear. This paper investigates Muon's feature-learning advantage through the lens of robustness and transferability. First, by evaluating pretrained models on corrupted images and texts, we show that features learned by Muon are consistently more robust than those learned by Adam and SGD across different architectures, including transformers and Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). Using trained layer-wise probes, we further show that this robustness advantage is reflected in larger logit margins across layers. Second, by training linear classifiers or fine-tuning full models from pretrained parameters on downstream tasks, we demonstrate that Muon-learned features transfer more effectively than those learned by Adam and SGD. This transferability advantage is further supported by the diversity of hidden states across layers, as measured by effective rank. Finally, in a representative classification problem with multi-component features, we prove that Muon attains larger margins and higher effective rank than Adam and SGD, providing theoretical support for our empirical findings.

</details>


### [251] [In-Context Learning for Latent Space Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09664)
*Tuan A. Vu,Harri Lähdesmäki,Julien Martinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对潜空间贝叶斯优化中表格基础模型与潜空间任务不匹配的问题，通过在分子VAE潜空间上构建合成优化任务进行继续预训练，并引入正则化器保持模型原始回归先验，从而提升LSBO中上下文代理模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间贝叶斯优化（LSBO）中使用表格基础模型（如TabPFN、TabICL）作为代理模型时，这些模型通过大规模合成预训练数据获得的贝叶斯行为与LSBO中从潜编码到目标值的映射存在显著不匹配，影响优化性能。

Method: 在表格基础模型的预训练基础上，利用分子VAE的潜空间定义合成优化任务进行继续预训练，并在训练目标中加入正则化器以锚定原始检查点，既保留广泛回归先验又避免过度适应。

Result: 在保留分子优化基准测试上，该方法取得了强性能，验证了LSBO特定适应对上下文代理模型的有效性。

Conclusion: LSBO特定适应对于提升上下文代理模型性能具有重要意义，通过继续预训练可以弥合表格基础模型与潜空间优化任务之间的不匹配。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a central tool for sample-efficient design, and latent-space Bayesian optimization (LSBO) extends it to structured objects such as molecules and proteins. In parallel, tabular foundation models such as TabPFN and TabICL now achieve state-of-the-art regression performance and are increasingly used as BO surrogates. Because their Bayesian behavior is induced by large synthetic pretraining collections, the composition of this pretraining distribution is crucial. LSBO creates a distinctive mismatch: the induced map from latent code to objective value differs markedly from the regression tasks used to train current in-context models. We address this mismatch by complementing the pretraining stage of tabular foundation model surrogates with synthetic optimization tasks defined on the latent space of a molecular VAE. The continued-pretraining objective features a regularizer that anchors the model to the original checkpoint, preserving its broad regression prior while avoiding overspecialization to the adaptation tasks. On held-out molecular optimization benchmarks, the resulting model achieves strong performance, supporting the relevance of LSBO-specific adaptation for in-context surrogates.

</details>


### [252] [Algorithm for Contextual Queueing Bandits with Rate-Optimal Queue Length Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09668)
*Seoungbin Bae,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CQB-η-2算法，将上下文队列博弈的队列长度遗憾从Õ(T^{-1/4})改进到Õ(T^{-1/2})，并证明该界在忽略对数因子下是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文队列博弈算法在随机上下文下仅能达到Õ(T^{-1/4})的队列长度遗憾界，存在改进空间。核心问题在于如何更高效地分配探索与利用，从而获得更优的遗憾界。

Method: 提出三阶段算法CQB-η-2：阶段一进行纯随机探索构建初始估计器；阶段二结合η-随机探索与UCB规则，在保持负漂移的同时继续学习；阶段三在探索截止后仅使用UCB。关键观察是随机探索只需进行到精心选择的截止轮次，而非整个时间范围。

Result: 队列长度遗憾上界为Õ(T^{-1/2})，同时证明匹配的极小化极大下界Ω(T^{-1/2})，从而刻画了关于时间T的最优依赖关系（忽略对数因子）。

Conclusion: 本文成功将上下文队列博弈的队列长度遗憾从Õ(T^{-1/4})改进到极小化极大最优的Õ(T^{-1/2})，完成了对该问题在随机上下文下最优遗憾率的刻画。

Abstract: Contextual queueing bandits provide a framework for learning to schedule heterogeneous jobs under unknown context-dependent service rates. Under stochastic contexts, existing algorithms achieve $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/4})$ queue length regret, defined as the expected difference between the learner's and oracle's queue lengths at horizon $T$. In this paper, we improve this rate to $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$. The key observation is that random exploration is needed only up to a carefully chosen cutoff round, rather than throughout the entire horizon. We propose CQB-$η$-2, a three-phase algorithm: (i) pure random exploration to construct an initial estimator, (ii) $η$-random exploration combined with a UCB rule to continue learning while maintaining negative drift, and (iii) pure UCB after the exploration cutoff. Our proof decomposes the queue length regret at the cutoff round. Before the cutoff, negative drift suppresses queue length differences caused by suboptimal choices. After the cutoff, the first two phases provide sufficient random exploration samples, ensuring that UCB decisions incur small departure-rate gaps. Combining these two bounds yields queue length regret of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$. We further prove a minimax lower bound of order $Ω(T^{-1/2})$. The proof constructs two hard instances that are statistically indistinguishable up to the final service decision, and uses a queue-specific coupling argument to convert the resulting testing error into queue length regret. Together, our upper and lower bounds characterize the minimax dependence on the horizon $T$ up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [253] [Transition-Based Digital Twin Modelling for Alzheimer's Disease under Sparse Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09671)
*Yinyu Huang,Yilin Zhang,Sofia Michopoulou,Christopher Kipps,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于个性化数字孪生框架的阿尔茨海默病预测方法，利用多模态纵向数据，结合转移建模和序列建模两种策略，在ADNI数据集上实现了认知状态和诊断类别的预测，并支持不确定性量化和假设情景分析。研究发现局部转移建模比序列建模更具数据效率和预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法多集中于静态分类或群体层面的风险估计，缺乏个体化建模和不确定性推理能力，难以应对AD进展的高度异质性和稀疏不规则纵向数据。

Method: 构建个性化数字孪生框架，集成转移建模（相邻访视间的局部转换）和序列建模（长时序依赖）两种互补策略，利用ADNI多模态数据（认知评估、临床变量、MRI表型），进行认知状态评分预测和诊断分类，并量化预测不确定性及支持患者特异性"what-if"轨迹分析。

Result: 在去泄漏的受试者层面分割评估中，转移建模在评分预测和诊断分类上均优于序列建模，表明局部转移建模在稀疏不规则数据下更具数据效率和鲁棒性；序列模型在不确定性感知的轨迹预测方面仍有价值。

Conclusion: 转移式数字孪生框架为神经退行性疾病的个性化预测提供了一种数据高效、可解释且实用的方法，提示时间建模策略的选择应与临床数据结构特性相匹配。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression is highly heterogeneous and is typically observed through sparse and irregular longitudinal data, posing challenges for prediction and personalised monitoring. Existing machine learning approaches have improved AD prediction using multimodal data, yet often focus on static classification or cohort-level risk estimation, providing limited support for subject-specific modelling and uncertainty-aware reasoning. To address these limitations, we present a personalised digital twin framework for AD prediction and scenario-based analysis using multimodal longitudinal data. The proposed approach integrates complementary modelling strategies to capture clinical transitions and temporal dependencies across visits. Using data from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), including cognitive assessments, clinical variables, and MRI-derived phenotypes, the framework predicts cognitive status and diagnostic categories while quantifying predictive uncertainty and enabling patient-specific what-if trajectory analysis. Evaluation on leak-free subject-level splits demonstrates strong performance in score forecasting and diagnosis classification. In this sparse and irregular ADNI setting, transition-based modelling of adjacent visits achieved higher predictive accuracy than the sequence-based branch, suggesting that local transition modelling may be more data-efficient. While sequence models remain valuable for uncertainty-aware trajectory forecasting, local transition modelling offers a more data-efficient and robust predictive strategy. These findings highlight the importance of aligning temporal modelling strategies with clinical data structure and suggest that transition-based digital twin formulations may provide a practical and interpretable approach for personalised disease forecasting in neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [254] [When Do Local Score Models Extrapolate Across Size? A Diagnostic Theory and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09705)
*Wenjie Xi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成模型中尺寸迁移（小系统训练、大系统评估）的稳定性问题。发现架构局部性不足以保证稳定外推，关键在于高斯平滑得分的准局部性。通过Tweedie公式揭示了远距离扰动通过后验协方差影响局部得分组件的机制，形式化证明了反向扩散下局部边缘的尺寸均匀比较定理，并提出了FDLF诊断基准。实验验证了空间混合、平滑得分准局部性与模型感受野之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在尺寸迁移时，仅依赖平移不变性架构的局部性无法保证稳定外推，需要揭示影响稳定性的根本机理。

Method: 理论分析上利用Tweedie公式建立远距离扰动通过后验协方差影响局部得分的机制，形式化证明尺寸均匀比较定理；实验上提出FDLF（有限深度局部流）作为白盒诊断基准，具备精确得分、密度和可控响应范围。

Result: 在空间混合条件下，平滑得分相对于感受野保持准局部性，实现稳定外推；当空间混合减弱时，得分局部性快速退化，导致尺寸迁移失败。

Conclusion: 生成模型尺寸迁移的稳定性由高斯平滑得分的准局部性决定，而非单纯依赖架构局部性，空间混合程度是关键调控因素。

Abstract: Scientific generative modeling often requires size transfer, where models trained on small systems are evaluated on larger ones. While translation-invariant architectures enable this evaluation, we show that architectural locality alone does not guarantee stable size extrapolation. Instead, stable extrapolation is governed by the quasi-locality of the Gaussian-smoothed score. Through Tweedie's formula, far-away perturbations can influence local score components via posterior covariance, meaning a local model succeeds only if its receptive field covers the smoothed score's response range. We formalize this mechanism, proving a size-uniform comparison theorem for local marginals under reverse diffusion. We also introduce Finite-Depth Local Flow (FDLF), a white-box diagnostic benchmark with exact scores, densities, and controllable response ranges. Empirically, we validate the interplay between spatial mixing, smoothed-score quasi-locality, and model receptive fields. Under spatial mixing, the smoothed score remains quasi-local relative to the receptive field, enabling stable extrapolation. Conversely, when spatial mixing weakens, the score's locality rapidly degrades, causing size transfer to fail.

</details>


### [255] [BrainSurgery: Reproducible and Reliable Declarative Weight Manipulations for Model Editing and Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09707)
*Gianluca Barmina,Annemette Broch Pirchert,Andrea Blasi Núñez,Lukas Galke Poech,Peter Schneider-Kamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainSurgery是一个用于神经网络检查点"张量手术"的工具，通过声明式YAML计划执行复杂的模型权重变换，支持结构修改、数学变换和张量重塑，并内置断言验证防止静默错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型规模增大，管理、检查和修改大型检查点变得越来越具挑战性。研究人员经常需要修改模型权重以进行层重组、精度转换、低秩分解和架构调试，但这些工作流通常依赖脆弱的临时Python脚本。

Method: BrainSurgery通过抽象存储格式和内存管理，使用声明式YAML计划执行复杂变换。支持通过表达式正则表达式和结构定位进行结构修改、数学变换和张量重塑，内置断言验证张量形状、数据类型和值以防止静默错误。

Result: 提供了四个示例和三个案例研究，涵盖从模型升级到LoRA提取等多种应用场景。

Conclusion: BrainSurgery通过其可重复和可验证的操作，为未来研究提供坚实基础。

Abstract: As deep learning models scale, managing, inspecting, and modifying large checkpoints has become increasingly challenging. Researchers often need to alter model weights for layer restructuring, precision casting, low-rank factorization, and architectural debugging, yet these workflows often rely on fragile ad-hoc Python scripts. Here, we introduce BrainSurgery, a tool for robust and reproducible "tensor surgery" on neural network checkpoints, and provide a system demonstration covering four examples and three case studies from model upcycling to LoRA extraction. By abstracting storage formats and memory management, BrainSurgery executes complex transformations through declarative YAML plans. It supports structural modifications, mathematical transformations, and tensor reshaping through expressive regex and structural targeting, while built-in assertions validate tensor shapes, data types, and values to prevent silent errors. We envision that BrainSurgery will provide a strong foundation for future research through its reproducible and validated operations.

</details>


### [256] [Evaluating the Representation Space of Diffusion Models via Self-Supervised Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09718)
*Xiao Li,Yixuan Jia,Zekai Zhang,Xiang Li,Lianghe Shi,Jinxin Zhou,Zhihui Zhu,Liyue Shen,Qing Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Fisher信息的ICR指标，统一评估扩散模型的表征与生成能力，发现中间噪声层表征不变性最优，且ICR可作为记忆化早期检测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成和自监督表征学习上均表现优异，但两者之间的联系尚未被充分探索。本文旨在建立统一框架，联合评估扩散模型的表征与生成能力。

Method: 将特征分解为不变分量和残差分量，推导出基于Fisher信息的ICR指标；利用该框架分析扩散模型的判别行为（不同噪声层）和生成行为（数据受限下的记忆化）。

Result: 表征不变性在中间噪声层达到峰值，且与下游分类性能一致；ICR可作为训练时记忆化早期检测的敏感指标，无需外部评估器或测试集。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可以通过自监督视角下的表征几何结构进行监测，ICR为统一理解生成与表征能力提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable generative capabilities and have also emerged as powerful self-supervised representation learners, yet the connection between these two abilities remains less explored. Drawing inspiration from self-supervised learning (SSL), we introduce a framework for jointly evaluating the representation and generation capabilities of diffusion models. Specifically, we decompose features into invariant and residual components and derive the Invariant Contamination Ratio (ICR), a Fisher-based metric that quantifies how residual variation contaminates invariant signal in feature space. We use this framework to analyze both discriminative and generative behavior of diffusion models. On the representation side, we find that invariance peaks at intermediate noise levels, which also yield the best downstream classification performance. On the generative side, we study how training transitions from genuine generalization to memorization in data-limited regimes, and show that ICR serves as a sensitive training-time indicator of early learning: increasing residual energy along Fisher directions marks the onset of memorization, detectable from training features alone without external evaluators or held-out test sets. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can be monitored from a self-supervised perspective through the geometry of their learned representations.

</details>


### [257] [Disentanglement with Holographic Reduced Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09725)
*Jhonny J. Velasquez Olivera,Christo K. Thomas,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于全息简化表示（HRR）的无监督解缠算法，利用HRR的解绑定操作作为归纳偏置来分离数据中的因子，通过信息论分析证明了该方法的有效性，并在多个指标上取得了与基线相当或更优的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解缠方法多依赖于连续表示（如VAE、GAN），而本文将解缠表示视为符号结构，受概念间组合关系的启发，旨在解决学习离散符号结构时保持可微性的困难。

Method: 提出一种无监督学习算法，使用全息简化表示（HRR）进行神经解缠。利用HRR的解绑定操作提供归纳偏置以分离因子，并结合信息论分析证明解绑定可产生近似独立的符号-值对，并推导出每个槽位的容量上界。

Result: 在潜在遍历和解缠指标上取得了与基线竞争的结果；HRR表示相比其他解缠表示对噪声更具鲁棒性，能在不同信噪比下保持重建质量；信息论分析量化了归纳偏置的效果。

Conclusion: HRR解绑定操作是一种有效的解缠归纳偏置，其表示（向量求和）不同于传统自编码器的标量潜在维度，在噪声鲁棒性和解缠性能上均表现出优势。

Abstract: Disentanglement, the separation of factors of variation in data using neural networks, remains a long-standing challenge in machine learning. Prior work has addressed this problem with variational autoencoders and generative adversarial networks that incorporate ideas from variational inference and information-theoretic constraints. In contrast to methods that rely on continuous representations, we propose a design that treats disentangled representations as symbolic structures, motivated by the compositional relationships among the concepts that make up samples from a distribution. However, learning discrete symbolic structures with neural networks while maintaining differentiability is difficult and often requires complex architectures. To address this, we introduce an unsupervised learning algorithm that uses holographic reduced representations (HRR) for neural disentanglement. We show that the HRR unbinding operation provides an inductive bias for separating factors and yields competitive results against baselines, as measured by latent traversals and disentanglement metrics. We complement these empirical findings with an information-theoretic analysis of the HRR unbinding channel. We prove that unbinding induces approximately independent symbol-value pairs and derive a per-slot capacity bound that quantifies how many distinct symbolic concepts can be reliably encoded, giving a quantitative account of the inductive bias toward disentanglement. The resulting representations differ from standard autoencoder-based models, in that their latent units are vectors that are summed together, rather than scalar dimensions of a low-dimensional latent vector. We show that this HRR representation is more robust to noise than other disentangled representations and maintains reconstruction quality across a range of SNRs.

</details>


### [258] [Tight Sample Complexity of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09731)
*Chenxiao Yang,Nathan Srebro,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格刻画了深度为L、总参数为W、输入序列长度为T的Transformer的VC维，上界为O(L W log(T W))，下界为Ω(L W log(T W / L))。同时严格刻画了使用思维链（chain-of-thought）学习的样本复杂度，教师强制（teacher forcing）的样本复杂度上界为O(L W log((T+T')W))，下界为Ω(L W log((T+T')W / L))，其中T'为自回归步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Transformer的统计学习能力提供严格的理论刻画，特别是其VC维以及思维链学习所需的样本复杂度，填补了该领域理论分析的空白。

Method: 采用VC维理论框架，分别推导了Transformer的VC维上界和下界；进一步在思维链学习场景下，分析了教师强制策略的样本复杂度上下界。

Result: Transformer的VC维上界为O(L W log(T W))，下界为Ω(L W log(T W / L))；思维链学习中教师强制策略的样本复杂度上界为O(L W log((T+T')W))，下界为Ω(L W log((T+T')W / L))。

Conclusion: Transformer的VC维及其思维链样本复杂度已被严格刻画，上下界在主要参数上几乎匹配。

Abstract: We tightly characterize the VC dimension of depth-$L$ Transformers with a total of $W$ parameters, mapping an input sequence of length $T$ to a single output, establishing an upper bound of $O(L W \log (T W))$ and a nearly matching lower bound of $Ω(L W \log (T W / L))$. We further tightly characterize the sample complexity of chain-of-thought learning using such a Transformer, showing teacher forcing (i.e. selecting a predictor consistent with the entire chain-of-thought on training data) learns with sample complexity $O\left(L W \log \left(\left(T+T^{\prime}\right) W\right)\right)$ and that any learning rule that uses chain-of-thought data requires at least $Ω\left(L W \log \left(\left(T+T^{\prime}\right) W / L\right)\right)$ examples, where $T$ is the input length and $T^{\prime}$ is the number of autoregressive steps.

</details>


### [259] [Preserving Plasticity in Continual Learning via Dynamical Isometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09762)
*Andries Rosseau,Robert Müller,Ann Nowé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现动态等距（各层雅可比奇异值接近1）是保持持续学习中可塑性的关键机制，提出了促进等距的正则化方法和AdamO优化器，在多个持续学习基准上匹配或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在非平稳环境下持续训练会导致塑性（plasticity）逐渐丧失，限制了后续学习能力。现有方法对塑性丧失的机制理解不足，需要从理论角度揭示保持可塑性的关键因素。

Method: 1) 将可塑性与经验神经正切核（Neural Tangent Kernel）关联；2) 重新审视几乎处处等距且保持通用Lipschitz函数逼近能力的网络类；3) 提出高效促进等距的正则化方案，发现其能重新激活休眠ReLU单元；4) 提出AdamO优化器，解耦等距正则化与梯度更新；5) 通过动态等距视角重新解释现有塑性保持方法。

Result: 在监督学习和强化学习的持续学习基准上，所提方法一致匹配或超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 动态等距是保持持续学习中可塑性的关键机制，通过促进等距的正则化和AdamO优化器可以有效缓解塑性丧失问题。

Abstract: Continual training of deep neural networks under non-stationarity often leads to a progressive loss of plasticity, eventually limiting further learning. We relate plasticity to the empirical Neural Tangent Kernel, and identify dynamical isometry (the condition that layer-wise Jacobian singular values remain close to one) as a key mechanism for preserving plasticity in continual learning. We revisit a class of networks that are almost-everywhere isometric while remaining universal Lipschitz function approximators, demonstrating that near-dynamical isometry is compatible with expressive nonlinear representations. For general architectures, we propose an efficient isometry-promoting regularization scheme and identify a novel mechanism by which it can reactivate dormant ReLU units. Building on this, we introduce AdamO, an Adam-style adaptive optimizer that decouples isometry regularization from gradient updates, analogous to AdamW. We further reinterpret prior plasticity-preserving approaches through the lens of dynamical isometry, showing that they target only a partial measure of isometry. Across supervised and reinforcement-learning continual-learning benchmarks designed to induce plasticity loss, our methods consistently match or outperform existing approaches.

</details>


### [260] [Learning Dynamics Reveal a Hierarchy of Weight-Induced Layerwise Gram Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09744)
*Claudio Nordio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有固定读出层和二次损失的ReLU前馈网络，将梯度下降重写为训练集空间上的集体动力学，揭示了从单隐藏层到深层网络的残差动力学核结构，并指出深度三层以上需要层级化的Gram算子来实现跨层信息传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图将梯度下降从传统的权重空间动力学视角，转换到训练集空间上以场变量定义的集体动力学视角，从而更深入地理解深度网络的训练动态。

Method: 针对单隐藏层网络，从激活动力学中消去权重变量，得到由输入几何矩阵和动态共激活矩阵分解的集体核驱动的残差闭式方程。对于更深网络，推导了逐层核结构，并引入权重诱导的Gram算子处理深度三层以上的跨层信息传输。

Result: 单隐藏层网络可得到残差的闭式动力学方程；深层网络保留清晰的逐层核结构，但深度≥3时需要层级Gram算子实现层间信息传递。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解深度ReLU网络的梯度下降动力学提供了训练集空间上的集体动力学框架，揭示了不同深度下核结构的层次复杂性。

Abstract: We study feed-forward ReLU networks with fixed readout and quadratic loss. The aim is to rewrite gradient descent not primarily as a dynamics in weight space, but as a collective dynamics closed in terms of fields defined on the training-set space. For a single hidden layer, the weight variables can be eliminated from the activation dynamics, yielding a closed equation for the residuals governed by a collective kernel that factorizes into an input-geometric matrix and a dynamical co-activation matrix. For deeper networks, the residual dynamics retains a clean layer-wise kernel structure. However, from depth three onward, closure requires a hierarchy of weight-induced Gram operators that mediate information transport across layers.

</details>


### [261] [Bandits for Efficient Experimentation: Adapting to Control Group, Preferences, and Context Drifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09802)
*Udvas Das,Waris Radji,Debabrota Basu,Odalric-Ambrym Maillard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对群组用户个性化推荐场景下的线性上下文随机多臂老虎机问题，提出了Dri-MED算法，该算法能处理随时间漂移的上下文分布，并确保每步决策的期望奖励不低于基线策略π₀。算法将问题转化为具有异方差非平稳噪声的线性老虎机，实现了遗憾界为Õ(κ d² log(T)/Δ̃)和期望约束违反为Õ(d)。实验表明Dri-MED显著优于忽略漂移和偏好结构的保守基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性上下文老虎机研究通常假设上下文分布固定，但现实推荐场景中用户兴趣和上下文分布会随时间漂移；同时，实际应用（如推荐系统）需要保证每次推荐结果不低于某个基线策略（如已有推荐算法）的质量，即存在安全约束。这两个问题尚未被联合研究。

Method: 1) 将具有漂移上下文分布的群组推荐问题约简为具有平稳均值但异方差非平稳噪声的线性老虎机问题；2) 基于线性MED策略，设计了Dri-MED算法，通过异方差回归技术处理方差感知的乘性项κ；3) 理论分析给出实例依赖的遗憾界和约束违反界。

Result: 遗憾界为Õ(κ d² log(T)/Δ̃)，其中Δ̃为约束感知的次优性间隙；约束违反期望为Õ(d)。数值实验表明Dri-MED显著优于忽略漂移和偏好结构的保守基线方法。

Conclusion: Dri-MED有效解决了上下文分布漂移下的约束线性老虎机问题，在理论保证和实际性能上均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: We consider a variant of the linear contextual stochastic multi-armed bandits, where the learner must provide recommendations to a group of users, each having its personalized preference vector, and in the presence of context distributions that are drifting over time. Under practitioner-friendly assumptions, we reduce this setting to linear bandit with stationary mean but heteroskedastic and non-stationary noise. We further study the case when the learner must ensure the mean reward of each decision must exceed that of a baseline strategy $\boldsymbolπ_0$ at each decision step. We introduce Dri-MED, an algorithm inspired from the linear version of the MED strategy, and carefully adapted to handle the non-stationary heteroskedastic noise. We show that the instance-dependent regret scales as $\tilde{\mathcal O}\left(\fracκ{\tildeΔ}d^2(\log(T)\right)$, where $\tildeΔ$ is the constraint-aware sub-optimality gap subject to policy $π_0$, with variance-aware multiplicative term $κ$ that we carefully handle using heteroskedastic regression. We further show Dri-MED enjoys $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$ expected constraint violations. Our numerical results suggest that Dri-MED significantly outperforms conservative baselines that ignores the drift and preference structure.

</details>


### [262] [Perturbative Contrastive Physical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09756)
*Kyungeun Kim,Amanuel Anteneh,Israel Klich,Olivier Pfister,J. M. Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Perturbative Contrastive Physical Learning (PCPL)框架，通过测量物理系统在受控扰动下的响应对比实现学习，无需外部处理器或显式反向传播。在弹簧网络和连续变量光子电路两个平台上验证了分类与模拟乘法任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解物理系统对扰动的响应是认识物理系统的关键，而对比不同条件下的响应差异为学习提供了机制。现有方法如平衡传播和频率传播各有局限，亟需一个统一且更通用的框架来实现物理系统中的自主学习。

Method: PCPL通过可控改变输入、边界条件、参数或解释函数，产生物理状态的可测量对比。对比驱动的更新可反映局部灵敏度或全局逆问题结构，但无需集中梯度计算。学习几何从系统自身物理响应中隐式涌现。在弹簧网络中通过测量位移和力更新键刚度；在光子电路中通过x正交测量和雅可比有限差分估计进行训练。

Result: 在弹簧网络和连续变量光子电路两个平台上成功学习了分类任务。进一步展示了连续变量光子电路可被训练实现模拟乘法，向更自主的物理学习系统迈进一步。

Conclusion: PCPL统一并扩展了平衡传播和频率传播等先前方法，证明了对比驱动的物理学习可以无需外部处理器或显式反向传播，为构建更自主的物理学习系统提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Responses to perturbations are key to understanding physical systems. The ability to contrast such responses by comparing how a system reacts under slightly different conditions provides a mechanism for learning. Here, we introduce Perturbative Contrastive Physical Learning (PCPL), a general framework in which learning emerges from measurable contrasts between physical states produced by controlled changes to inputs, boundary conditions, parameters, or interpreter functions. PCPL unifies and extends prior approaches: Equilibrium Propagation is rooted in contrasts between free and nudged equilibria in energy-based systems, while Frequency Propagation corresponds to contrasts extracted from sinusoidally driven, frequency-demodulated responses. We show that contrast-driven updates can reflect either local sensitivities or global inverse-problem structure, yet do not require centralized gradient computation. Instead, effective learning geometry emerges implicitly from the system's own physical response, allowing learning behavior to arise without an external processor or explicit backpropagation. We demonstrate PCPL in two platforms: (i) spring networks that update bond stiffness using measured displacements and forces, and (ii) continuous-variable photonic circuits trained via x quadrature measurements and finite-difference estimates of the Jacobian. Both platforms successfully learn classification tasks. We further show that a continuous-variable photonic circuit can be trained to implement analog multiplication, illustrating a step toward more autonomous physical learning systems.

</details>


### [263] [Topological Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09806)
*Lennart Bastian,Samuel Leventhal,Mustafa Hajij,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑神经算子（TNOs），一种在细胞复形上进行算子学习的框架，通过离散外微积分实现跨维度特征耦合，并进一步提出层次化TNOs（HTNOs）以捕获长程依赖，在多个PDE基准上取得更优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子（NOs）局限于定义在点或边上的函数，缺乏对物理量几何支撑（如梯度、旋度、散度）的显式建模，无法自然处理跨维度交互与拓扑约束。

Method: 将数据表示为定义在任意维细胞上的特征，利用离散外微积分实现梯度/旋度/散度型算子驱动的跨维度耦合；将信息流动路径（由固定拓扑算子决定）与信息变换方式（可学习）解耦；HTNOs进一步引入可学习的粗化复形以传播全局拓扑信息。

Result: 在多个PDE基准（含不规则几何流问题）上，TNOs和HTNOs相比现有方法精度提升；消融实验验证了原生高阶与拓扑结构的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了现有神经算子方法，为算子学习提供了尊重物理守恒性与几何兼容性的新范式。

Abstract: We introduce Topological Neural Operators (TNOs), a principled framework for operator learning on cell complexes that lifts neural operators (NOs) from functions on points and/or edges to topological domains. TNOs represent data as features defined on cells of varying dimension and model their interactions through Discrete Exterior Calculus, enabling explicit cross-dimensional coupling via gradient-, curl-, and divergence-type operators. The key design principle is to decouple where information flows, as governed by fixed topological operators, from how it is transformed (which is learned), yielding models that respect the geometric support of physical quantities and expose conservation and compatibility structure. We further propose Hierarchical TNOs (HTNOs), which incorporate learned coarse complexes to propagate long-range and topology-dependent information. Our framework subsumes existing NOs as a special case, providing a unified perspective on operator learning across discretizations. Across a range of PDE benchmarks, including irregular-geometry flow problems, TNOs and HTNOs improve accuracy; controlled studies further isolate the benefits of native higher-rank and topological structure. Project page: https://circle-group.github.io/research/TNO

</details>


### [264] [An Agency-Transferring Model-Free Policy Enhancement Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09825)
*Anton Bolychev,Georgiy Malaniya,Sinan Ibrahim,Pavel Osinenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将现有次优基线策略嵌入强化学习训练的方法，通过仲裁机制在训练初期依赖基线策略、逐步转移至学习策略，最终得到无需基线支持的独立神经网络，在保持高目标到达率的同时提升训练效率并超越基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从零训练RL策略成本高昂（需精心设计奖励、环境、大量调参和计算），而许多控制问题已有功能正常但次优的基线策略可用，本文旨在利用现有基线提升RL训练效率。

Method: 提出一种仲裁机制：每一步在基线策略和可训练的学习策略间进行选择，训练初期强依赖基线策略，逐步将控制权转移给学习策略；同时形式化定义了基线策略"功能性"的条件（高概率到达并停留在目标集），并利用该性质设计仲裁机制。

Result: 在连续控制基准上，所提方法回报与竞争方法相当或更优，且在整个训练过程中（包括最终无基线阶段）保持最高的目标到达率。

Conclusion: 理论分析给出了该行为的形式化解释，并推导了最终无基线阶段学习策略目标到达概率的下界；实验证明了方法在效率和性能上的优势。

Abstract: Training reinforcement learning (RL) policies from scratch is
  costly: it requires careful reward and environment design,
  extensive tuning, and substantial computation.
  Yet many control problems already have a functional but
  suboptimal policy available as a baseline.
  This paper proposes a method for embedding such a baseline into
  the RL training process, simultaneously improving training
  efficiency relative to from-scratch methods and producing a
  learning policy that outperforms the baseline.
  At each step, the method arbitrates between the baseline policy
  and a trainable learning policy, initially relying strongly on
  the baseline policy and then progressively transferring agency to
  the learning policy.
  By the end of training, the learning policy is a standalone
  neural network that operates without baseline policy support.
  The paper formalizes what it means for the baseline policy to be
  functional: under this policy, the agent reaches a goal set and
  remains there with high probability.
  The proposed arbitration mechanism is designed to exploit this
  property during training, yielding high goal-reaching rates right
  from the beginning of training.
  A theoretical analysis provides a formal interpretation of this
  behavior under stated assumptions and extends it to the final
  baseline-free regime, where explicit lower bounds are derived for
  the goal-reaching probability of the standalone learning policy.
  Empirical results on continuous-control benchmarks show that the
  proposed method achieves returns that match or exceed those of
  competitive approaches, while maintaining the highest
  goal-reaching rates throughout training among the compared
  methods -- including in the final stage, where the learning policy
  operates without any baseline support.

</details>


### [265] [iOSWorld: A Benchmark for Personally Intelligent Phone Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09764)
*Lawrence Keunho Jang,Mareks Woodside,Geronimo Carom,Andrew Keunwoo Jang,Jing Yu Koh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iOSWorld是首个基于原生iOS模拟器的交互式基准测试，包含26个新构建的iOS应用、持久化用户身份和133个任务，用于评估移动代理在单应用、多应用及记忆与个性化任务上的表现。最佳配置总体准确率52%，多应用任务仅37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动代理基准测试缺乏个性化能力，无法评估代理基于用户身份、历史记录和偏好进行推理的能力，而一个有用的手机代理需要具备这种个人智能。

Method: 构建了包含26个新iOS应用的模拟器环境，这些应用包含交易、消息、旅行记录、社交关系和金融活动等互联数据。设计了133个任务，分为单应用（27个）、多应用（60个，跨越2-8个应用）和记忆与个性化（46个）三类。在纯视觉和视觉+XML两种设置下评估前沿及开源计算机使用模型。

Result: 最佳配置总体准确率52%，多应用任务仅37%。视觉+XML特权访问将前沿模型性能提升最多26个百分点，但较小模型并未从辅助功能树输入中获益。

Conclusion: iOSWorld作为开源基准测试发布，包含所有应用、种子数据、任务、评分标准和评估代码，填补了移动代理个性化评估的空白。

Abstract: A useful phone agent needs to be personally intelligent. It should reason over a user's identity, history, and preferences as they exist on the device, not just follow isolated instructions in an impersonal sandbox. Existing mobile agent benchmarks lack this kind of personalization. We introduce iOSWorld, the first interactive native iOS simulator benchmark built around a persistent user identity spanning 26 newly built iOS apps. These apps contain connected data such as transactions, messages, travel records, social relationships, and financial activity. iOSWorld includes 133 tasks across three increasingly difficult categories. Single-app tasks (27) test one app, multi-app tasks (60) span 2 to 8 apps, and memory and personalization tasks (46) require agents to infer patterns from personal data. We evaluate frontier and open-source computer-use models in both vision-only and privileged vision+XML settings. The best configuration reaches 52\% overall but only 37\% on multi-app tasks. Privileged vision+XML access improves frontier models by up to 26 percentage points, while smaller models do not benefit from added accessibility-tree input. We release iOSWorld as an open-source benchmark with all apps, seeded data, tasks, rubrics, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [266] [Zero Touch Predictive Orchestration: Automating Time-Series Models for the Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09787)
*Abd Elghani Meliani,Arora Sagar,Adlen Ksentini,Raymond Knopp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用数据混合方法解决云边连续体中节点冷启动问题的时间序列预测架构，通过轻量级资源暴露器动态发现节点并收集遥测数据，结合公开高分辨率数据集TimeTrack进行混合训练，配合神经架构搜索引擎自动生成高精度基线模型，有效缓解了因缺乏历史数据导致的冷启动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边连续体（CEC）中新发现的节点缺乏历史数据，无法训练本地化预测模型；通用模型又无法捕捉独特硬件和微服务行为，导致编排器面临严重的"冷启动"问题。

Method: 提出完全自动化的时间序列预测架构：(1) 轻量级技术无关的资源暴露器（RE）动态发现节点并持续收集可定制遥测数据；(2) 将稀疏的本地样本与公开高分辨率数据集TimeTrack自动合并；(3) 通过神经架构搜索（NAS）引擎自动生成高精度基线模型。

Result: 将目标数据与TimeTrack混合有效缓解了冷启动挑战，显著提高了MSE、MAE和MAPE指标下的预测精度，并加速了收敛速度，优于仅使用稀疏本地样本、仅使用通用数据集或混合其他标准数据集的方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据混合方法为持续MLOps部署建立了稳健基础，能够有效应对云边连续体中的节点冷启动问题。

Abstract: The Cloud-Edge Continuum (CEC) enables latency-critical applications by distributing resources to the far edge, but its extreme volatility makes proactive Zero Touch Management via time-series forecasting essential. However, orchestrators face a severe "cold start" problem: newly discovered nodes lack the historical data required to train localized predictive models, while generalized models fail to capture unique hardware and microservice behaviors. To solve this, we propose a fully automated time-series prediction architecture driven by a novel data-mixing methodology. At the infrastructure level, we introduce a lightweight, technology-agnostic Resource Exposer (RE) that dynamically discovers nodes and continuously collects customizable telemetry (e.g., compute, network, energy). To overcome the sparsity of these initial local samples, our framework automatically merges them with TimeTrack, our publicly available, high-resolution dataset collected at 45-second intervals. This synergizes TimeTrack's foundational, high-frequency temporal patterns with the precise calibration of the local node data. Processed through a Neural Architecture Search (NAS) engine, the system automatically generates highly accurate baseline models. Experimental results demonstrate that merging the target data with TimeTrack effectively mitigates the cold start challenge. This integration significantly improves forecasting accuracy measured in Mean Squared Error (MSE), Mean Absolute Error (MAE), and Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) and accelerates convergence compared to training on the sparse local samples alone, training solely on generic datasets, or mixing the target data with standard alternative datasets, establishing a robust foundation for continuous MLOps deployment.

</details>


### [267] [Rethinking the Divergence Regularization in LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09821)
*Jiarui Yao,Xiangxin Zhou,Penghui Qi,Wee Sun Lee,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRPO提出用平滑的二次正则化替代硬掩码，在保持信任区域几何的同时提供连续梯度权重，提升了LLM RL训练的稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPO/GRPO的比率裁剪机制中重要性比率不能很好代理长尾词汇的分布偏移；DPPO虽然改用散度掩码但硬掩码机制在越界后直接丢弃梯度而非纠正，导致优化不稳定。

Method: 用平滑的优势加权二次正则化项替代DPPO中的硬掩码，在维持相同信任区域几何的前提下，对越界的策略偏移产生有界连续的梯度权重，提供纠正信号而非直接截断。

Result: 跨模型规模、架构和精度设置的实验表明DRPO在LLM RL训练中提升了稳定性和效率。

Conclusion: DRPO通过平滑正则化解决了硬掩码的梯度丢弃问题，是一种更稳定高效的LLM RL训练方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component of post-training large language models (LLMs). In practice, LLM RL is often off-policy because of training-inference mismatch and policy staleness, making trust-region control essential for stable optimization. Mainstream methods such as PPO and GRPO approximate this control with a ratio-clipping mechanism, but the importance ratio can be a poor proxy for distributional shift in long-tailed vocabularies. Recent work such as DPPO addresses this mismatch by replacing ratio-based clipping with a divergence-based mask, yielding a trust region defined by the sampled token's absolute probability shift. However, DPPO still relies on a hard mask: once a token crosses the trust-region boundary in a harmful direction, its gradient is discarded rather than corrected. To address this, we propose Divergence Regularized Policy Optimization (DRPO), which replaces the hard mask with a smooth advantage-weighted quadratic regularizer on policy shift. DRPO preserves the same trust-region geometry as DPPO while inducing bounded, continuous gradient weights that attenuate diverging updates and provide corrective signals beyond the boundary. Experiments across model scales, architectures, and precision settings show that DRPO improves the stability and efficiency of LLM RL training.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [268] [Accelerating Birkhoff Projection for Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07574)
*Chenrui Wang,Yixuan Qiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对4x4 Birkhoff投影问题，提出了一种基于对偶形式和牛顿法的端到端加速框架，用隐式微分替代展开微分，并设计了warp级CUDA内核，相比现有方案实现了超过20倍加速且投影精度更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流形约束超连接（mHC）通过Sinkhorn-Knopp迭代实现Birkhoff多面体投影，但该方法在反向传播时依赖展开迭代求解器，导致巨大的计算和内存开销，且在输入条件较差时收敛缓慢、投影不准确，影响mHC的范数控制和稳定性保证。

Method: 1) 利用对偶形式将4x4 Birkhoff投影问题转化为三维无约束凸优化问题，使用牛顿法快速高精度求解；2) 用隐式微分替代展开微分，实现精确梯度计算且无需存储中间状态；3) 设计warp级CUDA内核，仅使用寄存器级原语，避免全局和共享内存I/O，实现大规模并行。

Result: 相比代表性开源基线方法，所提求解器在大输入幅度下能产生更可靠的双随机投影，在大批量下实现超过20倍的端到端加速（含反向传播），且边际误差小多个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作为mHC中关键的Birkhoff投影步骤提供了高效、精确且可微的替代方案，有望推动mHC在实际深度学习系统中的部署。

Abstract: Manifold-constrained hyper-connections (mHCs) have recently been proposed as a principled extension of hyper-connections, where the residual mixing matrices are constrained to be doubly stochastic via projection onto the Birkhoff polytope. In practical mHC implementations, this constraint is enforced by Sinkhorn-Knopp iterations, and the backward pass relies on unrolling the iterative solver. This design introduces substantial computation and memory overhead, and may also yield inaccurate projections when the algorithm converges slowly on challenging inputs, undermining the intended norm-control and stability guarantees of mHCs.
  In this work, we focus on the practically important 4x4 Birkhoff projection setting and develop an end-to-end acceleration framework. By leveraging the dual formulation, we reduce the problem to a three-dimensional unconstrained convex problem and solve it with Newton's method, achieving fast convergence and high accuracy. For the backward pass, we replace the unrolled differentiation with implicit differentiation, yielding exact gradients without storing intermediate states. To exploit massive parallelism, we design a warp-level CUDA kernel that uses only register-level primitives, avoiding global and shared memory I/O.
  Extensive experiments against representative open-source baselines demonstrate that the proposed solver yields substantially more reliable doubly stochastic projections -- especially when the input magnitude is large -- and achieves significant end-to-end speedups (including the backward pass), reaching over 20x acceleration at large batch sizes while maintaining orders of magnitude smaller marginal errors.

</details>


### [269] [Large-Scale Regularized Matching on GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07777)
*Aida Rahmattalabi,Gregory Dexter,Sanjana Garg,Qinquan Song,Shenyinying Tu,Yuan Gao,Zhipeng Wang,Rahul Mazumder*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PyTorch构建的分布式多GPU LP求解器，针对大规模块对角结构线性规划问题，通过列分片并行、融合Triton内核、岭正则化连续调度和算子式编程模型，在合成负载上实现了比DuaLip-Scala数量级的加速、近线性多GPU扩展（4卡3.86倍），并突破了现有GPU求解器的规模限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产决策系统（如广告分配、内容匹配）中的大规模线性规划求解面临三大系统缺口：GPU求解器（如cuPDLP、D-PDLP）在固定硬件预算下无法容纳生产级规模；求解结果在多次运行间的时序不稳定性导致下游抖动且现有求解器缺乏显式控制；CPU求解器（如DuaLip-Scala）收敛慢且将问题形式与固定模式耦合，难以表达新约束族。

Method: 采用列分片并行策略，利用融合Triton内核和批处理操作降低每次迭代开销；用户增加时仅本地计算增长，通信限于物品级对偶变量的规约，在固定物品数下实现GPU数量近线性扩展；引入岭正则化LP提高稳定性，并通过正则化参数的连续调度平衡收敛速度与解保真度；提出算子式编程模型，以可组合原语替代DuaLip-Scala的模式绑定接口，无需修改求解循环或分布式基础设施即可表达新问题形式。

Result: 在合成负载上，该系统比DuaLip-Scala实现数量级墙钟加速；近线性多GPU扩展（4卡3.86倍）；可扩展到现有GPU求解器无法企及的规模。

Conclusion: 通过系统-算法协同设计，本文成功构建了能处理超大规模、提供时序稳定性且易于扩展的分布式多GPU LP求解器，有效填补了生产场景中现有求解器的关键缺口。

Abstract: Production decision systems such as ad allocation or content matching involve millions of users and thousands of items, reducing to large-scale linear programs with sparse block-diagonal structure across users. These LPs are solved repeatedly on recurring cadences over slowly evolving inputs. Three system gaps stand out. Scale: production instances routinely exceed the memory capacity of GPU solvers such as cuPDLP and D-PDLP under fixed hardware budgets. Temporal instability: solution variability across runs induces downstream churn and complicates SLAs, yet existing solvers provide no explicit control. Extensibility: CPU-based solvers such as DuaLip-Scala converge slowly and couple problem formulation to fixed schemas, making new constraint families difficult to express. We present a distributed multi-GPU LP solver built natively in PyTorch with systems-algorithm co-design for this structure. It adopts column-sharded parallelism with fused Triton kernels and batched operations to reduce per-iteration overhead. As users grow, only local computation increases, while communication is limited to a reduction of item-level dual variables, yielding near-linear scaling with GPU count at fixed item size. We also adopt ridge-regularized LPs to improve stability, a control absent from existing GPU solvers. A continuation schedule over the regularization parameter balances convergence speed and solution fidelity. Finally, we introduce an operator-centric programming model that replaces DuaLip-Scala's schema-bound interface with composable primitives, enabling new formulations without modifying the solve loop or distributed infrastructure. On synthetic workloads, our system achieves order-of-magnitude wall-clock speedup over DuaLip-Scala, near-linear multi-GPU scaling (3.86x on 4 GPUs), and scales beyond the reach of existing GPU solvers.

</details>


### [270] [Cost-Aware Speculative Execution for LLM-Agent Workflows: An Integrated Five-Dimension Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07846)
*Faisal Fareed*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LLM-agent工作流的投机执行方法，通过五项设计决策（定价、概率估计、成本阈值等）在延迟与成本之间进行权衡，并确保仅在可回滚的操作上触发投机，同时提供了完整的校准流水线和验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent工作流中大量时间浪费在等待上游操作完成，而投机执行可以利用空闲时间提前执行下游操作。但每次投机都涉及真实金钱成本（按token计费），且成功概率难以估计并随时间漂移，亟需一种能平衡延迟与成本的方法。

Method: 围绕五项设计决策：(D1)在上游未完成时启动下游操作；(D2)按输入/输出分别定价；(D3)暴露单一操作旋钮调节延迟与成本；(D4)基于期望值规则，包含失败加权成本和偏好调整阈值；(D5)使用贝叶斯Beta-Binomial后验估计成功概率，先验基于依赖类型分类。同时引入可容许性前置条件（无副作用、幂等或可提交屏障），并包含五阶段校准流水线。

Result: 提供闭式解证明规则在分支因子增长时自限；与四种最接近的系统（DSP、Speculative Actions v2、Sherlock、B-PASTE）对比，显示出各维度的差异化优势；合成验证套件验证了决策边界、概率阈值、后验恢复和流式取消行为。

Conclusion: 该方法为LLM-agent工作流中的投机执行提供了基于成本感知的实用方案，具有自限性、可校准和可回滚保障，适合在商业LLM场景中部署。

Abstract: LLM-agent workflows chain model calls and tool invocations, and spend most of their wall-clock time waiting on upstream operations before downstream ones can start. Speculative execution can reclaim that idle time by launching a downstream operation with a predicted upstream input, but here each speculation costs real money (per-token billing) and its success probability is hard to estimate and drifts over time. This paper presents a method organized around five design decisions: (D1) start a downstream operation before its upstream completes; (D2) price each speculation in real dollars at separate input and output rates; (D3) expose a single operator dial for latency versus cost; (D4) decide via an expected-value rule with a failure-weighted cost term and a preference-adjusted threshold; and (D5) estimate the success probability with a Bayesian Beta-Binomial posterior whose prior is keyed to a dependency-type taxonomy. Variants of these ideas appear in recent work; the combination, with every decision logged in dollars, is what is new. The rule fires only on edges passing an admissibility precondition (side-effect-free, idempotent, or stageable behind a commit barrier), since a wrong speculation is rolled back by re-execution, which refunds tokens but cannot un-send an irreversible side effect. We specify the runtime mechanics, a closed-form result that the rule self-limits as the upstream branching factor grows, a five-stage calibration pipeline (offline replay, shadow, canary, online calibration, drift-triggered kill-switch), and a workload-fit rubric over eight production archetypes. Contrast tables against the four closest published systems (DSP, Speculative Actions v2, Sherlock, B-PASTE) show differentiators on every dimension, and a synthetic validation suite confirms the predicted decision boundary, probability threshold, posterior recovery, and streaming-cancellation behavior.

</details>


### [271] [FlashCP: Load-Balanced Communication-Efficient Context Parallelism for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08476)
*Zheng Wang,Eric Liu,Linan Jiang,Zhongkai Yu,Zaifeng Pan,Yue Guan,Yuke Wang,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlashCP是一个用于长上下文语言模型训练的负载均衡且通信高效的上下文并行框架，通过分片感知通信机制、Whole-Doc分片策略和启发式搜索算法，实现了最高1.63倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文并行方法由于静态序列分片和KV张量通信，存在工作负载不均衡、内核效率低下以及冗余通信等问题。

Method: 提出分片感知通信机制消除冗余KV通信；提出Whole-Doc分片策略最大化通信节约并保持负载均衡；设计启发式算法搜索近最优的分片方案以有效结合Whole-Doc和Per-Doc分片。

Result: 在多种数据集上，FlashCP相比最先进的CP框架实现了最高1.63倍的加速。

Conclusion: FlashCP通过负载均衡和通信优化显著提升了上下文并行训练的效率。
