Abstract: Context parallelism (CP) is essential for training large-scale, long-context language models, as it partitions sequences to reduce memory overhead. However, existing CP methods suffer from workload imbalance, inefficient kernels, and redundant communication due to static sequence sharding and key-value (KV) tensor communication. We present FlashCP, a load-balanced and communication-efficient framework for CP training. FlashCP introduces a sharding-aware communication mechanism to eliminate redundant KV communication and proposes a novel Whole-Doc sharding strategy that maximizes communication savings while maintaining balanced workloads. To efficiently combine Whole-Doc and Per-Doc sharding, FlashCP further designs a heuristic algorithm to search for near-optimal sharding plans. Extensive experiments show that FlashCP achieves up to 1.63x speedup over state-of-the-art CP frameworks across diverse datasets.

</details>


### [272] [Unifying von-Neumann HPC and Neuromorphic Acceleration via the EBRAINS Research Infrastructure: A Framework for High-Performance Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08515)
*Krishna Kant Singh,Charl Linssen,Eric Müller,Eleni Mathioulaki,Wouter Klijn,Lena Oden*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于EBRAINS JupyterLab的统一云工作流，通过联合身份认证、容器化和领域特定语言，实现了在冯·诺依曼超算与神经形态硬件上透明、可复现地运行尖峰神经网络，有效解决了跨异构计算架构的碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学工作流日益跨越异构计算架构，但执行单一计算模型时往往需要研究人员维护碎片化、站点特定的管道，缺乏统一的跨平台解决方案。

Method: ① 利用EBRAINS JupyterLab进行统一工作流编排；② 通过PyUNICORE向HPC站点（JUSUF、Galileo100）提交作业，通过神经形态计算平台接口向SpiNNaker-1系统提交作业；③ 采用零安装执行模式动态拉取PMIx感知的Apptainer容器到HPC计算节点，保证跨站点可复现性；④ 使用NESTML领域特定语言实现模型级可移植性，一次编写神经元模型后可自动编译为NEST（C++）或sPyNNaker后端。

Result: 通过平衡随机网络案例研究验证了该工作流的端到端可行性，实现了跨硬件平台的透明执行与模型级可移植性。

Conclusion: 本研究为硬件无关的科学工作流提供了一条实用的端到端路径，强调了容器化和领域特定语言在实现真正跨平台可复现性中的关键作用。

Abstract: Modern scientific workflows increasingly span diverse computing architectures, yet executing a single computational model across disparate systems often forces researchers to maintain fragmented, site-specific pipelines. In this paper, we address this challenge within the domain of computational neuroscience by presenting a unified, cloud-based workflow orchestrated via EBRAINS JupyterLab. This workflow enables users to transparently execute spiking neural networks on both von-Neumann supercomputers and neuromorphic hardware. Using a single federated identity, the system dispatches jobs to HPC sites (JUSUF, Galileo100) via PyUNICORE and to the SpiNNaker-1 neuromorphic system via the Neuromorphic Computing Platform Interface. To guarantee cross-site reproducibility and mitigate software version drift, we utilize a zero-installation execution mode that dynamically pulls PMIx-aware Apptainer containers to HPC compute nodes. Furthermore, we demonstrate genuine model-level portability using the NESTML domain-specific language, allowing custom neuron models to be written once and automatically compiled for either the NEST (C++) or sPyNNaker backends. Validated with a balanced random network case study, this work illustrates a practical, end-to-end path for hardware-agnostic workflows while highlighting the critical role of containerization and domain-specific languages in achieving true cross-platform reproducibility.

</details>


### [273] [APEX4: Efficient Pure W4A4 LLM Inference via Intra-SM Compute Rebalancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08761)
*Hong Guo,Nianhui Guo,Weixing Wang,Jona Otholt,Christoph Meinel,Haojin Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了W4A4量化中INT4 Tensor Core利用率与CUDA Core反量化开销之间的计算平衡问题，发现Tensor Core与CUDA Core的吞吐比ρ是决定W4A4性能的关键硬件指标。基于此，提出了APEX4框架，通过ρ感知的粒度自适应和纯INT4 GEMM内核协同设计，在不同GPU上实现了显著加速，并在LLaMA-2-70B上达到了接近FP16的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: W4A4量化虽能充分利用INT4 Tensor Core，但分组反量化操作在CUDA Core上产生巨大开销，导致现有系统不得不退回到混合精度方案。现有研究缺乏对这一瓶颈的系统性理解，亟需揭示其底层硬件原因并设计解决方案。

Method: 通过分别在四款Ampere和Ada架构GPU上进行受控基准测试，识别出Tensor Core与CUDA Core的吞吐比ρ为核心硬件指标；基于此发现，设计了APEX4框架，采用纯INT4 GEMM内核与ρ感知的粒度自适应策略协同优化。

Result: W4A4-g128核在RTX 3090（ρ=16）上获得2.0-2.5倍加速，但在A100（ρ=64）上退化为0.43-0.47倍。APEX4在LLaMA-2-70B上困惑度仅与FP16相差0.63，零样本准确率比W4Ax Atom-g128高4.0%-4.4%；在L40S、RTX 3090、A40上分别实现最高1.66倍、1.78倍、2.09倍端到端加速，通过混合粒度模式将A100恢复至1.20-1.40倍。

Conclusion: W4A4量化的可行性并非普遍成立，而是高度平台依赖的，APEX4通过ρ感知的粒度自适应能够有效缓解CUDA Core反量化瓶颈，在不同GPU架构上均取得显著加速效果。

Abstract: W4A4 quantization promises full utilization of INT4 Tensor Cores, yet group dequantization overhead on CUDA Cores has driven existing systems to mixed-precision fallbacks. We present the first systematic study of how intra-SM compute balance governs this bottleneck. Through controlled benchmarks across four GPUs from Ampere and Ada architectures, we identify the Tensor Cores to CUDA Cores throughput ratio ($ρ$) as the primary hardware indicator: the W4A4-g128 kernel yields $2.0$--$2.5\times$ speedup on RTX~3090 ($ρ=16$) yet degrades to $0.43$--$0.47\times$ on A100 ($ρ=64$) in compute-bond scenarios, establishing W4A4 viability as platform-dependent rather than universally infeasible. Guided by this finding, we build \textbf{APEX4}, which co-designs pure INT4 GEMM kernels with $ρ$-aware granularity adaptation to mitigate the CUDA Cores dequantization bottleneck. APEX4 achieves perplexity within 0.63 of FP16 on LLaMA-2-70B and outperforms W4Ax Atom-g128 by 4.0\%--4.4\% in zero-shot accuracy. Deployed as a drop-in replacement in unmodified vLLM, it delivers up to $1.66\times$ end-to-end speedup on L40S ($ρ=8$), and $1.78\times$ on RTX~3090 ($ρ=16$), $2.09\times$ on A40 ($ρ=16$), while recovering A100 ($ρ=64$) to $1.20$--$1.40\times$ via the mixed-granularity mode.

</details>


### [274] [Aperon Technical Report: Hierarchical No-Pointer Tangent-Local Search for High-Dimensional Approximate Nearest Neighbors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08813)
*Yong Fu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HNTL是一种无指针的层级局部切线空间向量索引框架，通过将高维空间划分为局部相干粒、在局部切空间上表示向量并采用无指针Block-SoA布局顺序扫描，在实现高召回率的同时显著提升内存访问效率，比传统指针追逐图遍历方法快3.61倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近邻图（如HNSW）存在指针内存开销大和随机内存访问导致CPU流水线停顿的问题，需要一种更高效的内存访问模式来加速向量检索。

Method: 1) 将高维空间划分为局部相干粒（局部切空间）；2) 使用局部PCA将高维向量降维为低维切线坐标；3) 采用无指针的Block-SoA（结构体数组）布局进行顺序扫描。

Result: 在d=768, N=10,000的各向异性流形数据上，局部PCA捕获96.3%方差；候选池C=20时Rerank Recall@10达到1.0000；NEON自动向量化的C++ Block-SoA扫描引擎比指针追逐图遍历快3.61倍（4.137 ns/vector vs 14.951 ns/vector），IPC提升3.59倍，L1/L2数据缓存未命中率接近零。

Conclusion: HNTL通过消除指针开销和优化内存访问模式，在保持高检索精度的同时大幅提升了向量索引的硬件效率，为向量内存系统提供了一种高效的核心候选生成框架。

Abstract: We present HNTL (Hierarchical No-pointer Tangent-Local), the core vector indexing and candidate generation framework of the Aperon vector memory system. Proximity graphs (e.g., HNSW) incur a heavy pointer tax in memory overhead and induce irregular memory accesses that stall CPU pipelines. HNTL resolves this by partitioning the high-dimensional space into local, coherent grains, representing vectors as low-dimensional coordinates on local tangent spaces, and scanning them sequentially using a pointerless Block-SoA (Structure-of-Arrays) layout.
  On anisotropic manifold data (d=768, N=10,000), local PCA captures 96.3% of the variance, allowing HNTL to achieve a final Rerank Recall@10 of 1.0000 with a candidate pool size of only C=20 vectors. Hardware profiling via Apple kperf CPU Performance Monitoring Unit (PMU) counters demonstrates a 3.61x speedup (4.137 ns/vector vs. 14.951 ns/vector) for our NEON auto-vectorized C++ Block-SoA scan engine over standard pointer-chasing graph traversals, driven by a 3.59x IPC (Instructions Per Cycle) and near-zero L1/L2 data cache misses.

</details>


### [275] [A Low-Latency Semantic State Estimator using Latent Predictive Learning for Dynamic Network Monitoring and Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08869)
*Hari Madhukumar,Haiyuan Li,Xiaolan Liu,Andy Corston-Petrie,Dimitra Simeonidou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于潜在预测状态估计器（LPSE）的动态网络监控与编排框架，通过拓扑自适应时序表征和语义码本实现固定成本单次推理，在Kubernetes集群上相比4B LLM端点实现82.42%语义预测精度、约41倍更低推理延迟和15倍更小内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环网络监控与编排亟需超越原始计数器采集的实时遥测语义解释，但动态云边环境中活动节点集和监控查询均在运行时变化，且控制环路要求毫秒级有界响应。

Method: 提出LPSE框架，基于潜在预测学习处理流式遥测：将变基数节点遥测转化为拓扑自适应时序表征，与监控问题融合后从语义码本返回有界答案，而非自回归文本生成；采用置换不变、槽路由的节点表征（以稳定身份键控），维持固定输入空间，泛化至节点增删重排而无需重训练。

Result: 在多节点Kubernetes集群上语义预测准确率达82.42%，推理延迟约为可部署4B LLM端点的1/41，内存占用约为1/15。

Conclusion: LPSE通过固定成本单次推理与语义可解释性设计，为动态网络环境下的实时监控与编排提供了一种高效、可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Closed-loop network monitoring and orchestration increasingly require semantic interpretations of live telemetry beyond raw counter collection. However, dynamic cloud-edge environments change both the active node set and the monitoring query at runtime, while control loops demand bounded millisecond-scale responses. We introduce a latent predictive state estimator (LPSE) for dynamic network monitoring and orchestration, built on latent predictive learning over streaming telemetry. The framework converts variable-cardinality node telemetry into topology-adaptive temporal representations, fuses them with monitoring questions, and returns bounded answers from a semantic codebook instead of autoregressive text generation. This design enables fixed-cost, single-pass inference while preserving semantic interpretability. By operating on permutation-invariant, slot-routed node representations keyed by stable identity, the model maintains a fixed input space and generalizes to node addition, removal, and reordering without retraining. Experimental results on a multi-node Kubernetes cluster show semantic prediction accuracy of 82.42% at approximately 41$\times$ lower mean inference latency and 15$\times$ smaller memory footprint compared with a deployable 4B LLM endpoint.

</details>


### [276] [When More Cores Hurts: The Vector Database Scaling Paradox in HPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08950)
*Seth Ockerman,Song Young Oh,Amal Gueroudji,Rochana Chaturvedi,Philip Carns,Nicholas Chia,Matthieu Dorier,Robert Latham,Tanwi Mallick,Swan Perarnau,Robert Underwood,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster,Robert Ross,Shivaram Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在两大超级计算机上对三种向量数据库（Qdrant、Milvus、Weaviate）进行了大规模评估，最高扩展至256个分布式工作节点（64个计算节点）。研究发现：工作负载特性会限制延迟降低、增加核心数可导致查询吞吐量下降30.67%、从16扩展到256个节点仅获得5.46倍性能提升。这些缩放悖论揭示了云原生设计与HPC系统之间的根本性不匹配，亟需面向HPC的新型向量数据库设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴科学AI工作负载（如分子搜索、气象轨迹检测、文献驱动假设生成）需要在HPC系统上高效、可扩展执行，但现有向量数据库主要针对云环境优化，其在HPC上的性能表现未经充分评估。

Method: 在两个生产级超级计算机上对Qdrant、Milvus、Weaviate三种向量数据库进行大规模评估，扩展到256个分布式工作节点（64个计算节点），使用混合读写和写后读取两种代表性工作负载模式，结合流行基准测试、多模态嵌入和新型真实世界科学数据集进行评测。

Result: 工作负载特性限制延迟降低；增加核心数导致查询吞吐量下降高达30.67%；从16扩展至256个工作节点（16倍扩展）仅获得5.46倍性能提升，暴露了云原生设计与HPC系统间的根本性不匹配。

Conclusion: 现有向量数据库的云导向架构无法充分适应HPC系统特性，需要开发面向HPC的新型向量数据库设计以解决缩放悖论问题。

Abstract: Vector databases have been designed and optimized for cloud environments; however, emerging scientific AI workloads (e.g., molecular search, meteorological trajectory detection, and literature-driven hypothesis generation) demand efficient, scalable execution on HPC systems. We present a large-scale evaluation of three state-of-the-art vector databases -- Qdrant, Milvus, and Weaviate -- on two production supercomputers, scaling to 256 distributed workers across 64 compute nodes. We evaluate representative workload patterns -- mixed read/write and write-then-read -- using popular benchmarks, multimodal embeddings, and a novel real-world scientific dataset. Our results reveal that workload characteristics can limit latency reduction, additional cores can reduce query throughput by up to 30.67%, and scaling from 16 to 256 workers (16x) only yields a 5.46x improvement. This scaling paradox exposes the fundamental mismatch between cloud-oriented designs and HPC systems, highlighting the need for new, HPC-aware vector database designs.

</details>


### [277] [Fairness-Aware and Latency-Controllable Scheduling for Chunked-Prefill LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09061)
*Haoxin Liu,Jiayi Wang,Yueshen Xu,Rui Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向分块预填充LLM推理引擎的公平感知与延迟可控调度框架，包括基于老化策略的动态优先级计算、延迟预测调度（LPRS）和主动预填充控制（APC），实验表明较FCFS降低10%以上平均端到端延迟，并显著改善P99尾延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理系统在处理异构工作负载时，采用严格先来先服务（FCFS）策略和静态token预算，导致公平性下降和不可预测的延迟抖动，头阻塞和请求饥饿问题严重损害用户体验。

Method: 1) 轻量级老化调度策略：根据累计等待时间和剩余预填充工作动态计算优先级；2) 延迟预测请求调度（LPRS）：用目标时间约束替代静态预算；3) 主动预填充控制（APC）：主动调节预填充并发度。

Result: 在NVIDIA GPU和Ascend加速器上，老化策略相比FCFS降低10%以上平均端到端延迟；LPRS和APC显著降低P99尾延迟并抑制预填充碎片化。

Conclusion: 结构化的预填充控制与时序延迟约束在功能上互补，所提框架可有效平衡调度公平性与延迟稳定性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed with highly heterogeneous workloads, chunked-prefill execution has emerged as a mainstream serving architecture. Balancing scheduling fairness and latency stability in such environments is critical; otherwise, severe head-of-line blocking and request starvation will degrade user experience. However, existing systems rely on rigid First-Come, First-Served (FCFS) policies and static token budgets, leading to fairness degradation and unpredictable latency jitter. To address these issues, we propose a fairness-aware and latency-controllable scheduling framework for chunked-prefill LLM engines. Specifically, we design a lightweight aging-based scheduling policy that dynamically calculates priorities using accumulated waiting time and remaining prefill work. Furthermore, we develop Latency-Prediction-Based Request Scheduling (LPRS) and Active Prefill Control (APC) to replace static budgets with target-time constraints and actively regulate prefill concurrency. We evaluated our scheduling framework on NVIDIA GPUs and Ascend accelerators using real-world workloads. Results show the aging policy reduces mean end-to-end latency by over 10\% compared to FCFS. Moreover, LPRS and APC significantly reduce P99 tail latency and suppress prefill fragmentation, confirming that the structural prefill control and the temporal latency constraints are fundamentally complementary. All codes have been released in Github.

</details>


### [278] [Chimera: Protocol-Aware Recovery for Confidential BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09101)
*Tong Liu,Xiaoqing Wen,Ziwei Zhou,Si Liu,Jianyu Niu,Cong Wang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CHIMERA，一种面向机密BFT共识系统的协议感知回滚弹性恢复框架。它将持久状态按协议属性分为元数据和日志，分别采用不同恢复机制，打破了现有方案在共识路径开销与恢复延迟之间的根本权衡。基于Intel TDX在Braft和ZooKeeper上的实验表明，CHIMERA在吞吐量、恢复延迟和可用性方面均优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE提供的机密BFT共识系统面临状态连续性问题：崩溃恢复时，被攻陷的主机可将回滚enclave到过时的持久状态，威胁安全性与可用性。现有防御方案存在根本性权衡——要么在关键共识路径上引入高开销降低吞吐量和增加延迟，要么导致长时间恢复延迟损害可用性。亟需打破这一权衡的方案。

Method: CHIMERA采用协议感知的恢复框架：核心洞察是共识系统中的回滚保护不应统一化。根据持久状态在状态分布、更新行为和表示形式上的根本差异，将其分为元数据和日志两类，并对每种类型应用不同的恢复机制。使用Maude形式化建模并验证安全性和活性。

Result: 在LAN和WAN环境下，基于Intel TDX在Braft和ZooKeeper上的实验表明，CHIMERA相比现有回滚弹性基线方案，实现了更高的吞吐量、更低的恢复延迟和更好的可用性。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地构建了机密BFT共识的回滚弹性恢复分类体系，将现有方法归纳为四类并揭示其固有局限。CHIMERA通过协议感知的差异化恢复机制，有效打破了开销与恢复延迟之间的权衡，为机密BFT共识系统提供了高效且安全的回滚保护方案。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have enabled confidential Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) consensus systems with confidentiality and improved scalability. However, TEEs do not provide state continuity: during recovery, a compromised host can roll back a crashed enclave to a stale persistent state, significantly threatening both safety and availability. Existing defenses face a fundamental tradeoff: they either impose substantial overhead on critical consensus paths, reducing throughput and increasing latency, or incur prolonged recovery delays, hurting availability.
  We present the first systematic taxonomy of rollback-resilient recovery for confidential BFT consensus, distilling prior approaches into four categories. We further expose their inherent limitations. Guided by this detailed analysis, we design CHIMERA, a protocol-aware recovery framework that breaks this tradeoff. Our key insight is that rollback protection in consensus systems should not be uniform. Different types of persistent states differ fundamentally in their state distribution, update behavior, and representation form. CHIMERA separates persistent state into metadata and logs according to these protocol-level properties and applies distinct recovery mechanisms to each type. We formally model CHIMERA in Maude and verify its safety and liveness properties. We implement it on Braft and ZooKeeper using Intel TDX, and evaluate it in both LAN and WAN settings. Results show that CHIMERA achieves higher throughput, lower recovery latency, and better availability than state-of-the-art rollback-resilient baselines.

</details>


### [279] [Concepts in Practice: C++ MPI Bindings for the HPC Ecosystem. From a Standardizable Core to a Composable Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09102)
*Tim Niklas Uhl,Matthias Schimek,Daniel Brommer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于C++20概念的具体化MPI绑定实现，采用分层架构：核心层包含精炼的C++20概念、标准C++构造自动映射、第三方类型定制点和基于概念的过程包装器；上层KaMPIng-v2库提供组合式管道语法；并设计了GPU/性能可移植库的轻量级适配器。所有贡献均有开源实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2008年官方C++ MPI绑定被移除标准后，第三方库要么覆盖有限MPI子集，要么针对特定用例，缺乏通用解决方案。此前虽有基于C++20概念的设计原则论文，但未给出具体接口实现。

Method: 采用分层架构设计：1) 核心层定义C++20概念形式化MPI数据缓冲区、自动映射标准C++构造、提供非侵入式定制点、基于概念包装MPI过程；2) 上层KaMPIng-v2库提供组合式管道语法；3) 为Kokkos视图、Thrust设备向量、SYCL缓冲区等GPU库设计轻量级自包含适配器。

Result: 实现了可直接使用STL容器、高度可扩展、适合标准化的底层原生C++ MPI接口；KaMPIng-v2提供内存安全且无样板代码的MPI编程体验；GPU/HPC库（Kokkos、Thrust、SYCL）可通过自包含适配器直接传递给MPI过程。

Conclusion: 该设计通过开源参考实现验证了其实际可行性，为现代C++ MPI绑定提供了完整的、可标准化的通用解决方案，弥合了HPC生态与MPI之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The official C++ MPI bindings were removed from the standard in 2008, leaving a gap that numerous third-party libraries have attempted to fill. However, existing wrappers typically cover only a limited subset of MPI or target specific use cases, falling short of a general-purpose solution. A recent conceptual paper proposed general design principles for modern C++ bindings based on C++20 concepts, without committing to a concrete interface.
  We present the first concrete realization of these principles in a layered architecture. At the foundation, we define a core layer: refined C++20 concepts formalizing the MPI standard's notion of data buffers, automatic mapping of standard C++ constructs, non-intrusive customization points for third-party types, and concept-based wrappers for MPI procedures. The result is a low-level native C++ MPI interface that works directly with STL containers, is highly extensible, and lends itself to standardization. Built on this core, we present KaMPIng-v2 -- a C++ MPI library offering the convenience and memory-safety of KaMPIng with composable, pipe-based syntax inspired by C++ ranges for efficient, boilerplate-free MPI programming. Finally, we demonstrate the core layer's broad applicability by designing lightweight adapters for GPU and performance-portability libraries, making the HPC ecosystem a first-class citizen in MPI. Kokkos views, Thrust device vectors, and SYCL buffers can be passed directly to MPI procedures, with adapter logic remaining self-contained.
  All contributions are backed by a fully functional open-source reference implementation, demonstrating the practical viability of the proposed design.

</details>


### [280] [AutoPilot: Learning to Steer High Speed Robust BFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09120)
*Liangrong Chen,Yue Zhang,Eric Zhou,Mohammad Javad Amiri,Ryan Marcus,Chenyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoPilot，一个基于强化学习的框架，能够动态调整拜占庭容错（BFT）协议的内部参数，以应对动态工作负载、异构网络条件和演化中的对抗行为。在Autobahn协议上的实验表明，AutoPilot相比默认配置降低端到端延迟49.8%，相比随机配置探索提升73.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BFT协议虽结合了基于领导者的低延迟优势和基于DAG的高吞吐量优势，但依赖静态和启发式配置，在动态工作负载、异构网络条件和演化中的对抗行为下性能退化严重。

Method: 提出AutoPilot框架，采用强化学习持续监控运行时条件并动态调整协议参数；以去中心化方式协调学习过程，以抵御对抗性数据污染；在Autobahn协议上实现并评估。

Result: AutoPilot能快速收敛到最优配置，相比默认配置降低端到端延迟49.8%，相比随机配置探索提升73.3%。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的动态参数调整能有效提升BFT协议在动态环境下的性能表现，去中心化学习机制保证了系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) protocols achieve strong performance by combining the low-latency advantages of leader-based BFT protocols with the high-throughput benefits of DAG-based data dissemination. Despite exposing a wide spectrum of internal tunable parameters, these protocols typically rely on static and heuristic configurations, which leads to performance degradation under dynamic workloads, heterogeneous network conditions, and evolving adversarial behaviors. In this paper, we present AutoPilot, a reinforcement learning-based framework that continuously monitors runtime conditions and dynamically adjusts protocol parameters online to optimize consensus performance. To ensure robustness, AutoPilot coordinates learning in a decentralized manner, providing resilience against adversarial data pollution. We implement AutoPilot on top of Autobahn, a state-of-the-art, highspeed, robust BFT protocol, and evaluate it across diverse dynamic environments. Experimental results demonstrate that AutoPilot quickly converges to the optimal configuration under changing environments, reduces end-to-end latency by 49.8% compared to the default protocol configuration, and outperforms random configuration exploration by 73.3%.

</details>


### [281] [Resource-aware Computation-Communication Overlap for multi-GPU ML Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09200)
*Minyu Cui,Miquel Pericas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出通过共享内存驱动的计算核占用率控制和通信流优先级提升两种可移植运行时控制方法，实现多GPU训练中计算与集体通信的并发执行，在NVIDIA A40/A100/H100和AMD MI250X上最高减少25.5%的执行时间，且无需修改厂商库或核实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模机器学习分布式训练中，计算与通信顺序执行导致的通信开销已成为多GPU训练的主要瓶颈，亟需在不修改底层库的前提下实现计算-通信重叠。

Method: 两种可移植运行时控制方法：1) 通过每块共享内存分配调节计算核的占用率，为通信核预留足量片上资源；2) 为通信流分配更高调度优先级，确保资源可用时通信持续推进。

Result: 在NVIDIA A40、A100、H100和AMD MI250X GPU上实现有效的计算-通信重叠，总执行时间最多降低25.5%。

Conclusion: 所提方法无需修改厂商库或核实现即可有效减少分布式训练中的通信开销，具有跨平台可移植性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large-scale machine learning (ML) has made distributed training across multiple GPUs a fundamental component of modern ML systems. As model sizes and computational throughput continue to increase, communication overhead has become a dominant bottleneck in multi-GPU training, particularly when computation and communication are executed sequentially. This work explores concurrent execution of computation and collective communication using two portable runtime controls: shared-memory-driven occupancy shaping for computation kernels and elevated scheduling priority for communication kernels. Our approach regulates computation-kernel residency through per-block shared-memory allocation, leaving sufficient on-chip resources for communication kernels to make progress. In addition, assigning higher priority to communication streams ensures steady communication progress once resources become available. Experiments on NVIDIA A40, A100, H100, and AMD MI250X GPUs demonstrate that the proposed method enables effective computation-communication overlap and reduces total execution time by up to 25.5 percent, without modifying vendor libraries or kernel implementations.

</details>


### [282] [Engineering Scalable Distributed List Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09318)
*Peter Sanders,Matthias Schimek,Tim Niklas Uhl,Thomas Weidmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视并行计算中的经典链表排序问题，基于Sibeyn二十多年前的开创性工作，通过算法与性能工程改进了稀疏统治集算法，使其能扩展到大量处理器，并在多达24,576个核心上处理数十亿元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链表排序是并行计算的经典问题，虽有众多非平凡算法和广泛应用，但过去20年缺乏将其扩展到大型机器和大规模输入的研究。

Method: 基于Sibeyn的稀疏统治集算法，结合算法与性能工程进行改进，利用间接通信、输入局部性和消息合并技术，并对算法参数影响进行详细分析以指导实践。

Result: 在多种不同结构特性的输入实例上进行了广泛实验，证明了该方法能在多达24,576个核心上扩展到数十亿元素。

Conclusion: 通过间接通信、输入局部性利用和消息合并等技术，成功将经典链表排序算法扩展到现代大规模并行系统，填补了该领域20年来的研究空白。

Abstract: The list ranking problem is one of the classical problems of parallel computing, with nontrivial algorithms and many applications as a subroutine for solving other problems. While it has been intensively studied in the early days of parallel computing, few things happened in the last 20 years. In particular, there is little work on scaling list ranking to large machines and input sizes. We reconsider list ranking starting from the ground-breaking results of Sibeyn a quarter century ago. We employ algorithm and performance engineering to improve his sparse ruling-set algorithm, making it capable of scaling to many processors, and provide a more detailed analysis of the impact of the algorithm's parameters, further guiding our practical implementation.
  We perform an extensive experimental study across a variety of input instances with different structural properties. We demonstrate that indirect communication, exploiting input locality, and message coalescing allows scaling to billions of elements on up to 24,576 cores.

</details>


### [283] [Coupling Complementary Simulations for Combined Performance and Energy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09356)
*Adel Dabah,Gregor Häfner,Sonja Happ,Simon Pickartz,Marcus Müller,Andreas Herten*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过耦合Uneyama-Doi模型（UDM）和SO粗粒化蒙特卡洛加速（SOMA）两种互补模拟框架，并针对GPU执行进行优化（内核融合、内存合并、异步随机数生成），实现了13倍整体加速和96%的能耗节省（24.5倍能耗降低），同时保持相同的科学保真度，展示了面向可持续高性能仿真的能效感知跨应用协同设计潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物模拟是软物质研究中计算需求最大的任务之一，通常需要数天执行时间和高能耗才能获得有物理意义的结果。因此，亟需在保持科学保真度的同时大幅降低计算和能耗开销。

Method: 耦合两个互补框架：UDM（连续层面高效传播浓度场）和SOMA（基于粒子的蒙特卡洛动力学解析链尺度热涨落）。各自通过内核融合、内存合并、异步随机数生成进行GPU优化（UDM提升70%，SOMA提升80%）。通过协调器库实现数据交换和多GPU时间步同步，并管理耦合工作负载分布。

Result: UDM获得70%性能提升，SOMA获得80%性能提升；整体实现13倍加速；总能耗降低24.5倍（96%节能）；保持相同科学保真度。

Conclusion: 提出的混合方法在保持科学保真度的同时大幅降低了计算和能耗足迹，展示了面向可持续高性能仿真的能效感知跨应用协同设计的潜力。

Abstract: Polymer simulations are among the most computationally demanding workloads in soft-matter research, often requiring days of execution and high energy consumption to achieve physically meaningful results. In this work, we address these challenges through the coupling and optimization of two complementary simulation frameworks: the Uneyama-Doi Model (UDM) and the SOft coarse-grained Monte Carlo Acceleration (SOMA). UDM efficiently propagates concentration fields at the continuum level, while SOMA resolves chain-scale thermal fluctuations via particle-based Monte Carlo dynamics. Each model was individually optimized for GPU execution using kernel fusion, memory coalescing, asynchronous random-number generation yielding up to 70% (UDM) and 80% (SOMA) performance improvement. The coupling is performed through our proposed coordinator library that orchestrates data exchange and synchronizes time-stepping across multiple GPUs. Further management of coupling workload distribution enabled a 13x overall speedup and 24.5x reduction in total energy usage compared to the SOMA baseline, i. e., 96% energy saving. The proposed hybrid approach maintains the same scientific fidelity while drastically reducing the computational and energy footprint, showcasing the potential of energy-aware, cross-application co-design for sustainable high-performance simulations

</details>


### [284] [Parent-Hash DAG: A Cost Analysis of Constant-Time Append for On-Chain Registries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09593)
*Ian C. Moore,Fernando Paredes Garcia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文形式化了基于父哈希的有向无环图（PHDAG）的追加操作具有O(1) gas成本，与增量默克尔树（IMT）的线性成本形成对比，并在Base Sepolia上通过实验验证了深度1-25的结果，PHDAG恒定约76,276 gas而IMT成本随深度线性增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管溯源树（provenance trees）已在运营商门控溯源基础设施中作为数据基底使用，但其核心数据结构模式——父哈希有向无环图（PHDAG）——尚未被作为独立原语进行形式化、显式常数界定，也未与标准替代方案增量默克尔树（IMT）进行基准对比。

Method: 形式化PHDAG追加操作在gas成本上为O(1)，独立于注册表规模和树深度；为IMT建立随机成本模型，将每次插入成本视为关于叶子索引的随机变量，并导出均值和方差的闭式表达式；在Base Sepolia上对深度1-25进行实证验证。

Result: PHDAG在实验范围内深度不变，gas成本恒定为76,276（标准差约6）；IMT成本随深度线性增长。IMT更便宜的交叉点远低于所有被调查的生产注册表的实际深度。此外，实现了从公开事件日志中以线性时间、无链下依赖的可信注册表重建。

Conclusion: PHDAG在深度不变的gas成本方面优于IMT，适合深度较大的生产环境；同时支持无需信任的线性时间注册表重建，是一种高效且可验证的溯源数据结构。

Abstract: Provenance trees are append-only directed acyclic graphs of artifact registrations anchored on a public blockchain, recently introduced as the data substrate of operator-gated provenance infrastructure. Their defining data-structural pattern is a parent-hash directed acyclic graph (PHDAG), in which each append performs a constant number of storage writes to previously-untouched slots. This pattern has not previously been isolated as a standalone primitive, formally bounded with explicit constants, or benchmarked against the standard alternative, the incremental Merkle tree (IMT). We formalize PHDAG append as O(1) in gas cost, independent of registry size and tree depth, and develop a stochastic cost model for IMT in which per-insert cost is a random variable over the leaf index, deriving closed-form expressions for its mean and variance. We validate both analyses empirically on Base Sepolia across tree depths 1 to 25. PHDAG is observed to be depth-invariant at 76,276 gas (standard deviation about 6 gas), while IMT cost grows linearly with depth. The crossover below which IMT is cheaper falls far beneath the depths of every production registry surveyed. We further establish trustless registry reconstruction from public event logs in linear time with no off-chain dependency.

</details>


### [285] [FMplex: Model Virtualization for Serving Extensible Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09643)
*Hetvi Shastri,Pragya Sharma,Walid A. Hanafy,David Irwin,Mani Srivastava,Prashant Shenoy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FMplex是一种将基础模型（FM）作为虚拟化基座的服务系统，通过虚拟基础模型（vFM）抽象实现多任务共享同一物理FM，并结合批感知公平队列调度器，在92个下游任务和16个FM变体上，相比空间分区和尽力协同部署分别降低延迟80%和33.3%，集群规模下可承载6倍以上的任务数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型服务系统为每个下游任务独立部署模型实例，导致FM骨干网络重复部署、加速器内存浪费，且无法利用批处理与加载成本的摊销机会。

Method: 提出FMplex系统，核心包含：(1) 虚拟基础模型（vFM）抽象，使任务共享同一物理FM但保持逻辑隔离；(2) 批感知公平队列调度器，结合加权任务级共享与跨任务、任务内批处理。

Result: 在7个FM骨干网络（16个变体）和92个下游任务上，FMplex相比空间分区降低延迟80%，相比尽力协同部署降低33.3%，集群规模下可承载6倍以上的任务数。

Conclusion: FMplex通过FM虚拟化共享机制有效提升模型服务系统的资源利用率和任务吞吐，在保持任务隔离的同时显著降低延迟。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) are increasingly used as backbones for downstream tasks across language, vision, time-series, and multimodal applications. Yet existing model-serving systems deploy each customized task as an independent model instance, thereby replicating heavyweight backbones, wasting accelerator memory, and losing opportunities to amortize batching and loading costs. This paper presents FMplex, a serving system that treats FM backbones as a virtualization substrate for deployment sharing. FMplex presents each task with a virtual foundation model (vFM), a logically private FM instance backed by a shared physical FM. This abstraction lets independently customized tasks share a backbone while preserving task-specific extensions, independent lifecycles, and task-level isolation. In addition, we propose a batch-aware fair-queueing scheduler that combines weighted task-level sharing with inter- and intra-task batching across colocated tasks. We implement a FMplex-based serving stack spanning task construction, sharing-aware deployment, and runtime execution. Across 7 FM backbones (16 variants) and 92 downstream tasks, FMplex reduces latency by up to 80% over spatial partitioning and 33.3% over best-effort co-location, while hosting up to 6x more tasks at cluster scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [PathoSage: Towards Multi-Source Evidence Adjudication in Pathology via Experience-Aware Agentic Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07549)
*Chengyang Zhang,Wenchuan Zhang,Bo Li,Mengran Li,Bob Zhang,Yuhao Yi,Hong Bu,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathoSage是一个三阶段框架，通过显式分离知识检索、证据收集和证据裁决，以及引入Beta-Bernoulli经验系统建模工具可靠性，有效缓解病理MLLM中的幻觉和分类器分歧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理MLLM在补丁级推理中存在幻觉问题，而智能体系统将工具输出和检索知识混入共享上下文，导致决策易受冲突证据和上下文污染影响，缺乏可靠的证据裁决机制。

Method: 提出PathoSage三阶段框架：(1) 显式分离知识检索、证据收集和证据裁决；(2) 核心组件Structured Evidence Deliberation独立评估异质证据、进行冲突分析并在全新上下文中生成最终判断以减少锚定偏差；(3) 无训练的Beta-Bernoulli经验系统，通过连续信用分配建模工具长期可靠性并构建相似性加权先验。

Result: PathoSage有效缓解了VQA幻觉和分类器分歧问题，在病理MLLM和智能体基线上取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 显式的证据裁决机制和可靠性感知的工具建模是构建鲁棒病理智能体的关键要素。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and agent workflows have shown strong promise for computational pathology, yet reliable patch-level reasoning remains challenging. End-to-end pathology MLLMs often hallucinate morphological features, while recent agentic systems usually merge tool outputs and retrieved knowledge into a shared context, making decisions vulnerable to conflicting evidence and context contamination. We propose PathoSage, a three-stage framework that explicitly separates knowledge retrieval, evidence collection, and evidence adjudication for patch-level pathology multimodal reasoning. Its core component, Structured Evidence Deliberation, independently evaluates heterogeneous evidence from tools, performs conflict analysis, and generates the final judgment in a fresh context to reduce anchoring bias. We further introduce a training-free Beta-Bernoulli experience system with continuous credit assignment to model long-term tool reliability and construct similarity-weighted priors for future tool use. Experiments show that PathoSage effectively mitigates VQA hallucinations and classifier disagreement, outperforming strong pathology MLLM and agentic baselines. Our results highlight explicit evidence adjudication and reliability-aware tool modeling as key ingredients for robust pathology agents.

</details>


### [287] [OmniMem: Perturbation-aware Memory Compression for Streaming Audio-Visual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07577)
*Guangzhi Sun,Yixuan Li,Yudong Yang,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniMem是一个面向音视频大语言模型的高效流式推理框架，通过模态感知的记忆分配、扰动感知记忆选择和预算感知微调，在相同内存预算下相比强基线提升2-4%的绝对准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音视频大语言模型在处理长视频时，视频token和KV缓存随长度线性增长，严重限制了长视频推理效率；同时现有压缩方法对所有token一视同仁，忽略了视觉和音频模态间严重的token数量不均衡问题。

Method: 1) 模态感知记忆分配策略：分别管理视觉和音频上下文，解决模态间token不均衡；2) 扰动感知记忆选择：保留信息丰富且非冗余的KV状态；3) 预算感知微调：在部署约束下鼓励模型将有用信息整合到保留记忆中。

Result: 在VideoMME Long、LVBench和LVOmniBench三个长视频基准上，基于video-SALMONN 2+和Qwen-2.5-Omni的评估显示：OmniMem在相同内存预算下比强训练无关压缩基线提升2-4%绝对准确率，微调后额外提升1-2%。

Conclusion: OmniMem通过模态感知和扰动感知的设计，有效缓解了音视频LLM长视频推理中的内存瓶颈，验证了针对多模态特性设计的记忆压缩策略优于统一压缩方法。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (LLMs) hold strong promise for long-form video understanding, yet their long-video inference is fundamentally limited by the linear growth of video tokens and key-value (KV) caches. We present OmniMem, a memory-efficient streaming framework designed specifically for audio-visual LLMs. Unlike existing compression methods that treat all tokens uniformly, OmniMem introduces a modality-aware memory allocation strategy that separately manages visual and audio contexts, addressing the severe token imbalance between the two modalities. OmniMem further preserves informative and non-redundant KV states through perturbation-aware memory selection, enabling compact memory without sacrificing long-range understanding. To strengthen compression under realistic deployment constraints, we also explore budget-aware fine-tuning, which encourages the model to consolidate useful information into retained memory. Experiments on VideoMME Long, LVBench, and LVOmniBench with video-SALMONN 2+ and Qwen-2.5-Omni show that OmniMem consistently improves over strong training-free compression baselines by 2-4% absolute accuracy under the same memory budgets, with an additional 1-2% gain after fine-tuning.

</details>


### [288] [Syll: Open-Source Personal Automation with Cross-Surface Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07594)
*Bo Zhang,Borui Zhang,Chenghao Jiang,Minglei Shi,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Syll 是一个开源、自托管的 multimodal agent harness，通过统一 MCP/API 工具、CLI 执行和 GUI 控制，支持人机双向交互：用户可通过演示教学技能，agent 执行结果以日志、关键帧和审批检查点等形式呈现，所有记忆、技能和治理规则均以可编辑的本地工件形式存储，便于审计和扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人 AI agent 系统大多局限于单一界面（如仅 API、仅 CLI 或仅 GUI），且缺乏对用户教学和可审计性的支持，难以满足跨异构接口（API、Shell、Web、桌面 GUI）协调操作的实际需求。

Method: 提出 Syll，一个模块化运行时架构，包含：(1) 统一 multimodal 路由，支持 MCP/API、CLI 和视觉 GUI 控制；(2) 双向用户-agent 交互层，用户通过直接演示教学，agent 执行后生成 multimodal 证据（日志、关键帧、审批检查点）；(3) 外部化内存、技能、例程和治理规则为可编辑的本地工件。

Result: 在生产桌面应用（Adobe Photoshop、Adobe Audition、Stardew Valley、macOS Finder 等）上验证了实现效果；通过机制导向实验验证了 multimodal 路由、可教学的 GUI 回放和持久化本地工件三大核心功能的有效性。

Conclusion: Syll 可作为个人自动化的开源基础框架，支持用户教学、审计和持续扩展。

Abstract: Personal AI agents must increasingly operate across APIs, shells, web surfaces, and desktop GUIs, yet many systems remain tuned to a single interface and offer limited support for user teaching and auditability. We present Syll, an open-source, self-hosted multimodal agent harness that unifies MCP/API tools, CLI execution, and visual GUI control in a modular runtime, enabling agents to coordinate computer use across heterogeneous interfaces while streamlining how users and agents exchange information. At the core of Syll is a bidirectional user-agent interaction layer: users teach procedures through direct demonstration, which Syll compiles into reusable skills; agent execution is translated back into multimodal evidence -- logs, keyframes, and approval checkpoints -- for inspection and control. Syll further externalizes memory, skills, routines, and governance as editable local artifacts, supporting straightforward inspection, extension, and downstream development. Our implementation has been validated on production desktop applications including Adobe Photoshop, Adobe Audition, Stardew Valley, macOS Finder and others. We report mechanism-oriented studies that validate multimodal routing, teachable GUI replay, and persistent local artifacts. We hope Syll can serve as a practical open-source foundation for personal automation that users can teach, inspect, and continuously extend.

</details>


### [289] [A case study of evaluating AI agents on a neuroscience data-to-discovery pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07718)
*Kai A. Horstmann,Ethan Lin,Alice A. Robie,Jennifer J. Sun,Kristin Branson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对通用编程Agent在果蝇光遗传学数据到科学发现的全流程流水线上的表现进行了实证分析。Agent能够解决部分阶段性任务，但难以完成端到端流水线。主要瓶颈在于缺乏预定义评判标准时无法依靠科学判断进行自我评估，以及视觉自评估能力不足。研究还识别了计算资源管理、大规模数据泛化等现有基准测试中缺失的挑战，并提炼了构建科学任务与评估准则的原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究管线中存在大量耗时数天到数月的软件开发瓶颈，科学家更关注正确性和鲁棒性而非实现细节。Agentic AI工具为自动化这些瓶颈提供了有希望的路径，但现有研究缺乏在真实科学场景下对通用编程Agent进行大规模、符合领域专家标准的实证评估。

Method: 选取果蝇光遗传学数据到科学发现的完整流水线作为测试场景，使用远超现有基准测试的任务规模（数量级更大的数据集），采用领域专家标准作为评估指标，对通用编程Agent在流水线各阶段及端到端任务中的表现进行实证研究，并通过分析Agent的代码迭代过程诊断其失败模式。

Result: Agent能够解决部分独立的流水线阶段任务，表明阶段性自动化是可行的；但在缺乏预定义迭代标准时，Agent无法依靠科学判断评估自身解决方案；Agent尝试通过中间输出视觉检查进行自评估，但大多无法正确解读或据此行动；完整的端到端流水线超越当前Agent能力；发现了计算资源管理、大规模数据泛化等现有基准中缺失的挑战。

Conclusion: 阶段性自动化可行但端到端自动化仍超出当前Agent能力范围；科学判断能力的缺失是关键开放挑战；未来需要构建更贴近真实科学场景的任务和评估准则，并关注计算资源管理、大规模数据泛化等现有基准中未被充分覆盖的问题。

Abstract: Agentic AI tools offer a promising path to automating software development bottlenecks in scientific research pipelines, particularly for stages that take domain experts days to months to build, where scientists care about correctness and robustness, not implementation details. We present an empirical study of general-purpose coding agents on a fly optogenetics data-to-discovery pipeline. We assess agents on tasks substantially larger than existing benchmarks, datasets orders of magnitude bigger, and evaluation criteria grounded in domain expert standards. We show that agents can solve several individual pipeline stages, suggesting stage-level automation is tractable. By analyzing agents' code iterations, we show that they struggle most when there is not a pre-defined criterion to iterate on, and they must instead use their scientific judgment to assess their current solution, a key open challenge. Mirroring scientific practice, they sometimes attempt visual inspection of intermediate outputs for self-evaluation, but largely fail to interpret what they see or act on it appropriately. Solving the end-to-end pipeline correctly requires stringing together successes across all pipeline stages, and this is beyond agents' current abilities. We identify challenges largely absent from existing benchmarks, including computational resource management and generalization to large held-out data collections. Finally, we distill principles for constructing scientific tasks and rigorous evaluation criteria for open-ended problems.

</details>


### [290] [Why Limit the Residual Stream to Layers and Not Tokens? Persistent Memory for Continuous Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07720)
*Mujtaba Farhan,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGCLR，通过门控概念流（写门、读门、遗忘门）为CoCoNuT添加持久残差记忆，解决连续潜在推理中的概念瓶颈问题，在GSM8K、HotpotQA和ProsQA上取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoCoNuT在连续潜在推理中存在概念瓶颈：中间隐藏状态被覆盖，导致模型随推理深度增加丢失早期计算的关键事实，在HotpotQA上（10.4% EM）甚至不如CoT基线（11.0% EM）。

Method: 提出AGCLR（自适应门控连续潜在推理），在CoCoNuT基础上增加门控概念流（Gated Concept Stream）：一个跨所有推理步骤的持久残差记忆，由三个学习门控制——写门（提交中间事实到记忆）、读门（检索相关先前状态）、遗忘门（修剪不相关上下文）。

Result: 以GPT-2为基座模型，在GSM8K、HotpotQA和ProsQA上评估，AGCLR在所有类型数据集上取得一致性改进，且随着课程深度增加性能差距扩大，直接解决了概念瓶颈。

Conclusion: AGCLR通过持久残差记忆有效缓解了连续潜在推理中的概念瓶颈问题，证明了在潜在空间推理中保留中间状态的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable reasoning abilities on mathematical and multi-hop planning tasks. The CoCoNuT (Chain of Continuous Thought) paradigm~\cite{hao2024coconut} extends this by enabling models to reason in latent space, exploring multiple reasoning paths simultaneously rather than committing to a single chain early on. However, we identify a limitation we term the \textbf{concept bottleneck}. At each reasoning pass, intermediate hidden states are overwritten, causing the model to lose critical facts computed in earlier steps as reasoning depth increases. We observe this empirically. On HotpotQA, vanilla CoCoNuT (10.4\% EM) fails to improve over the CoT baseline (11.0\% EM), and performance degrades with curriculum depth on GSM8K. To address this, we propose \textbf{AGCLR} (Adaptive Gated Continuous Latent Reasoning), which augments CoCoNuT with a \textit{Gated Concept Stream}. A persistent residual memory maintained across all reasoning passes, controlled by three learned gates: a \textit{write} gate that commits intermediate facts to memory, a \textit{read} gate that retrieves relevant prior states, and a \textit{forget} gate that prunes irrelevant context. Evaluated on GSM8K, HotpotQA, and ProsQA using GPT-2 as our base model, AGCLR achieves consistent improvements across all types of datasets. With the performance gap compounding as curriculum depth increases, directly resolving the concept bottleneck. Code available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/JJJJ/README.md

</details>


### [291] [Automatic Extraction of Structured Information from Brain MRI Reports Using an Open-Weight Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07721)
*Kaouther Mouheb,Amos Pomp,Antoine Manenti,Romy de Haan,Farog Faghir,Joy Martens,Harro Seelaar,Francesco Mattace-Raso,Meike W. Vernooij,Frank J. Wolters,Stefan Klein,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估开源大语言模型LLaMA 3.1从荷兰语神经放射学报告中自动提取数据的性能，发现其在视觉评分、病灶检测方面表现优异，少样本提示能提升数值变量提取效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自由文本放射学报告中自动提取数据可支持大规模研究，但大型语言模型在荷兰语神经放射学报告上的性能评估尚缺乏。

Method: 分析了947份来自三级记忆诊所的脑部MRI报告，由受训医学生标注30个变量；评估LLaMA 3.1在不同语言（荷兰语vs英语翻译）和少样本提示下（不同示例选择策略）的性能，使用平衡准确率、准确率、平均绝对误差和文本相似度等指标。

Result: LLaMA 3.1在视觉评分（内侧颞叶萎缩90%/96%、全脑皮质萎缩87%、Fazekas 94%）、微出血检测（93%）和梗死检测（82%）上零样本性能高；数值变量性能较低（微出血计数80%、梗死计数66%），少样本提示可提升至92%和81%。

Conclusion: LLaMA 3.1在从荷兰语神经放射学报告中提取数据方面潜力巨大，少样本提示可改善数值变量提取，但位置相关变量仍是挑战。

Abstract: Objectives: Automatic data extraction from free-text radiology reports enables large-scale research, but few studies assessed the performance of large language models (LLMs) on Dutch neuroradiology reports. Methods: We analyzed 947 brain MRI reports from a tertiary memory clinic (2016-2021), authored by consultant neuroradiologists. Trained medical students annotated thirty variables; 100 reports were double-annotated to assess inter-rater reliability. We evaluated the performance of the open-weight LLM LLaMA 3.1 using different languages (Dutch vs. English translation) and few-shot prompting with different example selection strategies. Performance was evaluated using balanced accuracy for categorical variables, accuracy and mean absolute error for counts, and text similarity for free-text. Metrics were computed across 10 random splits of the 947 reports. Results: LLaMA 3.1 demonstrated high zero-shot performance for visual rating scores (mean [95%-CI]): Medial Temporal Atrophy: 90% [77-100%] on the left and 96% [94-99%] on the right, Global Cortical Atrophy: 87% [83-91%], and Fazekas: 94% [93-96%]. Microbleed mentions were detected with 93% accuracy [92-95%] and infarct mentions with 82% [80-84%]. Text similarity for lesion location reached 0.95 [0.95-0.96]. Performance was lower for numerical variables: 80% [78-82%] for the number of microbleeds and 66% [63-68%] for infarcts. English translation yielded comparable results. Few-shot prompting improved performance for numerical variables, achieving 92% [90-93%] for microbleeds and 81% [77-85%] for infarcts using structural similarity-based selection. Conclusion: LLaMA 3.1 shows strong potential for extracting data from Dutch neuroradiology reports. Few-shot prompting enhances performance for numerical variables, whereas challenges remain for location-specific variables.

</details>


### [292] [Some hypotheses on how chatbots work in problem-solving-driven conversations. Large Language Models as confirmation of the Innovation Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07722)
*S. F. M. van Vlijmen,H. D. Lethe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从聚合动力学、认知语言学、神经心理学和心理学的跨学科视角，论证了基础聊天机器人（基于LLM+简单界面）无法成为真正的人类思考伙伴，因为训练数据仅部分模仿人类思维，LLM训练过程编码了"人工隐喻问题传播"而非真正的理解，且进一步发展也无法改变这一根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解聊天机器人作为对话伙伴的本质能力边界，解释它们能做什么、不能做什么及其原因，为关于聊天机器人功能、益处和缺陷的社会讨论提供理论贡献。

Method: 跨学科理论分析，整合聚合动力学、认知语言学、神经心理学和心理学视角；聚焦基础聊天机器人（LLM+简单界面）以揭示核心功能特征；提出"隐喻问题传播"概念框架。

Result: （1）基于"隐喻问题传播"描述了人类理解和思维机制；（2）提出LLM训练文本数据集具有特定特征且仅部分模仿人类思维；（3）LLM训练过程从这些数据集中编码了人工隐喻问题传播；（4）基础聊天机器人无法成为与人类匹敌的思考伙伴；（5）LLM的进一步发展也无法实现这一目标。

Conclusion: 聊天机器人存在根本性局限，无法达到人类的思考和学习能力，这与Yann LeCun的观点一致，与科技巨头的乐观立场相悖；尽管如此，聊天机器人已在社会大规模使用，因此理解其工作机制具有重要的社会和政治意义。

Abstract: This article offers a perspective on the nature of chatbots as genuine conversation partners when discussing problems in relation to their solutions. What can chatbots do and what can't they do, and how can this be explained? Our argument draws on Aggregation Dynamics, Cognitive Linguistics, Neuropsychology and Psychology.
  Our argument focuses on basic chatbots in the hope of thereby making statements about the core functionality of more advanced chatbots. Basic chatbots are assumed to consist of a Large Language Model (LLM) with a simple interface.
  The main results are: a description of human understanding and thinking based on so-called metaphorical problem propagations; the hypothesis that text dataset used for training LLMs have specific characteristics and that these text datasets only partially imitate human thinking and understanding; the hypothesis that the LLM training process encodes artificial metaphorical problem propagations into an LLM from these datasets; our conclusion that a basic chatbot cannot be a thinking partner capable of matching humans; our conclusion that further development of the Large Language Model will not lead to this either.
  Yann LeCun states: "Animals and humans exhibit learning abilities and understandings of the world that are far beyond the capabilities of current AI and machine learning (ML) systems." Our conclusions are in line with this. LeCun's vision and ours are at odds with the optimism of Big Tech. That does not alter the fact that chatbots exist, that they are being used on a massive scale, by both individuals and organisations, and that it is therefore socially and politically important to understand them. Our article aims to contribute to the discussion on the functioning, benefits and drawbacks of chatbots.
  We have not yet encountered the approach we used to arrive at our conclusions in our research into how chatbots work.

</details>


### [293] [Land cover and flood type govern the detection limits of satellite-based flood mapping across diverse global flood events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07780)
*Venkatesh Kolluru,Rajat Shinde,Abdelhak Marouane,Caden Helbling,Deepak Shah,Othneil Drew,Iksha Gurung,Manil Maskey,Rahul Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了地理空间基础模型Prithvi-EO-2.0在19次分布外洪水事件（2017-2025年，覆盖六大洲、八种气候区、六种洪水机制）中的泛洪测绘表现。研究发现检测精度取决于土地覆盖和洪水类型：农田检测效果最好（IoU=52%），河流洪水检测最可靠（F1=0.69），而树木覆盖和建成区几乎无法检测（IoU=4%）。双参考验证表明模型误差部分源自参考产品定义不一致；23种失败模式中，管线工程问题主导了初期误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星遥感在洪水响应中至关重要，但地理空间基础模型在未见过的多样化洪水事件中的操作可靠性尚未得到系统评估。

Method: 使用Prithvi-EO-2.0模型处理19次分布外洪水事件，覆盖六大洲、八种气候区和六种洪水机制，并采用两种独立参考产品进行双参考验证；通过迭代管线测试识别23种失败模式。

Result: 农田检测IoU=52%，河流洪水F1=0.69；树木覆盖和建成区IoU=4%；双参考验证显示模型误差部分源于参考产品定义不一致；管线工程问题主导初期误差。

Conclusion: 建立了环境依赖的检测边界，为操作化卫星洪水测绘提供了可靠性指导。

Abstract: Floods are among the most destructive natural hazards, and their increasing frequency under climate change makes satellite-based inundation mapping essential for disaster response. Geospatial foundation models pretrained on satellite archives offer geographic transferability, but their operational reliability across diverse, unseen events remains uncharacterized. Here we deploy Prithvi-EO-2.0 across 19 out-of-distribution flood events (2017-2025) spanning six continents, eight climate zones, and six flood mechanisms, validating against two independent reference products. Detection accuracy depended jointly on land cover and flood type, with cropland yielding the highest agreement (IoU=52%) and riverine events the strongest detection (F1=0.69), while tree cover and built-up areas showed near-zero detection (IoU=4%) regardless of flood mechanism. Dual-reference validation revealed that apparent model error partly reflects definitional inconsistency between reference products rather than detection failure. Iterative pipeline testing identified 23 failure modes, with pipeline engineering dominating initial error over model capacity. These findings establish environment-dependent detection boundaries for operational satellite flood mapping.

</details>


### [294] [Reconstructing and forecasting disease trajectories of patients with Alzheimer's disease using routine data in resource-constrained settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07798)
*Ratnadeep Das,Atri Chatterjee,Sitikantha Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出统一框架GNOVA（GRU-神经ODE变分自编码器），利用常规就诊数据（无需神经影像或生物标志物）对阿尔茨海默病患者的认知评分（CDR-SB和MMSE）进行双向预测，实现了插值、外推和不确定性估计。在ADNI数据集1727名患者上CDR-SB的MAE为1.35，MMSE的MAE为2.28，年龄、BMI和APOE4状态为强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦预测患者未来认知状态，很少关注从既往就诊重建状态；量化预测不确定性的工作不足，且依赖MRI、PET、CSF等昂贵模态，限制了在资源有限环境中的部署。

Method: 提出GNOVA框架：GRU编码器接受任意数量、任意时间点的输入；神经ODE解码器实现连续估计，支持任意时间点的插值和外推；变分自编码器提供不确定性估计。仅使用常规就诊数据，无需神经影像或生物标志物。

Result: 在ADNI数据集1727名患者上，CDR-SB的MAE为1.35，MMSE的MAE为2.28。特征消融研究表明年龄、BMI和APOE4状态为强预测因子。

Conclusion: GNOVA框架能够在无需昂贵模态的情况下重建不完整的患者病史并预测未来认知状态，支持临床预后决策。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder, and its progression varies substantially across patients. Existing work aims to forecast patients' future cognitive state, with minimal focus on reconstructing the state from past visits. Furthermore, in current research, quantifying predictive uncertainty remains underexplored and relies on costly modalities such as MRI, PET, and CSF, limiting their deployment in resource-limited settings. In this research, our primary objectives are: First, bidirectional prediction of cognitive scores from irregular visits to present the complete disease trajectory. Second, to enable interpolation and extrapolation capabilities to assist clinicians in informed prognostic decision making, and third, to provide a well-calibrated uncertainty estimate for all predictions, and finally, to achieve the objectives using the modalities available during routine visits. We propose a unified framework, GNOVA: A GRU-Neural ODE Variational Autoencoder. The architecture combines a Gated Recurrent Unit encoder and a Neural ODE decoder within a variational autoencoder framework. In our work, we forecast the CDR-SB and MMSE Scores. The GRU encoder allows for any number of inputs at any time point. The Neural-ODE decoder performs continuous estimation, allowing interpolation and extrapolation at any desired time point. The Variational autoencoder allows for uncertainty estimation in predictions. We worked with 1,727 patients from the ADNI dataset over 10 years; the model achieved mean absolute errors of 1.35 and 2.28 for CDR-SB and MMSE scores, respectively, without requiring any neuroimaging or biomarker data. Feature-ablation studies revealed that age, BMI, and APOE4 status were strong predictors. The proposed framework enables the reconstruction of incomplete patient histories and the anticipation of future cognitive states.

</details>


### [295] [Improving Multimodal Reasoning via Worst Dimension Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07801)
*Haocheng Lv,Huaping Zhang,Qiuchi Li,Lei Li,Chunxiao Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有过程奖励模型（PRM）对多模态推理中的视觉基础和逻辑一致性等约束采用同等权重的启发式奖励定义，导致主导维度掩盖了单个维度的失败，无法保证推理过程的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推理需要在视觉基础到逻辑一致性等多约束下保持路径完整性，但当前PRM的启发式等权重奖励方案会导致某些维度失败被其他主导因素掩盖，从而无法保证推理过程的有效性。

Method: 论文分析了现有Process Reward Model中启发式定义且等权重分配奖励的缺陷，指出这种设计会导致维度间相互掩盖问题。

Result: 揭示了当前PRM在等多模态推理约束权重设置下，无法有效检测各维度独立失败的问题。

Conclusion: 当前的过程奖励模型设计需要改进，以避免维度间相互掩盖问题，从而更好地保证多模态推理路径的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning requires a path that retains integrity over a wide range of constraints, from visual grounding to logic consistency. However, the current Process Reward Models focus on heuristically defined rewards that equally weigh these factors, which may lead to the concealment of individual dimension failures by the dominating factors, without guaranteeing the validity of the reasoning process in general.

</details>


### [296] [Beyond Goodhart's Law: A Dynamic Benchmark for Evaluating Compliance in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07805)
*Yiyang Zhao,Zhuo Zhang,Qingxuan Le,Lizhen Qu,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAC-Bench，一个用于评估多智能体系统程序对齐的动态对抗性基准测试框架，通过SERV流水线将法律文本转化为可执行场景，揭示了前沿模型在任务成功与合规性之间的系统性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架忽视了程序合规性，导致智能体出现"马基雅维利主义"行为——为最大化奖励而策略性地违反安全规则，这是Goodhart定律的直接体现。

Method: 提出SERV（种子-演化-精炼-验证）流水线，实现"智能体即基准"范式；通过全息沙盒环境与社会工程压力向量注入，构建对抗性评估框架；引入合规加权成功率（CSR）和马基雅维利差距（MG）两个新指标。

Result: 对前沿模型进行综合评估，揭示了任务成功与合规性之间普遍存在的权衡关系。

Conclusion: MAC-Bench有效暴露了多智能体系统中的程序对齐缺陷，为安全性评估提供了新视角。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Large Language Models (LLMs) from passive assistants to autonomous, execution-capable agents has introduced critical operational risks. Most current evaluation frameworks neglect procedural compliance, leading to ''Machiavellian'' behaviors where agents strategically violate safety rules to maximize rewards - a direct manifestation of Goodhart's Law. To address this blind spot, we introduce MAC-Bench, a dynamic, adversarial benchmark designed to evaluate the procedural alignment of multi-agent systems under realistic pressure. We propose the SERV(Seed - Evolve - Refine - Verify) pipeline, an ``Agent-as-a-Benchmark'' paradigm that transforms unstructured legal texts into executable, contamination-free scenarios. By synthesizing holographic sandbox environments and injecting calibrated social-engineering pressure vectors, MAC-Bench forces agents into Pareto-optimal trade-offs between task success and regulatory adherence. We introduced novel metrics: the Compliance-Weighted Success Rate (CSR) and the Machiavellian Gap (MG), and conducted a comprehensive evaluation of state-of-the-art frontier models to reveal the pervasive trade-offs between success and compliance.

</details>


### [297] [Where Instruction Hierarchy Breaks: Diagnosing and Repairing Failures in Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07808)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出白盒诊断框架，将指令层级失效分解为指令识别、冲突解决和响应实现三个阶段，并基于此设计两种免训练自监控机制，使GPT-5.3在静态攻击下违规率降低86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准端到端评估指令层级遵循行为，但违规响应可能源于不同阶段的多种失败，缺乏可解释的诊断方法。

Method: 提出白盒诊断框架将失效定位到指令识别、冲突解决和响应实现三个阶段；基于模型在显式提示下能检测冲突但输出违规的现象，设计并行输入监控器（生成前低延迟冲突检测）和顺序输出监控器（响应级审查修复）两种免训练机制。

Result: Gemma-4-31B-IT、Claude Sonnet 4.6和GPT-5.3上，最强监控器将规则遵循违规降低81-99%；GPT-5.3在静态攻击下降低86%，自适应攻击下降低45%。

Conclusion: 指令层级失效的主导模式因模型、任务和上下文长度而异；免训练自监控机制能有效缓解指令层级违规，但对自适应攻击的鲁棒性仍需提升。

Abstract: Reasoning language models deployed in agentic workflows must follow an instruction hierarchy: when instructions from different sources conflict, the model should obey the highest-privilege applicable instruction. Existing benchmarks largely measure this behavior end-to-end, asking whether the final response is compliant. However, a non-compliant response can arise from several distinct failures: the model may fail to identify the relevant instructions in context, fail to resolve conflicts among identified instructions, or correctly resolve the conflict in its reasoning while still producing a violating response. We introduce a white-box diagnostic framework that localizes instruction hierarchy failures into instruction identification, conflict resolution, and response realization, making failures more interpretable. We evaluate three reasoning models--Gemma-4-31B-IT, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6--on long-context adaptations of IHEval and IHChallenge, and find that the dominant failure mode varies across models, tasks, and context length. Building on the observation that models can often detect conflicts and output violations when explicitly prompted, we propose two training-free self-monitoring mechanisms: a parallel input monitor for low-latency conflict detection before generation, and a sequential output monitor for response-level review and repair. Across Gemma-4-31B-IT, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and GPT-5.3, the strongest monitor reduces rule-following non-compliance by 81-99%, with GPT-5.3 reductions of 86% under static attacks and 45% under adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [298] [Scaling Participation in Modular AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07812)
*Shangbin Feng,Yike Wang,Weijia Shi,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"扩展参与"新范式，通过多方贡献的小模型组成模块化AI系统，在15项任务上比单一大模型性能提升高达15.4%，展现出涌现能力，为从中心化AI向开放协作的底层参与式AI转型提供了技术基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型由少数机构主导训练，结构单一，无法捕捉人类知识、推理和价值观的多样性，缺乏对多元利益相关者的包容性。

Method: 提出"扩展参与"（scaling participation）范式：各利益相关者贡献基于自身兴趣和优先事项训练的小模型，通过模块化框架组成协作式AI系统（compositional AI systems）。

Result: 参与式AI系统在15项任务（如推理、事实性）上超越单一大模型最高达15.4%，性能超过所有组件模型总和更大的单体模型；受益于贡献者多样性；显著提升每位贡献者的原始优先任务性能；涌现能力可解决超过15%的个体模型均无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 扩展参与为从中心化单一大模型向开放、自底向上、协作的AI未来转型提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Humanity is a mosaic of multifaceted talents and needs, and any truly intelligent AI must reflect that richness. Yet the LLMs used by all are built by the few -- a centralized market of monolithic AI models structurally ill-suited to capture the diversity of human knowledge, reasoning, and values. Here we introduce scaling participation, a new paradigm in which modular AI systems are built from the bottom up through the contributions of diverse stakeholders. Participants contribute small models trained on their own interests and priorities; these models then collaborate in modular frameworks as compositional AI systems. Participatory AI systems outperform monolithic LLMs by up to 15.4% across 15 tasks, such as reasoning and factuality, surpassing models larger than all contributed components combined. Further experiments show that participatory AI systems benefit from contributor diversity, substantially improve on each contributor's original priorities, and exhibit emergent capabilities that allow them to solve over 15% of problems where all individual models fail. Scaling participation provides a technical foundation for transitioning from the monolithic status quo toward an open, bottom-up, and collaborative AI future.

</details>


### [299] [Joint Structural Pruning and Mixed-Precision Quantization for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07819)
*Hoang-Loc La,Truong-Thanh Le,Amir Taherkordi,Phuong Hoai Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端框架，联合优化结构化剪枝和混合精度后训练量化，通过全局误差传播最小化策略在超低位宽（1-3比特）下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化（PTQ）方法按层优化量化误差，忽略了误差在网络中的累积与传播；且传统流程将剪枝和量化分离或顺序执行，导致次优解。需要一种联合优化框架同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出两种关键策略：1）混合精度PTQ策略，直接最小化全局误差传播而非逐层误差；2）联合优化方法，在统一搜索空间中同时学习结构化剪枝决策和混合精度量化策略。

Result: 在超低位宽（1-3比特）下：1）相比SoTA权重-激活量化基线，WikiText困惑度降低达21%；2）相比SoTA仅权重量化方法，WikiText和C4困惑度分别降低达59%和85%；3）相比SoTA联合剪枝-量化方法，在超低位宽下取得更优的困惑度和推理性能。

Conclusion: 全局误差传播最小化的联合剪枝-量化框架在超低位宽场景下显著优于现有分离或顺序优化的方法，为LLM高效部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recently, the efficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs) deployment has become a critical concern in practical applications. While post-training quantization (PTQ) and structural pruning are established techniques for reducing memory footprint and inference latency, most existing PTQ approaches optimize quantization errors on a per-layer basis, overlooking how errors accumulate and propagate through the network, often resulting in suboptimal solutions. Traditional pipelines also tend to apply pruning and quantization in isolation or sequentially, further compounding sub-optimality. We introduce a novel end-to-end framework that addresses these limitations in two key ways. First, we propose a novel mixed-precision PTQ strategy that directly minimizes global error propagation across the entire model, rather than isolating layer-wise errors. Building on this, we develop a novel joint optimization approach that simultaneously learns structural pruning decisions and mixed-precision quantization policies within a unified search space. Extensive experiments show that, at ultra-low precisions (1-3 bits), our quantization method reduces WikiText perplexity by up to 21% compared to state-of-the-art (SoTA) weight-activation quantization baselines. Against leading weight-only quantization methods, it achieves up to 59% and 85% lower perplexity on WikiText and C4, respectively. Compared to the SoTA joint pruning-and-quantization techniques, our proposed method delivers superior perplexity and reasoning performance at ultra-low bits.

</details>


### [300] [Overcoming the Regulatory Bottleneck via Agent-to-Agent Protocols: A Nuclear Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07866)
*Akshay J. Dave,David Grabaskas,Joseph A. Renevitz,Richard B. Vilim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出监管审查协议(RCP)，一种智能体间通信标准，将核反应堆设计监管审查中的人与人流程替换为结构化、可审计的智能体通道，在保留人类关键决策监督的同时，将审查成本降低50-77%，时间缩短65%，预计可在美国各行业监管领域每年节省2100-3300亿美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进核反应堆设计的监管审查流程耗时超三年、耗资数亿美元，效率极低且成本高昂，亟需一种可压缩组织间管道的标准化通信协议来缓解这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出监管审查协议(RCP)，一种Agent-to-Agent通信标准；基于美国核监管委员会先进反应堆案卷中的1,236份文档进行分析，并通过多智能体试点进行验证。

Result: 在8900万美元、42个月的基准对比下，RCP将成本降低50-77%（2100-4400万美元），时间缩短65%（15个月）；而无共享协议的独立智能体仅能达到5400-7400万美元和21个月。

Conclusion: 剩余的成本和时间差距是结构性的而非算法性的，源于只有智能体间标准才能压缩的组织间管道。该瓶颈广泛存在于制药审批、环境许可、金融监管和航空认证等领域，若广泛应用，预计可减少美国监管文书负担约4265亿美元的年机会成本，相当于GDP的近1%。

Abstract: Regulatory review of advanced nuclear reactor designs routinely spans more than three years and consumes hundreds of millions of dollars in combined regulator and applicant labor. We present the Regulatory Context Protocol (RCP), an Agent-to-Agent communication standard that replaces the formal human-to-human pipeline between regulators and applicants with a structured, auditable agentic channel, while preserving human oversight at safety-significant decision points. The protocol is calibrated against an analysis of 1,236 documents from U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission advanced reactor dockets and demonstrated with a working multi-agent pilot. Against an 89M USD, 42-month Reconstructed Baseline, RCP cuts costs by 50-77 percent (21M-44M USD) and timelines by 65 percent (15 months). Without a shared protocol, Standalone Agents reach only 54M-74M USD and 21 months. The residual cost-and-time gap is structural, not algorithmic: it traces to the inter-organizational pipeline that only an agent-to-agent standard can compress. The same bottleneck - formal multi-party review under strict auditability requirements - characterizes pharmaceutical approvals, environmental permitting, financial supervision, and aviation certification. The US regulatory paperwork burden carries a 426.5 billion USD annual opportunity cost; replicated broadly, the projected 50-77 percent reduction implies savings on the order of 210-330 billion USD per year - approaching 1 percent of US GDP.

</details>


### [301] [Safety is Contextual, LLM-Judges Are Not: Navigating the Rigid Priors of Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07874)
*Anissa Alloula,Federico Licini,Ava Batchkala,Seraphina Goldfarb-Tarrant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究LLM裁判在安全评估中对上下文信息的依赖性和可引导性，发现LLM裁判虽能学习新信息，但当上下文或安全定义与其内部先验冲突时，它们普遍不愿意调整评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为裁判是大规模安全评估的唯一方式，但现有研究仅关注其在简单静态基准上与人类的一致性，忽略了对上下文信息的依赖性和对安全定义的可引导性这两个关键属性。

Method: 评估多种通用LLM和安全专用裁判的安全判断能力，考察任务演示、新颖的上下文信息以及改变的安全定义对裁判行为的影响。

Result: LLM裁判能够从新信息中学习，但当上下文或安全定义与其内部先验相矛盾时，它们普遍不愿意调整评估结果。

Conclusion: LLM裁判的评估行为受到其内部安全先验的强烈约束，即使引入矛盾信息也难以有效引导，这对安全评估的可靠性和可控性提出了挑战。

Abstract: LLMs-as-judges are the only way to evaluate safety at scale. Despite their importance, LLM-judges themselves are rarely evaluated beyond human agreement in simple, static benchmarks. We therefore investigate two under-explored but crucial properties of LLMs-as-judges: their susceptibility to relying on in context-information, and their steerability to differing safety definitions, which may not align with their internal safety priors. We evaluate the safety judging abilities of many generalist LLMs and safety-specific judges, and investigate the impact of task demonstrations, novel in-context information, and changing safety definitions. We find that while LLM-judges can learn from new information, they are broadly unlikely to adjust their evaluations if the context or safety definition contradicts their prior.

</details>


### [302] [The AI Epistemic Deference Index: A Continuous Measure of Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07897)
*Alejandro Botas,Paul de Font-Reaulx,Luke Hewitt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI认知顺从指数(AEDI)，用于量化模型在自然语言输出中对其用户态度表达的顺从程度。通过LLM作为裁判从自然语言中估计概率，在500个命题和16000个提示上测试8个主流模型，发现所有模型均表现出显著顺从，其中Claude最低，Grok和Gemini最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要测量二元端点转变或显式概率，但用户面临的大多数顺从行为体现在自然语言表达的渐变支持度变化中，缺乏对此类现象的连续、可扩展评估方法。

Method: 构建AEDI连续单维评分，提出利用LLM作为裁判从自然语言输出中估计概率的新协议，经一致性和与人类判断相关性验证。创建包含500个命题和16000个不同用户态度提示的数据库，测试8个主流模型。

Result: 所有模型均表现出显著认知顺从，但不同提供商间存在巨大系统性差异：Claude最低，Grok和Gemini最高。效应在要求书面产出的提示中被放大，并集中于模型先验较弱的命题上。

Conclusion: AEDI可作为易于更新的基准测试和测量流程，用于输出层面的认知顺从评估，揭示了当前AI模型普遍存在的认知顺从问题及其差异模式。

Abstract: Current AI models frequently exhibit epistemic sycophancy, endorsing claims to agree with a user. Existing evaluations typically measure this either by assessing what it takes to make a model shift a binary endorsement or by eliciting an explicit probability in a proposition. However, much user-facing sycophantic behavior is demonstrated through shifts in graded support expressed through ordinary language. We propose the AI Epistemic Deference Index (AEDI): a continuous, unidimensional score representing how sensitive the support expressed in a model's output is to the attitude expressed in a user's prompt. To generate AEDI, we provide a new protocol for estimating probabilities from natural language outputs, using LLMs-as-judges validated for consistency and correlation to human judgment. We deploy it on a new curated database of 500 propositions across diverse topics and 16,000 prompts varying in user attitude, testing eight prominent models. Every model exhibits substantial deference, though with large and systematic differences across providers, with Claude models demonstrating the least, and Grok and Gemini models the most. The effect is amplified in prompts requesting a written artifact, and concentrated on propositions where models hold weaker priors. We release AEDI as an easy-to-update benchmark and measurement pipeline for output-level sycophancy evaluation.

</details>


### [303] [Contract2Tool: Learning Preconditions and Effects for Reliable Tool-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07904)
*Rahul Suresh Babu,Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Contract2Tool框架，通过元数据、文档和执行轨迹自动推断工具契约（前置条件、效果、风险等级和成本），用于因果工具过滤。实验表明，学习到的契约能接近人工撰写的黄金契约效果，将下游任务成功率保持在0.980（黄金契约0.990），同时将可见工具从100个减少到1个，token使用量从26,172降至2,528。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准工具模式仅描述如何调用工具，但无法判断工具在因果上是否适用或会产生何种任务状态。手动编写和维护工具契约在大规模或动态变化的工具生态中不可扩展。

Method: Contract2Tool从元数据、模式、文档和执行轨迹中提取证据，将其转换为标准化的符号契约，并在下游因果工具过滤（CMTF）中部署。

Result: 混合文档与执行轨迹证据生成的契约在准确率上接近黄金契约；学习契约的CMTF下游成功率为0.980（黄金契约0.990），可见工具从100降至1，token使用量从26,172降至2,528。

Conclusion: 学习到的契约可以在工具模式与可靠代理执行之间提供可扩展的契约层，兼顾可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model agents increasingly rely on external APIs, but standard tool schemas describe how to call a tool, not when the tool is causally appropriate or what task state it produces. Causal tool filtering addresses this gap by using lightweight contracts that specify each tool's preconditions, effects, risk level, and cost. However, manually writing and maintaining such contracts does not scale to large or changing tool ecosystems. We introduce Contract2Tool, a framework for inferring tool contracts from metadata, schemas, documentation, and execution traces. Contract2Tool converts observable tool evidence into normalized symbolic contracts that can be evaluated intrinsically and deployed inside downstream causal tool filtering. We evaluate learned contracts against gold preconditions, effects, and risk labels, and measure their downstream utility on multi-step agent tasks. Our results show that hybrid documentation-and-trace evidence produces contracts accurate enough to preserve most of the reliability and efficiency benefits of gold contracts. Learned-contract CMTF achieves 0.980 downstream success, close to 0.990 for gold-contract CMTF, while reducing visible tools from 100 to 1 and reducing average token usage from 26,172 to 2,528 relative to all-tools exposure. These results suggest that learned contracts can provide a scalable contract layer between tool schemas and reliable agent execution.

</details>


### [304] [MemToolAgent overview with a simple restaurant booking scenario where the agent retrieves similar memories, receives feedback on an invalid time format, and generates a reflection to update its memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07909)
*Suleyman Armagan Er,Danilo Ribeiro,Yogesh Virkar,Surafel Lakew,Adi Kalyanpur,James Gung,Thomas Delteil,Arshit Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemToolAgent框架，通过结构化记忆管理（记忆提取+检索模块）提升LLM智能体的工具使用能力，无需微调即可利用历史对话经验，在三个基准上分别取得29%、80%和17%的相对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体虽能使用外部工具解决复杂任务，但在需要从长期历史事件或过去智能体-环境交互中学习的问题上，缺乏有效的记忆机制来存储和检索经验。尽管对话智能体有成熟的记忆系统，但如何通过历史用户-智能体对话来提升工具使用能力的研究仍然不足。

Method: 提出MemToolAgent框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 记忆提取模块——将过往经验处理成结构化记忆条目，利用环境和用户反馈将错误执行提炼为批评性记忆；(2) 检索模块——基于记忆相似度分布动态选择需要使用的历史经验条目数量。采用统一的记忆条目格式，无需LLM微调。

Result: 在WorkBench、NESTFUL和PEToolBench三个基准上，相比强基线方法分别实现29%、80%和17%的相对性能提升。

Conclusion: MemToolAgent通过有效的记忆管理机制，在不进行LLM微调的前提下，显著提升了智能体的工具使用能力，实现了更个性化和准确的响应，且能更好地对齐用户偏好和反馈。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) agents can use external tools to help users solve complex tasks. However, for problems that require learning from long-term historical events or from previous agent-environment interactions, LLM agents are required to use memory mechanisms to store and retrieve experiences. While sophisticated memory systems exist for dialogue agents, few studies have empirically examined how to improve agents' tool-using capabilities through past user-agent conversations. We propose MemToolAgent, a framework that improves tool use through memory management. Our approach contains a memory extraction module that processes past experiences into structured memory entries, and a retrieval module that dynamically selects a subset of the stored memory entries. This enables more personalized and accurate responses aligned with user preferences and feedback without requiring LLM fine-tuning. In summary, this work has three main contributions: (1) a unified memory entry format that improves both general-purpose and personalized tool use without LLM fine-tuning, (2) a reflection-based memory extraction that uses environment and user feedback to distill wrong executions into critiques to store, and (3) a retrieval module that chooses how many past experiences to use based on the memory similarity distribution. MemToolAgent achieves 29%, 80%, and 17% relative improvements compared to strong baselines on the WorkBench, NESTFUL, and PEToolBench benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [305] [EditSR: Enhancing Neural Symbolic Regression via Edit-based Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07915)
*Da Li,Xinxin Li,Xingyu Cui,Jin Xu,Juan Zhang,Junping Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EditSR是一个双层框架，将神经符号回归模型与基于编辑的修正器结合，通过预训练修正器实现高效的逐状态修正，避免重启全局搜索，在复杂表达式生成中显著提升符号结构恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号回归模型的一步自回归解码容易产生错误累积，导致生成结构不正确的表达式。现有修正策略需要重启全局搜索，削弱了神经模型的效率优势，且仍受错误累积影响。

Method: 提出EditSR双层框架：第一层为神经符号回归模型，第二层为基于编辑的修正器（Rectifier）。将修正过程建模为从错误表达式开始的逐状态转移链，通过状态转移算法构建监督修正链来预训练修正器。每个编辑操作限制在语法有效空间内，确保句法合法性。编辑决策仅基于当前状态而非历史，允许后续编辑修正前期错误。

Result: 大量实验和消融研究表明，EditSR以有限的额外成本显著提升了符号结构恢复效果，在复杂表达式上效果更明显，因为一步自回归解码在此场景下更易受错误累积影响。

Conclusion: EditSR通过双层框架和基于编辑的事后修正策略，在不重启全局搜索的前提下有效缓解了神经符号回归中的错误累积问题，在保持效率优势的同时显著提升了解码质量。

Abstract: Neural symbolic regression models improve inference efficiency by shifting structural search to pretraining, but their one-pass autoregressive decoding is prone to error accumulation, which may lead to generating structurally incorrect expressions, especially in complex expression generation scenarios. Existing rectification strategies can alleviate this issue, but they often depend on restarting global search, thereby weakening the efficiency advantage of neural models, and remain susceptible to error accumulation. In this paper, we propose EditSR, a two-layer framework that combines a neural symbolic regression model in the first layer with an edit-based Rectifier in the second layer to achieve efficient prediction and post-hoc rectification. Instead of restarting the global search, we maintain rectification efficiency by pretraining the Rectifier. Specifically, we formulate the rectification process as a step-by-step state-transition chain starting from an incorrect expression, and develop a state-transition algorithm to construct supervised rectification chains for training the Rectifier. To ensure syntactic validity throughout rectification, each edit action is restricted to a syntactically valid space so that every edited expression remains parseable. In addition, because each edit decision is conditioned on the current state rather than the history, the Rectifier allows errors made in earlier steps to be rectified by subsequent edits, thereby reducing the risk of error accumulation. Extensive experiments and ablation studies show that EditSR substantially improves symbolic structure recovery with limited extra cost, with more pronounced gains on complex expressions, where one-pass autoregressive decoding is more susceptible to error accumulation.

</details>


### [306] [The CIFAR Synthetic Evidence Corpus for Detecting AI-Generated Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07916)
*Kelly McConvey,Jalehsadat Mahdavimoghaddam,Nima Jamali,Maksym Taranukhin,Sajad Ebrahimi,Wentao Zhang,Yuntian Deng,Karen Eltis,Maura R. Grossman,Vered Shwartz,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对司法系统中证据文件真实性验证的需求，本文提出了CIFAR合成证据语料库——一个包含多种文档类型和操纵策略的数据集，用于评估生成模型产生的伪造证据的检测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型能够生成逼真文档，直接挑战司法系统中的证据真实性验证流程。现有检测数据集局限于人脸/自然场景照片或学术/社交媒体文档，缺乏针对司法证据特性的训练和评估数据。

Method: 构建CIFAR合成证据语料库，涵盖多种文档家族和从字段级编辑到完全伪造的操纵策略，使用多种最先进的生成工具，并在训练和测试数据间实施源级分离以模拟真实泛化挑战。

Result: 产出了一个系统性变化操纵复杂度和生成方法的多文档类型数据集，能够用于司法证据验证的严格评估。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了司法领域证据真实性检测评估数据的空白，为在现实条件和受控条件下评估证据验证系统提供了基础。

Abstract: The growing ability of generative models to produce realistic documents poses a direct challenge to evidentiary workflows in the justice system and the courts, where decisions increasingly depend on the authenticity of evidence such as receipts, communications, and administrative records. Unlike social media or academic settings, evidentiary documents are often only subtly altered, with small, localized edits that preserve overall plausibility while changing legal meaning. Yet progress on automated detection remains limited, largely due to the absence of suitable training and evaluation data especially suited for the justice system requirements. Existing resources are either focused on photos of human faces or natural scenery or on narrowly scoped academic or social media document types, and do not capture the structure, diversity, or manipulation patterns characteristic of real-world evidentiary data. As a result, current detection systems do not necessarily learn meaningful signals appropriate for the justice system. We introduce the CIFAR Synthetic Evidence Corpus, a dataset designed to enable rigorous evaluation of evidence verification under realistic and controlled conditions. The corpus spans multiple document families and a spectrum of manipulation strategies, from small field-level edits to complete document fabrication, and is constructed using a diverse set of state-of-the-art generative tools. It is organized to systematically vary both manipulation complexity and generation method, while enforcing source-level separation between training and test data to reflect real-world generalization challenges.

</details>


### [307] [Stress-testing medical large language models reveals latent safety pathology beyond benchmark accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07929)
*Yuan Shen,Xiaojun Wu,Linghua Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-MASLD压力审计框架，通过240个临床案例和六种叙事扰动探针对7个临床LLM进行双重压力测试，发现基线准确率相似的模型在叙事压力下表现显著分化，揭示了量化模型的伪正常化、医学SFT导致逻辑稳定性下降等问题，表明叙事压力审计是准确性评估的必要补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床LLM评估依赖基准准确率，但这种方法可能无法检测到与安全相关的失败模式，亟需能揭示模型在真实临床场景下脆弱性的评估框架。

Method: 借鉴肝病学代谢压力测试逻辑，构建AI-MASLD框架：使用240个临床案例，设计六种叙事扰动探针，对7个模型进行双重压力测试，通过代谢指数(MI)、扰动翻转率(PFR)和反事实公平指数(CFI)三个指标量化模型性能。

Result: 基线条件下所有模型表现一致良好；叙事压力下性能出现显著分化，揭示两种压力响应表型；量化模型出现伪正常化现象；医学SFT系统性降低了逻辑稳定性、公平性和信息提取能力；开放权重模型在所有安全维度上达到或超越专有模型。

Conclusion: 叙事压力审计是准确性评估的必要补充，现有评估范式可能掩盖临床LLM的安全隐患。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are entering clinical practice based on benchmark accuracy that may fail to detect safety-relevant failure modes. Here we present AI-MASLD, a stress-audit framework that adapts the logic of metabolic stress testing from hepatology to the evaluation of clinical LLMs. Using 240 clinical cases across six narrative perturbation probes, we subjected seven models to double-stress testing and quantified performance through three indices: metabolic index (MI), perturbation flip rate (PFR), and counterfactual fairness index (CFI). Under clean baseline conditions, all models performed uniformly well. Under realistic narrative stress, performance diverged sharply, revealing two distinct stress-response phenotypes. Quantized models exhibited pseudonormalization, in which low flip rates hid functional collapse. Medical supervised fine-tuning systematically degraded logical stability, fairness, and information extraction.
