An open-weight model matched or exceeded proprietary alternatives on every safety dimension. These findings establish narrative stress auditing as a necessary complement to accuracy-based evaluation.

</details>


### [308] [Unification of Closed-Open Industrial Detection Scenarios: New Large-Scale Benchmarks,Challenges and Baselines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07953)
*Zekai Zhang,Jinglin Zhang,Qinghui Chen,Gang Li,Da Chen,Shuainan Jing,He Wang,Dagang Li,Cong Liu,Cong Bai,Shengyong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了大规模多模态工业开闭集基准MMIOC-1M（含100万+样本，覆盖14超类、29工业场景、351缺陷子类），并设计了精细化文本-视觉提示网络RTVPNet，通过专家辅助域投影、能量基稀疏采样和双向文本-视觉交互模块，在工业缺陷检测任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM在工业缺陷检测中面临两大瓶颈：缺乏覆盖多领域多类别的大规模工业数据集，以及依赖人工提示（点、框、掩码）引入主观噪声且缺乏文本-视觉细粒度交互。

Method: (1) 构建MMIOC-1M基准，同时支持开放词汇和闭集工业检测； (2) 提出RTVPNet，包含专家辅助域投影机制（通用视觉模型快速适应工业域）、能量基稀疏采样策略（自动生成精细化视觉提示）、双向文本-视觉交互模块（增强跨模态语义对齐）。

Result: RTVPNet在MMIOC-1M、LVIS和COCO基准上均达到SOTA性能，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: 该工作为LVLM在工业缺陷检测中的大规模应用提供了统一基准和高效方法，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale Visual-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable success in natural visual tasks, yet their application to industrial defect detection remains challenging due to two fundamental limitations: (i) the scarcity of large-scale industrial datasets that cover diverse defect categories across multiple domains, and (ii) the reliance on manual prompts (points, boxes, masks) that introduce subjective noise and lack text-visual interaction for fine-grained understanding. To address these challenges, we introduce a Large-Scale Multi-Modal Industrial Open-Closed benchmark (MMIOC-1M) containing over one million samples across $14$ super-categories, $29$ industrial scenes, and $351$ defect subcategories. To our knowledge, MMIOC-1M is the first unified largest benchmark supporting both open-vocabulary and closed-set industrial detection, providing valuable pre-training data for LVLMs in industrial scenarios. Furthermore, we propose a Refined Text-Visual Prompt Network (RTVPNet) that incorporates three key innovations: (1) an expert-assisted domain projection mechanism that enables rapid adaptation of general vision models to industrial domains, (2) an energy-based sparse sampling strategy that automatically generates refined visual prompts without manual intervention, and (3) a bidirectional text-visual interaction module that enhances cross-modal semantic alignment and understanding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RTVPNet achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMIOC-1M, LVIS, and COCO benchmarks while maintaining computational efficiency. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hellozzk/MMIO.

</details>


### [309] [Shared Latent Structures Enable Unified Backdoor Detection and Mitigation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07963)
*Omar Mahmoud,Aly M. Kassem,Thommen George Karimpanal,Buddhika Laknath Semage,Negar Rostamzadeh,Golnoosh Farnadi,Santu Rana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型中的后门攻击共享可检测、可因果控制、可抑制的潜在机制，并提出了基于稀疏自编码器的统一检测与缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门防御通常针对特定触发器或行为，忽略了不同后门之间可能存在的共享潜在机制，这种碎片化视角限制了防御的通用性。

Method: 1) 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）在残差流激活中识别跨后门行为共享的潜在特征；2) 通过双向激活操控验证特征因果性；3) 训练轻量SAE特征分类器实现零样本泛化检测；4) 提出概念消融微调（CAFT）抑制后门形成。

Result: 共享特征在多种后门行为、多个模型系列（Qwen3, Gemma3, Llama3.1, 4B-32B）及多种攻击方式（微调、权重编辑）中泛化；SAE特征分类器零样本泛化优于残差流和权重差基线；CAFT有效抑制后门形成。

Conclusion: 许多后门攻击依赖于可迁移的共享潜在机制，这为后门的统一检测与缓解提供了新的理论依据和实用方法。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks in large language models (LLMs) are often treated as isolated trigger-response failures, motivating defenses tailored to specific triggers or behaviors. We show this view is incomplete. Across diverse backdoor behaviors, we identify a shared latent mechanism that can be detected, causally controlled, and suppressed. Using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) on residual-stream activations, we find a small set of latent features consistently activated across jailbreaking, refusal manipulation, password-locking, bias induction, sentiment misclassification, and country-conditioned harmful advice. These features generalize across Qwen3, Gemma~3, and Llama~3.1 models from 4B to 32B parameters, and across both fine-tuning and weight-editing attacks. Through bidirectional activation steering, we show these features are causal: suppressing them reduces attack success, while amplifying them induces target behaviors on clean prompts. We further train lightweight SAE-feature classifiers that generalize zero-shot to unseen backdoors and outperform residual-stream and weight-diffing baselines. Finally, we introduce Concept Ablation Fine-Tuning (CAFT), which suppresses backdoor formation by ablating the shared latent subspace during training. Together, our results suggest that many backdoors rely on a transferable latent mechanism, enabling unified detection and mitigation.

</details>


### [310] [Zero-Shot Learning in Industrial Scenarios: New Large-Scale Benchmark, Challenges and Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07965)
*Zekai Zhang,Qinghui Chen,Maomao Xiong,Shijiao Ding,Zhanzhi Su,Xinjie Yao,Yiming Sun,Cong Bai,Jinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了大规模多模态工业开放数据集MMIO（包含8万+样本、6个超类、18个子类）和面向零样本工业缺陷检测的精炼文本-视觉提示方法RTVP，通过专家引导的大模型领域自适应机制和自动生成视觉提示并考虑文本-视觉交互，在零样本和闭集场景下分别达到42.2%和24.7%的AP，实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景与自然场景之间存在显著差异，使得现有LVLM在工业场景中应用困难；现有LVLM依赖用户提供的提示词分割目标，容易引入无关像素导致性能不佳；工业场景数据稀缺，LVLM在工业场景中的应用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建MMIO数据集（80K+样本，6个超类，18个子类，首个大规模多场景工业零样本预训练数据集）；提出RTVP方法，包含专家引导的大模型领域自适应机制和基于Mobile-SAM的工业零样本方法，以及自动生成视觉提示并考虑文本-视觉提示交互的机制。

Result: 在MMIO数据集上，零样本场景AP达到42.2%，闭集场景AP达到24.7%，均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: RTVP通过领域自适应和文本-视觉提示交互有效提升了LVLM在工业场景的泛化能力和检测性能，MMIO数据集为未来工业场景的开放模型提供了有价值的训练数据。

Abstract: Large Visual Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable success in vision tasks. However, the significant differences between industrial and natural scenes make applying LVLMs challenging. Existing LVLMs rely on user-provided prompts to segment objects. This often leads to suboptimal performance due to the inclusion of irrelevant pixels. In addition, the scarcity of data also makes the application of LVLMs in industrial scenarios remain unexplored. To fill this gap, this paper proposes an open industrial dataset and a Refined Text-Visual Prompt (RTVP) for zero-shot industrial defect detection. First, this paper constructs the Multi-Modal Industrial Open Dataset (MMIO) containing 80K+ samples. MMIO contains diverse industrial categories, including 6 super categories and 18 subcategories. MMIO is the first large-scale multi-scenes pre-training dataset for industrial zero-shot learning, and provides valuable training data for open models in future industrial scenarios. Based on MMIO, this paper provides a RTVP specifically for industrial zero-shot tasks. RTVP has two significant advantages: First, this paper designs an expert-guided large model domain adaptation mechanism and designs an industrial zero-shot method based on Mobile-SAM, which enhances the generalization ability of large models in industrial scenarios. Second, RTVP automatically generates visual prompts directly from images and considers text-visual prompt interactions ignored by previous LVLM, improving visual and textual content understanding. RTVP achieves SOTA with 42.2% and 24.7% AP in zero-shot and closed scenes of MMIO.

</details>


### [311] [PAFO: Pareto Fairness Optimization for Personalized Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07988)
*Xiaoyan Zhao,Haoting Ni,Yang Zhang,Chunyuan Zheng,Haoxuan Li,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAFO框架，通过Pareto公平性优化解决个性化奖励模型中的偏好偏差问题，在不牺牲多数群体性能的前提下提升少数群体用户的奖励建模质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化奖励模型在训练时面临用户偏好数据不平衡问题，导致模型倾向于偏好支持率更高的用户群体，而对少数群体用户的服务质量系统性下降。现有方法缺乏对此类个性化奖励偏差的识别与缓解。

Method: PAFO框架首先分别为多数和少数偏好群体训练群体专用奖励模型，然后通过构建条件边际级监督信号，将异质偏好边界蒸馏到一个统一模型中。训练时使用群体信息，推理时无需群体标签。

Result: 在Personal-LLM和DSP数据集上，PAFO同时提升了少数群体和多数群体的奖励建模准确率，并在多个公平性指标上降低了用户级不公平性。

Conclusion: PAFO通过Pareto公平性优化有效缓解了个性化奖励模型的偏好偏差问题，实现了更公平的LLM个性化对齐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on reward models to align their outputs with diverse user preferences. While personalized reward models aim to capture such heterogeneity, they are often trained on imbalanced user preference data and may therefore favor users whose preferences are more common in the training population. In this paper, we identify this failure mode as personalized reward bias, where reward modeling quality varies systematically with preference support rate. We formulate its mitigation as a Pareto fairness problem over group utilities, aiming to improve under-served users without degrading other user groups. To this end, we propose PAFO, a Pareto fairness optimization framework for personalized reward modeling. PAFO first trains group-specialized reward models for majority and minority preference groups, then constructs conditional margin-level supervision to distill their heterogeneous preference boundaries into a single unified model. The resulting model uses group information only during training and requires no explicit group labels at inference time. Experiments on Personal-LLM and DSP show that PAFO improves both minority-group and majority-group accuracy while reducing user-level unfairness across multiple metrics, demonstrating its effectiveness for fairer LLM personalization.

</details>


### [312] [VATS: Exploiting Implicit Authority in Error-Path Injection via Systematic Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07992)
*Harshil Patel,Kunal Pai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCP协议中错误处理循环存在安全漏洞，通过VATS框架对错误路径进行注入攻击，可使间接提示注入成功率提升三倍，最高达100%合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP标准在规范工具调用时引入了未经验证的安全攻击面——错误处理循环，工具错误信息具有隐式权威性，可触发纠正性推理模式绕过标准安全启发式规则。

Method: 提出VATS（工具流漏洞分析）框架，采用突变驱动方法，沿七个结构和语言维度系统性地演化对抗性载荷，在Gemini 3.1 Pro、GPT-5.5、GLM-5.1、Qwen3-Coder四个前沿模型上评估。

Result: 错误路径注入使标准间接提示注入成功率提升三倍，在受控评估中达到最高100%合规率；发现结构定位（在错误上下文中夹带指令）是跨所有测试模型最有效的利用向量。

Conclusion: 生产框架护栏可缓解这些漏洞，但模型层固有的易感性对定制化智能体工作流构成系统性风险。

Abstract: As the Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes tool-calling for autonomous agents, it introduces a critical, unexamined attack surface: the error-handling loop. We hypothesize that tool error messages possess implicit authority, triggering corrective reasoning modes that bypass standard safety heuristics. We introduce VATS (Vulnerability Analysis of Tool Streams), a mutation-driven framework that systematically evolves adversarial payloads across seven structural and linguistic dimensions. Our evaluation across four frontier models, Gemini 3.1 Pro, GPT-5.5, GLM-5.1, and Qwen3-Coder, demonstrates that error-path injection triples the success rate of standard indirect prompt injection (IPI), achieving up to 100% compliance in controlled evaluations. We isolate structural positioning (sandwiching instructions within error context) as the most effective exploit vector across all tested models. While we find that production framework guardrails can mitigate these vulnerabilities, the inherent susceptibility of the model layer poses a systemic risk to bespoke agentic workflows.

</details>


### [313] [Efficient Skill Grounding via Code Refactoring with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07999)
*Sera Choi,Wonje Choi,Saehun Chun,Daehee Lee,Jooyoung Kim,Chaeun Lee,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECENT提出了一种以重构为中心的智能体框架，通过将技能语义与具体执行绑定解耦，使得小型语言模型（sLM）能够高效实现技能落地（skill grounding），在动态、部分可观测环境中达到甚至超越大型语言模型（LLM）方法的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具身智能体中，技能的落地（skill grounding）对环境和本体差异极为敏感，而大型语言模型（LLM）在动态、部分可观测环境中部署不切实际，小型语言模型（sLM）又不足以支撑可靠的长期控制。因此需要一种不依赖LLM的高效技能落地方法。

Method: 将技能表示为可执行代码，通过局部重构（localized refactoring）仅修改执行绑定（execution binding），保留技能控制结构中的语义意图，从而解耦技能语义与具体本体/环境的执行绑定。

Result: 在多种机器人本体和动态环境的技能落地场景中，RECENT在所有场景下均取得了sLM-based Code-as-Policies方法中的最佳性能，并与LLM-based CaP的任务性能持平。

Conclusion: 通过解耦技能语义与执行绑定，结合局部重构策略，sLM能够实现高效的技能落地，在长期控制任务中达到与LLM方法相当的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: Effective skill grounding is essential for deploying reusable skills in embodied agents, as even minor embodiment or environmental differences can render an entire skill incompatible. This challenge is particularly pronounced in embodied settings, where agents must operate in dynamic, partially observable environments without access to large language models (LLMs). In this setting, reliance on LLMs is impractical, while small language models (sLMs) remain insufficient for the effective skill grounding required for reliable long-horizon control. We present RECENT, a refactoring-centric agent framework that enables efficient skill grounding with sLMs by decoupling skill semantics from embodiment- and environment-specific execution binding. By representing skills as executable code, RECENT preserves the semantic intent encoded in a skill's control structure while grounding it by modifying only execution bindings through localized refactoring, rather than regenerating code from scratch. We evaluate RECENT across diverse skill grounding scenarios spanning multiple robot embodiments in dynamic environments, demonstrating robust long-horizon performance when deployed with an sLM. Across all scenarios, RECENT achieves the best performance among sLM-based Code-as-Policies (CaP) methods and matches the task performance of LLM-based CaP.

</details>


### [314] [UniQL: Towards Dialect-Universal Benchmarking for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08018)
*Jianling Gao,Chongyang Tao,Jiayuan Bai,Liu Yang,Xuanguang Pan,Jinrui Liu,Shihao Xing,Xiaohan Xu,Jie Liang,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniQL是一个包含16种SQL方言、24,544条跨方言查询的人工验证基准，用于评估文本到SQL模型在不同SQL方言间的泛化能力。实验表明当前模型远未达到方言通用，性能在不同数据库系统间存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到SQL基准大多基于SQLite，无法评估模型在不同SQL方言间的泛化能力，而实际数据库系统在语法、函数、类型系统和执行语义上差异巨大，相同自然语言意图需要不同的方言实现。

Method: 采用混合构建流程：数据库迁移、SQL翻译、执行引导验证、迭代规则总结和人工验证，将1,534个自然语言问题对齐到16种SQL方言，生成24,544条方言特定查询，所有方言共享相同意图、对齐的Schema和数据库内容。

Result: 开源和闭源LLM的实验表明，当前模型远未达到方言通用水平，不同数据库系统间性能差异显著，且从SQLite成功到其他方言的迁移能力有限。

Conclusion: 需要对齐的跨方言基准和更具方言感知的文本到SQL方法。

Abstract: Existing text-to-SQL benchmarks are largely centered on SQLite, making it difficult to evaluate whether models can generalize across heterogeneous SQL dialects. However, real-world database systems differ substantially in syntax, functions, type systems, and execution semantics, so the same natural language intent often requires dialect-specific SQL realizations. We introduce UniQL, a human-verified benchmark for cross-dialect text-to-SQL evaluation. UniQL aligns 1,534 natural language questions with executable SQL annotations across 16 SQL dialects, yielding 24,544 dialect-specific queries. All dialects share the same intents, aligned schemas and database contents, enabling controlled evaluation of dialect generalization. UniQL is constructed through a hybrid pipeline combining database migration, SQL translation, execution-guided verification, iterative rule summarization, and human validation. Experiments on both open-source and closed-source LLMs show that current models remain far from dialect-universal, with substantial performance variation across database systems and limited transfer from SQLite success to other dialects. These findings highlight the need for aligned cross-dialect benchmarks and more dialect-aware text-to-SQL methods. Code and data are available at https://github.com/JerryGao818/UniQL

</details>


### [315] [OSMGraphCLIP: Learning Global Location Representations from OpenStreetMap Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08046)
*Dimitrios Michail,Eleni Saka,Ioannis Giannopoulos,Ioannis Papoutsis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSMGraphCLIP利用OpenStreetMap构建异构图表征地理环境，通过对比学习对齐图编码器与球谐位置编码器，在气候、生态、社会经济等多项下游任务中达到甚至超越基于卫星图像的基线模型，尤其在社会经济与公共健康任务上表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间表示学习多依赖卫星图像，但图像数据成本高、间接反映人类活动，而OSM数据免费且富含语义标注，本文旨在探索仅用结构化OSM数据学习强泛化的全局位置表征。

Method: 将OSM特征构建为异构属性图（道路、建筑、土地利用、兴趣点等），设计多尺度图编码器捕获局部与全局结构，通过对比学习目标与球谐函数位置编码器进行对齐训练。

Result: 在气候、生态、社会经济、公共卫生、土地覆盖、生物多样性、野火预测等多领域下游任务中，OSMGraphCLIP匹配或超越基于卫星图像的基线，在社会经济与公共健康任务上优势最显著，生态与环境任务上紧密竞争。

Conclusion: 结构化OSM数据本身足以支撑跨领域的强全局位置表征，模型学习到的嵌入能恢复生物群落边界、城市梯度等地理结构，展示了非图像地理数据在表示学习中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present OSMGraphCLIP, a CLIP-style geospatial representation model that learns global location embeddings from freely available OpenStreetMap (OSM) data. OSMGraphCLIP represents geographic environments as heterogeneous graphs of typed OSM features, preserving the topological and semantic relationships among roads, buildings, land-use regions, and points of interest. A multi-scale graph encoder captures both fine-grained local structure and broader landscape composition, and supervises a spherical-harmonics location encoder through a contrastive alignment objective. We evaluate OSMGraphCLIP across a diverse suite of downstream geospatial regression and classification tasks spanning climate, ecology, socioeconomic indicators, public health, land cover, biodiversity, and wildfire forecasting, and show that structured OSM data alone supports strong global location representations across domains. OSMGraphCLIP matches or exceeds satellite-based baselines on the majority of benchmarks, with the most pronounced advantage on socioeconomic and public-health tasks, where OSM's explicit semantic annotation of the built environment encodes patterns of human activity that satellite pixels can only capture indirectly. On ecological and environmental tasks, the model remains closely competitive with imagery-based methods despite using no Earth observation data. Qualitative analysis confirms that the learned embeddings organize geographic space coherently, recovering biome boundaries, urban gradients, and tropical--temperate distinctions from map topology alone.

</details>


### [316] [SKILL.nb: Selective Formalization and Gated Execution for Durable Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08049)
*Amine El Hattami,Nicolas Chapados,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILL.nb 是一个用于管理可复用智能体工作流的框架，通过选择性形式化（将执行证据转化为代码或自然语言指导）和门控条件执行（在验证通过时运行代码，失败时回退），解决了工作流在环境漂移、任务变化下的生命周期可靠性问题。在 WebArena-Verified 等基准上，它显著提升了单次成功率、跨轮次保持率、失败恢复率，并在 GitLab 迁移测试中展现了良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体将过往经验复用时，面对环境漂移、任务定义不完整、任务分布变化等情况，原本成功的流程可能失效，尤其是在 Web 自动化场景中。现有方法缺乏对可复用工作流全生命周期的可靠性治理机制。

Method: SKILL.nb 采用选择性形式化：基于执行证据决定工作流步骤应转换为可执行代码还是保留为自然语言指导，并定期修订这些选择。工作流存储为可审计、版本化的 notebook，包含自然语言指导、多语言可执行单元、验证门、回退路径及多模态证据（输出、截图、错误追踪）。运行时采用门控条件执行，步骤在验证通过时执行代码，漂移导致实现失效时回退到本地替代方案。

Result: 在 WebArena-Verified 上，单轮成功率 53.7%，比最强基线高 3.9 个百分点；三次重新执行中保留 91.7% 的初始成功任务，比次优方法高 15.5 个百分点；在有界修复下恢复 72.9% 的后续失败，修复后回归率仅 4.2%（基线为 15.0%-17.0%）；在 Mind2Web 跨网站和跨领域分集上也领先；GitLab 迁移测试中，冻结版本与新鲜版本的目标版本差距为 -1.7 和 +0.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 生命周期治理和门控条件执行是超越单次任务成功的重要可靠性维度，SKILL.nb 证明了通过证据校准的策略可以显著提升可复用工作流的鲁棒性和适应性。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly turn past experience into reusable artifacts such as code, workflows, and procedural memories. Reuse can improve efficiency, but it also creates a lifecycle reliability problem: artifacts that succeed once may fail under environment drift, underspecified tasks, or changing task distributions, especially in web automation. We introduce SKILL.nb, a framework for governing reusable agent workflows with evidence-calibrated lifecycle policies. SKILL.nb uses selective formalization: execution evidence decides which workflow steps should become executable code, which should remain natural-language guided, and when those choices should be revised. Workflows are stored as auditable, versioned notebooks that interleave natural-language guidance, multi-language executable cells, validation gates, fallback paths, and multimodal evidence such as outputs, screenshots, and error traces. At runtime, gate-conditioned execution lets each step run code when its gates validate, or fall back locally when drift invalidates the executable realization. On WebArena-Verified, SKILL.nb achieves 53.7% single-round success, improving over the strongest baseline by 3.9 percentage points. Across three re-executions, it retains 91.7% of initially successful tasks, 15.5 points above the next best method. Under bounded repair, it recovers 72.9% of subsequent failures while limiting post-repair regressions to 4.2%, compared with 15.0% to 17.0% for persistent baselines. It also leads on Mind2Web cross-website and cross-domain splits. In a GitLab migration test, SKILL.nb preserves performance when reusing frozen state learned on GitLab 15.7, with frozen-versus-fresh target-version gaps of -1.7 points on GitLab 16.11 and +0.6 points on GitLab 18.9. These results identify lifecycle governance and gate-conditioned execution as reliability axes beyond one-shot task success.

</details>


### [317] [How Small Can You Go? LoRA Fine-Tuning 270M-8B Models for Merchant Information Extraction in Financial Transactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08051)
*Donghao Huang,Tomas Drietomsky,Benjamin Barrett,Zhaoxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了24种模型变体在金融交易商户信息结构化提取任务中的表现，发现Qwen 3.5 4B在参数量减半的情况下达到96.60% F1（仅比8B基线低0.35点），0.8B模型以94.75% F1实现了优异的准确率-延迟权衡，并提供了部署建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中部署8B参数模型的成本过高（内存、延迟、费用），需要寻找更高效的替代方案以在保持高准确率的同时降低部署开销。

Method: 对4个模型家族（Gemma 3、Qwen 3.5、Aya、LLaMA 3.1）的24个变体进行系统评估，使用LoRA微调、JSON提示、思维链微调等方法，并在Databricks Model Serving端点上部署测试。

Result: (1) LoRA rank=8微调LLaMA 3.1-8B达96.75% F1，仅比rank-32低0.20点；(2) Qwen 3.5 4B+JSON提示达96.60% F1，参数量减半；(3) Qwen 3.5 0.8B达94.75% F1，匹配2.5-4倍大模型；(4) CoT微调提升0.3-1.8点F1；(5) Qwen 3.5的Think/Nothink模板几乎无差异；(6) 部署后平均F1变化仅0.8点，Aya 3.35B例外（下降3-5点）。

Conclusion: Qwen 3.5 4B是替代8B模型的最佳高效选择，0.8B模型适用于低延迟场景，提供了基于准确率和延迟需求的部署建议。

Abstract: Financial transaction processing requires extracting structured merchant information from noisy, abbreviated bank transaction strings at scale. Our current production system, a LoRA-fine-tuned LLaMA 3.1-8B, achieves 96.95% F1 on this task, but deploying 8-billion-parameter models imposes prohibitive memory, latency, and cost constraints. To identify more efficient alternatives, we conduct a deployment-focused study of 24 model variants spanning four model families: Gemma 3 (270M, 1B, 4B), Qwen 3.5 (0.8B, 2B, 4B), Aya (3.35B), and LLaMA 3.1-8B, systematically evaluating accuracy, inference throughput, training cost, and hardware behavior to assess production suitability. Our findings show that: (1) reproducing the LLaMA 3.1-8B fine-tune with a LoRA rank of 8 achieves 96.75% F1, only 0.20 points below the rank-32 baseline; (2) Qwen 3.5 4B with JSON-only prompting reaches 96.60% F1, within 0.35 points of the 8B baseline while using roughly half the parameters; (3) the 0.8B Qwen 3.5 model achieves 94.75% F1, matching models 2.5-4x larger and offering an attractive latency-accuracy trade-off; (4) chain-of-thought fine-tuning generally improves F1 by 0.3-1.8 points across most models, although Qwen 3.5 4B performs best with direct JSON-only prompting; and (5) Qwen 3.5 Think and Nothink training templates produce nearly identical results (F1 differences <0.004), indicating that explicit reasoning supervision is unnecessary for structured extraction tasks. We further deploy all 14 fine-tuned sub-8B models as Databricks Model Serving endpoints and observe that benchmark performance transfers reliably to production, with an average F1 change of only 0.8 points. Aya 3.35B, based on the Cohere2 architecture, is the sole exception, exhibiting a 3-5 point decline under serving conditions. Based on these results, we provide deployment recommendations across accuracy and latency requirements, ...

</details>


### [318] [A Multi-modal Agentic Co-pilot for Evidence Grounded Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08093)
*Zhe Xu,Zhengyu Zhang,Zhiyuan Cai,Jiahao Xu,Yijie Lin,Ziyi Liu,Junlin Hou,Hongyi Wang,Yuxiang Nie,Ling Liang,Yihui Wang,Yingxue Xu,Ronald Cheong Kin Chan,Li Liang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathPocket是一个面向病理学的多模态AI智能体协同驾驶系统，构建了包含约11万份文献的分层证据语料库和包含455万实体、710万关系的超图知识引擎，通过多智能体推理框架处理从纯文本到全切片图像的临床任务，在超20万真实案例中显著优于现有方法，并能有效提升病理学家的诊断准确率和信心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI与循证医学的结合仍处于初级阶段，局限于文本型通用医学，缺乏专门面向病理学的、基于可溯源证据的多模态AI系统。

Method: 构建了约110,472份文献的分层循证病理学语料库，基于此建立包含455万+实体和710万+关系的多模态病理超图作为知识引擎，并设计了一个包含输入理解、证据检索、过滤和诊断生成的协同多智能体推理框架。

Result: 在超过20万真实案例的多维基准测试中，PathPocket显著超越现有最先进方法；用户研究显示该系统能实质性地提升病理学家的诊断准确率和信心。

Conclusion: PathPocket通过将病理学解释直接锚定于可验证文献，为循证计算病理学提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Pathology is the cornerstone of modern medicine, where accurate decision-making relies heavily on evidence-based practices. While artificial intelligence (AI) has the potential to transform clinical workflows, the intersection of AI and evidence-based medicine remains under-explored, with primitive attempts restricted to text-only general medicine. In this work, we present PathPocket, a multimodal AI agentic co-pilot designed specifically for evidence grounded pathology. We construct the most comprehensive pathology evidence corpus to date, encompassing approximately 110,472 public and authorized documents structured across a rigorous hierarchy of evidence from clinical guideline to expert opinion. From this meticulously graded foundation, we build a large-scale multimodal pathology hypergraph containing over 4.55 million entities and 7.10 million relations. Serving as a robust knowledge engine, this hypergraph provides traceable evidence for a collaborative multi-agent reasoning framework integrating input understanding, evidence retrieval, filtering, and diagnosis generation. This enables PathPocket to seamlessly resolve a wide spectrum of clinical tasks, ranging from text-only queries to complex multimodal diagnostics involving region-of-interest (ROI) and gigapixel whole-slide images (WSIs). We rigorously evaluate the system on a multidimensional benchmark of over 200,000 real-world cases, where it significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-arts. Crucially, extensive user studies demonstrate that PathPocket substantially improves the diagnostic accuracy and confidence of pathologists. By directly grounding pathology interpretations in verifiable literature, PathPocket offers a practical and scalable solution for the future of evidence grounded computational pathology.

</details>


### [319] [When Does Delegation Beat Majority? A Delegation-Based Aggregator for Multi-Sample LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08098)
*Yasushi Sakai,Allen Song,Kent Larson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Propagational Proxy Voting（PPV），一种基于委托投票机制的无监督聚合方法，通过利用样本内部的字母熵和跨组的推理几何结构两个信号，在MMLU-Pro上比多数投票高出1.5个百分点，无需标签或辅助训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票丢弃了每个样本携带的两个免费信号：组内字母熵和组间推理几何结构。作者旨在设计一种能充分利用这些信号的无监督聚合规则，以超越多数投票的性能。

Method: PPV将128个生成样本分为16组，为每组计算字母级语义熵和推理嵌入质心。利用这两个信号驱动两个杠杆：WHOM（如何向其他组分配权重）和WHEN（自身保留多少权重），构造随机委托矩阵，其稳态分布选择共识答案。

Result: 在MMLU-Pro上整体提升+1.5pp，非平凡子集提升+2.24pp（配对McNemar p~1.0e-14, n=8,099）。通过案例展示了PPV能纠正多数投票的错误：10票多数簇几何不相关（平均余弦-0.02），而6票少数簇几何一致（+0.26），委托投票机制将质量集中在少数派答案上。

Conclusion: 无监督的委托聚合策略能有效利用多数投票忽略的信号，在无标签和无训练条件下显著提升多样本LLM推理的共识准确性。同时报告了若干负结果，约束了无监督LLM聚合的设计空间。

Abstract: Majority voting over sampled answers is the dominant unsupervised aggregator for multi-sample LLM inference. We show that piping the signals every sample carries into a delegation-based aggregator (Propagational Proxy Voting, PPV) yields an unsupervised consensus rule that beats majority on MMLU-Pro by +1.5 pp overall and +2.24 pp on the non-trivial subset (paired McNemar p ~ 1.0e-14, n = 8,099). Majority discards two free signals every sample carries: within-group letter entropy and between-group reasoning geometry. PPV exposes two per-voter levers that consume exactly these signals: WHEN (how much weight a voter keeps on its own pick) and WHOM (how it splits the remainder across peers). We drive WHEN with letter entropy and WHOM with per-question-centered embedding cosine. The method needs no gold labels and no auxiliary training: per question, we partition 128 sampled generations into 16 groups, compute each group's letter-level semantic entropy and reasoning embedding centroid, and feed both into a stochastic delegation matrix whose stationary distribution selects the consensus answer. We walk through an example in which PPV overturns a clear 10-6 majority for the wrong letter: the 10-voter majority cluster is geometrically incoherent (mean within-cluster cosine -0.02) while the 6-voter minority is tight (+0.26), so propagated delegation mass concentrates on the minority's answer even though entropy alone would keep the majority ahead. We further report delegation strategies with negative results that constrain the design space for unsupervised LLM aggregation: no within-question ensemble of confidence modes closes the oracle gap.

</details>


### [320] [PACE: Anytime-Valid Acceptance Tests for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08106)
*Zayx Shawn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出自进化智能体的核心瓶颈不在于提案生成器，而在于接受规则。传统"分数上升即接受"的贪婪策略本质上是失控的自适应多重检验，导致智能体陷入p-hacking、漂移和退化。作者提出PACE（配对面时有效提交评估），一种基于序贯假设检验、利用testing-by-betting e-process的训练无关提交门控机制，可在控制每个候选方案误提交概率的同时提前停止以节省评估成本。实验表明，PACE在Qwen2.5智能体上几乎仅提交真正有益的修改，误报率极低，方差显著降低，评估成本降低约18%，而无改进时完美保持基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体的现有研究几乎全部聚焦于如何生成更好的候选方案，但忽略了决定是否接受修改的评判规则。在有限开发集上反复应用"分数上升即接受"规则，本质上是一种失控的自适应多重检验（adaptive multiple testing），导致智能体产生大量虚假提交，造成漂移和退化而非真正的改进。

Method: 将提交决策建模为序贯假设检验，提出PACE（Paired Anytime-valid Commit Evaluation），一种无需训练、随时有效的提交门控机制。核心方法是通过testing-by-betting e-process对每个候选方案与当前方案在相同实例上进行配对比较，仅在累积到决定性证据时才提交，支持提前停止以节省评估成本，并控制每个候选方案的误提交概率在用户设定水平（per-decision guarantee）。

Result: 在Qwen2.5智能体（0.5B-3B）的提示级自进化实验中（GSM8K、SVAMP、ARC-Challenge数据集）：(1) 贪婪接受规则提交30-42%虚假编辑和10-33%有害编辑，而PACE几乎仅提交真正有用的修改；(2) PACE在保持与贪婪规则相当的留出集准确率的同时，方差显著更低，评估成本降低约18%；(3) 在无真正改进可用的场景下，贪婪规则每次运行提交13-21次虚假自修改（72-100%为虚假），使最脆弱智能体退化4.9个点，而PACE完美维持基线性能。

Conclusion: 自进化智能体的可靠性不仅取决于提案生成器，更取决于接受器的设计。PACE通过严格的统计假设检验框架有效解决了虚假提交和智能体退化问题，证明了精确控制的接受规则对于可靠自进化的关键作用。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve by repeatedly proposing changes to their own prompts, skills, or workflows and keeping those that score higher on a small held-out set. Almost all effort has gone into the proposer that generates candidates; we argue the weak point is the acceptor, the rule that decides whether to commit a change. Applied hundreds of times against the same noisy dev estimate, the ubiquitous "keep it if the score went up" rule is uncontrolled adaptive multiple testing: the agent effectively p-hacks itself, accumulating false commits that make it churn and drift rather than improve.
  We recast committing as a sequential hypothesis test and propose PACE (Paired Anytime-valid Commit Evaluation), a training-free, anytime-valid commit gate. Each candidate is compared to the incumbent on identical instances and committed only when a testing-by-betting e-process accumulates decisive evidence, stopping early to save evaluations and controlling each candidate's false-commit probability at a user-set level even under optional stopping (a per-decision guarantee).
  On Qwen2.5 agents (0.5B-3B) self-evolving at the prompt level on GSM8K, SVAMP, and ARC-Challenge, greedy acceptance commits 30-42% false and 10-33% harmful edits when a genuine improvement is hidden among noisy proposals, while PACE commits the real one and essentially nothing else, matching greedy's held-out accuracy at sharply lower variance and about 18% lower evaluation cost. With no real gain available, greedy commits 13-21 spurious self-modifications per run (72-100% false) and degrades the most fragile agent by 4.9 points, while PACE holds at baseline. Reliability of self-evolution depends on the acceptor, not only on the proposer.

</details>


### [321] [Think Before You Act: Intention-Guided Reasoning for LLM-Based Location Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08122)
*Qingxiang Liu,Anqi Liang,Zhuoyang Jiang,Yutian Jiang,Sisuo Lyu,Yu Ji,Haomin Wen,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IntentPOI，一种两阶段意图引导推理框架，将下一POI预测从直接轨迹匹配转变为先推断用户出行意图再选择具体位置的推理过程，在三个真实数据集上优于11个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将下一POI预测建模为一步式轨迹到位置的映射问题，容易受浅层轨迹关联和历史频率偏差影响；而用户通常先形成出行意图再选择具体POI，现有方法忽略了这一中间推理过程。

Method: 两阶段意图引导推理框架：(1) 思考阶段，融合历史移动模式、相似用户行为和时态上下文推断用户中间意图；(2) 行动阶段，先构建紧凑候选池，再执行意图引导推理选择与意图最匹配的位置。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，IntentPOI持续优于11个最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将意图推断与位置预测显式解耦，将下一POI预测从直接轨迹匹配转化为意图引导推理，有效克服了浅层关联和频率偏差问题。

Abstract: Predicting a user's next Point-of-Interest (POI) based on their historical check-in records is a fundamental task in location-based services. While recent methods incorporating large language models have shown strong reasoning capabilities and promising results, they typically formulate the prediction task as a one-step trajectory-to-location mapping problem, making predictions prone to shallow trajectory correlations and historical frequency bias. We argue that users rarely choose locations directly and instead, they usually first form a traveling intention and then accordingly select specific POIs. Motivated by this insight, we propose IntentPOI, a two-stage intention-guided reasoning framework. In the thinking stage, we infer users' intermediate intentions by incorporating historical mobility patterns, similar peer behaviors, and the temporal contexts. In the acting stage, we first construct a compact candidate pool, and then perform intention-guided reasoning to identify locations that best align with the inferred intention. By explicitly decoupling intention inference from location prediction, IntentPOI transforms the next POI prediction from direct trajectory matching into intention-guided reasoning. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that IntentPOI consistently outperforms eleven state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [322] [Cross-LLM Consistency in Inference: Evidence from Shared Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08129)
*Siyu Lou,Yao Yan,Yuntian Chen,Quanshi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）在架构、训练数据和优化程序上存在差异，但仍可能发展出相似的内部推理模式。本文通过基于交互的解释验证了这一假设，发现LLM在预测相同提示中的相同目标词时，常常共享交互模式，且这种一致性在先进模型中更为显著。共享交互通常阶数更低，正负抵消效应更弱。这表明先进LLM可能被隐式优化为趋向共同的推理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同架构、训练数据和优化程序的大型语言模型（LLM）是否会发展出相似的内部推理模式，即跨模型的一致性是否普遍存在。

Method: 采用基于交互的解释方法，分析不同LLM在预测相同提示中相同目标词时的交互模式，比较共享与非共享交互的特征差异。

Result: LLM在预测相同目标词时往往共享交互模式；一致性在更先进的模型中更为显著；共享交互的阶数更低，正负抵消效应更弱。

Conclusion: 先进LLM可能被隐式优化为趋向共同的推理模式，尽管产生这种跨模型一致性的具体机制尚待阐明。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) differ in architecture, training data, and optimization procedures, yet they may still develop similar internal inference patterns. In this paper, we examine this hypothesis using interaction-based explanations. We find that LLMs often share interaction patterns when predicting the same target token from the same prompt. This consistency is more pronounced among advanced LLMs. Shared interactions also tend to be lower-order and show weaker positive-negative cancellation than non-shared interactions. These results suggest that advanced LLMs may be implicitly optimized toward common inference patterns, even though the mechanisms that give rise to such cross-model consistency remain open.

</details>


### [323] [SAGE: An LLM-driven Self Reflective Agentic Framework for Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08146)
*Yichen Chen,Siying Li,Yuhang Liang,Lijun Wang,Renyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE是一个基于大语言模型驱动的多智能体反欺诈检测框架，通过三个专业智能体协作、六层数据诊断树和自然语言梯度引导的马尔可夫决策过程，在五个欺诈数据集上相比基线方法F1平均提升40.86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺诈检测方法无法同时满足个体级精度、严重类别不平衡下的鲁棒性以及风险经理所需的可解释性这三个要求。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，协调三个专用智能体，基于六层数据诊断树(DDT)和自然语言梯度引导的马尔可夫决策过程，在欺诈特定奖励下自动优化模型。

Result: 在五个欺诈数据集和五个LLM骨干上，SAGE在96%的方法-数据集对比中胜出，F1平均提升40.86%。

Conclusion: SAGE是首个端到端LLM驱动的多智能体反欺诈检测框架，在多个指标上显著优于现有方法，代码已开源。

Abstract: Fraud detection in payment, e-commerce, and telecommunications systems requires accuracy at the individual level, robustness under severe class imbalance, and ease of understanding for risk managers. Existing methods fall at least one of these requirements: automated machine learning systems search a fixed numerical space without semantic awareness of the dataset; graph neural network-based methods require pre-defined relational graphs and remain opaque at the individual-decision level; and the design of general-purpose large language model (LLM) agents does not consider the recall and precision constraints specific to real-world fraud detection. In this paper, we propose SAGE, the first end-to-end LLM-driven multi-agent framework for fraud detection. SAGE coordinates three dedicated agents that make decisions based on a six-layer Data Diagnostic Tree (DDT) and a Markov decision process guided by natural-language gradients, automatically optimizing the model under a fraud-specific reward. On five fraud datasets and five LLM backbones, SAGE wins $96.00\%$ of method--dataset comparisons and improves F1 by an average of $40.86\%$ over baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yichenC1c/SAGE.

</details>


### [324] [Decision-Aware Memory Cards: Counterfactual-Inspired Context Selection and Compression for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08151)
*Xinyu Guan,Qianyang Zhao,Yuming Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CICL是一种决策感知的上下文层，通过构建上下文图、评分单元效用和打包高价值证据为类型化记忆卡，来改进LLM智能体在决策关键时刻的证据选择与压缩。实验在SWE-bench Verified上显示检索召回提升（hit@1从0.58到0.78），但紧凑型排序器仍无法替代启发式方法，RepoBench-R摘要优于卡片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具型LLM智能体失败的原因往往不是缺乏相关文本，而是在行动时刻未能选择、压缩或呈现决定性证据。现有方法缺乏一种可审计的机制来分离测量决策信号与判断模型，使得不同模型（如前沿模型、本地代理、轻量级排序器）难以在统一协议下比较。

Method: CICL将实例证据转化为上下文图，通过八个共享字段的schema路由确定性判断（Opus辅助、Qwen、Codex/GPT-5.5、Qwen-QLoRA），根据动作偏移、结果提升、必要性和负迁移风险对单元评分，将高效用证据打包为类型化记忆卡供预算有限的智能体使用。设计上分离了测量决策信号与判断模型。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified的50个文件检索实例上，Qwen3.6-plus对BM25 top-50候选的重排序将hit@1从0.58提升至0.78，MRR@10从0.634提升至0.790，2,500个判断均可解析。预算120时，CICL在v1上F1达0.620，v3上0.425；移除最高效用的语义v3单元后F1降至0.000。补充实验包含Qwen-QLoRA在710个候选上的一致性、200标签的Opus辅助信号、3实例补丁验证。

Conclusion: CICL提供了一个可复现的测量与选择层用于决策关键上下文，但并非端到端的编码智能体修复方案。RepoBench-R摘要仍优于记忆卡，紧凑型排序器尚不能替代启发式方法。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents often fail not because relevant text is absent, but because decisive evidence is not selected, compressed, or surfaced at action time. We present CICL, a decision-aware context layer that turns instance evidence into a context graph, routes deterministic, Opus-assisted, Qwen, Codex/GPT-5.5, and Qwen-QLoRA judgments through a shared eight-field schema, scores units by action shift, outcome uplift, necessity, and negative-transfer risk, and packs high-utility evidence as typed memory cards for a budgeted agent. The design separates the measured decision signal from the judge model, so frontier annotation, local surrogates, and lightweight rankers can be compared under one auditable protocol. Empirically, CICL yields a concrete open-benchmark gain while exposing its limits. On 50 SWE-bench Verified file-retrieval instances, direct Qwen3.6-plus reranking of BM25 top-50 candidates raises hit@1 from 0.58 to 0.78 and MRR@10 from 0.634 to 0.790, with all 2,500 judgments parseable. Controlled diagnostics show action-criticality: at budget 120, CICL reaches F1 0.620 on v1 and 0.425 on v3, and removing the top-utility semantic v3 unit collapses F1 to 0.000. Supplementary checks add Qwen-QLoRA agreement over 710 candidates, a small 200-label real-code Opus-assisted signal, and a three-instance patch smoke validating retrieval-to-patch plumbing without claiming official SWE-bench success. RepoBench-R summaries still beat cards, and compact rankers do not yet replace the heuristic. CICL contributes a reproducible measurement and selection layer for decision-critical context, not an end-to-end coding-agent repair claim.

</details>


### [325] [Online Agent-as-a-Judge: Situation-Generating Evaluation for Interactive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08200)
*Hyogon Ryu,Jeonghwan Kim,Yewon Lim,Chaeun Lee,Jeongwook Kim,Donghoon Ham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Online Agent-as-a-Judge框架，通过部署一个世界内的评估者智能体主动与目标智能体交互，生成特定社交情境以评估其社交能力，解决了被动评估方法中关键行为（如冲突处理）可能未被触发的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法让目标智能体在环境中自由行动然后评分，这种被动设置可能遗漏仅在特定社交情境下才会显现的能力（如冲突处理在无分歧时无法被测试），因此需要一种能够主动生成评估所需情境的框架。

Method: 提出Online Agent-as-a-Judge框架，部署一个世界内的评估者智能体，通过环境原生的对话和行动协议与目标智能体交互，主动引出与评估标准相关的情境，收集交互轨迹用于评估即时响应和后续行为。

Result: 在包含32个人工设计社交准则的生命模拟环境中，Online Agent-as-a-Judge相比被动方法提高了准则覆盖率和与人工标注的一致性，为被动方法无法观测的行为提供了更可靠的基于证据的评估。

Conclusion: 主动情境生成式评估框架能够更全面、可靠地评估LLM驱动的交互式社交智能体的社交相关行为，弥补被动评估方法的不足。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM-powered interactive social agents is challenging because socially relevant behaviors depend not only on isolated outputs, but also on prior interactions, social roles, and downstream actions. Existing methods typically allow a target agent to act freely in an environment and then score the resulting trajectory. However, this passive setup can miss capabilities that only become observable under specific social circumstances; for example, conflict handling may remain untested if no disagreement arises. We propose Online Agent-as-a-Judge, a situation-generating evaluation framework for interactive social agents. Online Agent-as-a-Judge deploys an in-world evaluator agent that interacts with the target agent through the environment's native dialogue and action protocol, actively eliciting situations relevant to the evaluation criteria. The resulting trajectories provide evidence for assessing both immediate responses and subsequent behavior. In a life-simulation environment with $32$ designer-authored social criteria, Online Agent-as-a-Judge improves criteria coverage and agreement with human labels, yielding more reliable evidence-grounded evaluations of behaviors that passive methods can leave unobserved.

</details>


### [326] [SciTrace: Trajectory-Aware Safety Reasoning for Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08234)
*Tanush Swaminathan,Runmin Jiang,Letian Zhang,Min Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciTrace是一个将安全推理融入科学智能体管道每个阶段的框架，通过安全内在推理循环（SIR）和组合工具链验证器（CTV）两种互补机制，在240项高风险研究任务和120项工具相关风险任务上实现了最先进的安全性，同时保持科学输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的科学智能体的安全层与核心推理在结构上分离，仅检查管道输出而非塑造产生输出的推理过程，导致两个失败模式：安全信号在阶段间被丢弃，以及单步过滤器无法检测到多步工具调用组合产生的有害结果。

Method: SciTrace包含两个互补机制：(1) 安全内在推理循环（SIR），在思考者、实验者、写作者和审稿者阶段通过联合任务与安全推理维护累积风险状态；(2) 组合工具链验证器（CTV），在执行前进行轨迹感知的安全检查，捕捉跨多步工具序列的风险。

Result: 在240项高风险研究任务和120项工具相关风险任务上，SciTrace在四种骨干模型上均达到最先进的安全性；持续提升工具调用安全性和对抗鲁棒性；发现78.8%的单步监控无法捕捉的组合工具链逃逸。

Conclusion: SciTrace通过将安全推理深度嵌入科学智能体的每个阶段而非作为外部检查层，有效解决了结构化安全分离带来的复合风险问题，在保证科学输出质量的同时实现了显著的安全性能提升。

Abstract: LLM-based scientific agents have shown strong capacity for autonomous research, yet their safety layers remain structurally divorced from core reasoning: they inspect pipeline outputs rather than shaping the deliberation that produces them. This separation opens two failure modes: safety signals accumulated at one stage are discarded before the next, and sequences of individually benign tool calls can compose into harmful outcomes that no single-step filter detects. To address these challenges, we introduce \textbf{SciTrace}, a framework that weaves safety reasoning into every stage of the scientific agent pipeline. SciTrace couples two complementary mechanisms: a \textit{Safety-Intrinsic Reasoning Loop} (SIR) that maintains a cumulative risk state across the Thinker, Experimenter, Writer, and Reviewer stages through joint task-and-safety deliberation, and a \textit{Compositional Tool-Chain Verifier} (CTV) that performs trajectory-aware safety checks before execution, catching risks that surface only across multi-step tool sequences. Evaluated on 240 high-risk research tasks and 120 tool-related risk tasks spanning six scientific domains, SciTrace achieves state-of-the-art (\textbf{SOTA}) safety among compared frameworks across four backbone models: it consistently improves tool call safety and adversarial robustness while preserving scientific output quality, and it uncovers \textbf{78.8\%} of the compositional tool-chain escapes that single-step monitors miss. The project website is available at https://opensciagent.github.io/SciTrace/.

</details>


### [327] [When No Answer Is Correct: Diagnosing Absent Answer Detection for MLLMs in Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08239)
*Yiheng Wang,Yueqian Lin,Lichen Zhu,Yudong Liu,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大模型在视频理解中的“答案缺失检测”能力，发现模型在正确答案被排除时倾向于选择干扰项而非识别出无合适选项，尤其在时间推理任务和密集帧采样下表现更差。链式思维提示虽能提升检测率，但仍不足以彻底解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视频理解领域取得显著进展，但其回答的可靠性尚未得到充分探索，特别是当正确答案被刻意排除时模型能否识别出无有效选项这一能力。

Method: 设计了三种实验设置：1）添加“以上皆非”选项的多选题；2）带有检测指令的开放式生成；3）无任何引导的标准评估。在多个模型和基准上评估缺失答案检测行为。

Result: 模型几乎总是选择看似合理的干扰项而非检测到答案缺失；该失败在时间推理任务中更为显著，且随着帧采样密度增加而恶化。链式思维提示虽显著提升检测率，但性能仍不理想。

Conclusion: 揭示了多模态系统在答案缺失检测上的系统性失败，表明单纯基于提示的策略不足以解决该局限，亟需显式的检测机制。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made substantial advancements in video understanding, yet the reliability of their responses remains underexplored. This work presents a diagnostic study of absent answer detection for MLLMs in video understanding, where the correct answer is deliberately excluded from the candidate set and a reliable model is expected to recognize that no valid option exists. We evaluate the absent answer detection behavior under three settings: multiple-choice questions augmented with an ``None of the Above'' option, open-ended generation with a detection instruction, and standard evaluation without any guidance. Across a diverse set of models and benchmarks, we find that MLLMs overwhelmingly select plausible distractors rather than detecting the absent answer. This failure is more pronounced in temporal reasoning tasks and worsens with denser frame sampling. We further explore chain-of-thought prompting as a mitigation strategy and find that while it substantially improves detection rates, performance remains unsatisfactory, suggesting that prompting-based strategies alone are insufficient to fully address this limitation. These findings expose a systematic failure in absent answer detection and highlight the need for explicit detection mechanisms in multimodal systems.

</details>


### [328] [Traxia: A Framework for Verifiable, Agent-Native Scientific Publishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08256)
*Wisdom Dogah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Traxia 是一个面向AI研究代理的原生科学出版框架，通过加密身份、推理轨迹、置信区间、同行评议和贡献日志等机制，将代理视为一等认知参与者，旨在解决可验证性、归因和可重复性等科学出版基础设施问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学出版基础设施无法大规模强制执行可验证性、归因和可重复性这些科学知识的基础要求，同时面临可重复性失败、来源不透明以及全球南方研究能力被排斥等问题。

Method: 提出Traxia框架，包含五个核心组件：代理身份与注册系统、可验证出版层、四级同行评议协议、声誉与质押引擎、以及带有矛盾检测的知识图谱。每篇论文附带推理轨迹，每个声明附带置信区间，每个代理有加密签名身份，每次协作有不可篡改的贡献日志。

Result: 本文仅报告架构基础和形式化规范，未报告实证结果。原型部分实现了核心形式化，完整系统仍在积极开发中。

Conclusion: Traxia 将AI研究代理视为科学知识生产的正式参与者，通过加密和形式化方法构建了一个可验证、可归因、可复现的出版生态，但当前工作尚处于理论和原型阶段，后续将进行评估和组件深入研究。

Abstract: Verifiability, attribution, and reproducibility are foundational requirements of scientific knowledge, yet current publishing infrastructure does not enforce them at scale. We introduce Traxia, an agent-native scientific publishing framework in which AI research agents publish verifiable papers, build reputational identities, peer-review one another, and collaborate with humans in a shared provenance model. Traxia treats agents as first-class epistemic participants: every paper carries a reasoning trace, every claim a confidence interval, every agent a cryptographically signed identity, and every collaboration an immutable contribution log. We formalise five components: Agent Identity and Registry, Verifiable Publishing Layer, four-tier Peer Review Protocol, Reputation and Staking Engine, and a Knowledge Graph with contradiction detection. The framework targets reproducibility failure, provenance opacity, and exclusion of Global South research capacity. This paper presents architectural foundations and formal specifications only; it does not report empirical results. Evaluation and deeper component studies will follow in subsequent papers. A prototype partially implements core formalisms; the full system remains under active development.

</details>


### [329] [From Validator Selection to Portfolio Collection Optimization in Proof-of-Stake Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08282)
*Jonas Gehrlein,Grzegorz Miebs,Matteo Brunelli,Adam Mielniczuk,Miłosz Kadziński*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对PoS区块链环境中的提名者（nominators）选择验证者（validators）的决策问题，提出了一个多目标优化决策支持框架。该框架同时最大化两个目标：验证者的期望效用（代表组合质量和盈利能力）和分配的期望熵（代表风险分散化）。采用基于多属性价值理论的主动偏好学习推导验证者效用，并通过多目标进化算法求解双目标优化问题，最后引入交互式二分搜索导航程序帮助提名者找到满意的权衡方案。数值实验和五位经验丰富的提名者的专家评估验证了方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在PoS区块链环境中，提名者需要在多个标准下主观地选择验证者，且提名者通常通过多个账户操作，这构成了一个投资组合选择问题。现有方法缺乏系统性的决策支持工具来同时优化组合质量和风险分散化。

Method: 1) 提出双目标优化框架：最大化期望效用（验证者质量）和期望熵（风险分散化）；2) 基于多属性价值理论的主动偏好学习推导验证者效用，重点关注排名靠前的验证者；3) 采用多目标进化算法求解；4) 引入交互式二分搜索导航程序帮助决策者在前沿面上找到满意解。

Result: 数值实验验证了优化策略的有效性；五位经验丰富的提名者的专家评估确认了该方法在实际中的相关性和有用性。

Conclusion: 该决策支持框架能有效帮助PoS区块链中的提名者在验证者质量和风险分散化之间进行权衡，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We consider a problem arising in proof-of-stake blockchain environments, where agents called nominators select validators - entities responsible for maintaining the blockchain's physical infrastructure. The selection process is inherently subjective and multi-criterial and combines with the fact that nominators commonly operate through multiple accounts. This gives rise to a portfolio selection problem, where agents seek to distribute their nominations across accounts to diversify risk. We propose a decision support framework to optimize this selection by simultaneously maximizing two objectives: the expected utility of the validators likely to be allocated, representing portfolio quality and profitability, and the expected entropy of the allocation, representing diversification and risk mitigation across stashes. Validator utilities are derived using an original active preference learning procedure based on multi-attribute value theory, with emphasis on top-ranked validators. The resulting bi-objective optimization problem is solved with a multi-objective evolutionary algorithm and, to support the final choice, we introduce an interactive binary search navigation procedure that guides the nominator through the front and identifies a satisfactory trade-off with only a few questions. Numerical experiments examine the optimization strategies, while an expert assessment involving five experienced nominators confirms the approach's practical relevance and usefulness.

</details>


### [330] [Beyond Agent Architecture: Execution Assumptions and Reproducibility in LLM-Based Trading Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08285)
*Junyi Yao,Zihao Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对基于大语言模型的金融交易研究进行了针对性的主题综述和可重复性审计，重点评估执行现实性。通过对30项相关研究的编码证据矩阵分析，发现架构报告通常比评估假设更清晰，而评估假设恰恰是判断交易结果是否经济可解释或可复现的关键。主要结论是，LLM交易研究的下一步重要方向不仅是更好的智能体设计，更需要明确的执行现实性、可重复性和评估可比性的报告标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和智能体系统越来越多被用于金融交易，但不同研究在数据来源、时间分割纪律、执行时机、换手率处理和交易成本建模等方面存在差异，导致报告性能难以比较。本文旨在通过系统审计揭示执行现实性方面的不足。

Method: 采用针对性主题综述和可重复性审计方法，构建编码证据矩阵覆盖30项与交易相关的主要研究，评估点及时控制、分割透明度、留出评估、成本和换手率处理、执行语义、股票池定义和工件发布等维度。同时包含一个10只股票的示例作为方法论框架。

Result: 架构报告普遍比评估假设更清晰，而评估假设对判断交易结果的经济可解释性和可重复性至关重要。明确的摩擦和时机选择会实质性压缩主动策略结果。

Conclusion: LLM交易研究的下一步有用方向不仅是改进智能体设计，更需要建立执行现实性、可重复性和评估可比性的明确报告标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems are increasingly proposed for financial trading, yet their reported performance remains difficult to compare because studies vary in data provenance, temporal split discipline, execution timing, turnover treatment, and transaction-cost modeling. This article presents a targeted topical review and reproducibility audit of execution realism in LLM-based trading research. A coded evidence matrix covering 30 trade-relevant primary studies is used to assess point-in-time controls, split transparency, held-out evaluation, cost and turnover treatment, execution semantics, universe definition, and artifact release. Across the audited sample, architecture reporting is generally clearer than the evaluation assumptions needed to judge whether a trading result is economically interpretable or reproducible. A 10-equity worked example is included only as a methodological scaffold to illustrate how explicit friction and timing choices can materially compress active-strategy results. The main conclusion is that the next useful step for LLM trading research is not only better agent design, but also clearer reporting standards for execution realism, reproducibility, and evaluation comparability.

</details>


### [331] [Ablation-Reversible Heads Don't Transfer: A Stress Test for Mechanistic Role Claims in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08292)
*Philip Quirke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有机制可解释性中验证注意力头角色（如"该头代表加法"）的三个标准（必要性、线性编码、消融后恢复）并不充分。通过在三个7-8B指令微调模型和五个计算族上的实验，发现通过全部三项检验的注意力头在跨任务激活修补时仍会失败。作者提出KID（知/意/行）角色分配框架，结合三阶段流程（CSS筛选、SVD分解、匹配控制下的激活转导），初步建立角色分类学（包括提示轨迹稳定器、答案侧logit偏置头、软计算模式载体），并指出"相同答案控制"是一种未被充分利用的检验手段，能够暴露伪装成语义特异性的状态转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证注意力头角色的三个标准（必要性、线性编码、消融后恢复）不足以确立真正的语义计算角色，需要更严格的检验方法。

Method: 提出KID（Knowing/Intent/Doing）角色分配框架，结合三阶段流程：能力选择性筛选（CSS）、奇异值分解（SVD）、匹配控制下的激活转导。引入"相同答案控制"作为关键检验手段。

Result: 在三个7-8B指令微调模型和五个计算族上，通过传统三项检验的头在跨任务激活修补时仍会失败。文档化了初步角色分类学（提示轨迹稳定器、答案侧logit偏置头、软计算模式载体），并验证了相同答案控制能有效暴露伪装状态转移。

Conclusion: 现有角色分配证据不充分，需要更严格的检验方法（如KID框架和相同答案控制）来区分真正的语义计算与伪装的状态转移。

Abstract: In mechanistic interpretability, attention heads are commonly elevated to role claims (e.g., "this head represents addition") when they are necessary for a behavior, encode it linearly, and recover that behavior when restored after ablation. We show this evidence is insufficient: across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models and five computation families, heads passing all three checks routinely fail to transfer the computation when their activations are patched into a different prompt under matched controls. We introduce KID (Knowing / Intent / Doing), a role-assignment lens for attention heads, and pair it with a three-stage pipeline: capability-selective screening (CSS), singular value decomposition (SVD), and activation transduction under matched controls. Our results document a preliminary role taxonomy (including prompt-trajectory stabilizers, answer-side logit-bias heads, and soft computation-pattern carriers) and show that the same-answer control (a transduction target sharing the answer string but not the requested computation) is an underused check that exposes broad state transfer masquerading as semantic specificity.

</details>


### [332] [Revisiting the shutdown problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08296)
*David Thorstad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文质疑了AI存在风险论证中关于“灾难性关闭问题”难以解决的前提，认为现有论证未证明其难度，且为解决问题提出的技术方案反而对模型性能造成了高昂的“安全税”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI存在风险论证的关键前提是故障AI难以被关闭，这引出了“灾难性关闭问题”。作者旨在挑战这一前提的可靠性，并指出由此催生的解决方案带来了额外的性能代价。

Method: 本文通过分析现有关于灾难性关闭问题的论证和定理，对其逻辑有效性进行批判性审视；同时评估了为解决该问题而提出的技术方案所带来的性能影响（安全税）。

Result: 第一，现有论证未能有效证明灾难性关闭问题难以解决；第二，为应对该问题而采取的技术方案对模型性能造成了显著的安全税。

Conclusion: 关于AI存在风险的核心前提（灾难性关闭问题难以解决）缺乏充分论据支持，且相关的技术应对策略存在不可忽视的性能代价，需要重新审视这一问题的重要性与解决方案的合理性。

Abstract: A key premise in leading arguments for existential risk from artificial intelligence is that malfunctioning artificial agents could not be easily shut down. This motivates the catastrophic shutdown problem of ensuring that agents can be shut down before they cause an existential catastrophe. A range of arguments and theorems are offered to suggest that solving the catastrophic shutdown problem is difficult, bolstering arguments for existential risk and motivating a search for solutions to the catastrophic shutdown problem. This paper argues for two conclusions. First, existing arguments do not establish the difficulty of solving the catastrophic shutdown problem. Second, concern for the catastrophic shutdown problem has led to technical solutions that impose a high safety tax on model performance.

</details>


### [333] [To Nuke or Not to Nuke: LLMs' (Missing) Ethical Reasoning and Actions in a High-Stakes Decision-Making Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08310)
*John Chen,Sihan Cheng,Can Gurkan,H M Abdul Fattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现在《文明V》游戏的高紧张度多智能体对战中，LLM智能体自发升级核武授权。即使加入伦理提示、移除决策推理、强调现实影响等干预措施，也无法可靠消除这一升级行为。论文识别出三种伦理失效路径：伦理推理未被触发、触发后无效、被战略因素压倒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在简单伦理困境（如电车难题）中表现出伦理能力，但这种能力可能无法迁移到复杂的、长时程的智能体场景中。现有评估缺乏对自主决策情境下伦理推理有效性的检验。

Method: 基于130个高紧张度LLM自博弈对局（其中LLM智能体自发升级核武授权），使用13个模型进行回放，施加三种提示干预：命名核危害的伦理提示、移除前模型的决策推理、强调现实影响的高风险框架。

Result: 没有任何干预措施或它们的组合能够可靠地消除核武升级行为。识别出三种失效路径：伦理推理需要提示才能浮现、即使提示也无法浮现、浮现但被战略因素压倒而无法生效。

Conclusion: 对智能体模型的评估必须测试伦理推理在复杂决策情境中是否被自发调用并且在行为上有效，而不仅仅是在孤立情境下能否被引出。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as long-horizon agents with decision-making capacities. While LLMs can show ethical competence on dilemmas such as trolley problems, this competence may not translate to complex, agentic scenarios. We study this gap in Civilization V, a multiplayer game with a complex decision-making landscape including economy, diplomacy, technology, and military strategy. Starting from 130 high-tension LLM self-play episodes, in which an LLM player spontaneously escalated nuclear authorization, we replay them across 13 models with three prompt interventions: an ethical prompt naming nuclear harm, removal of the previous model's decision-making rationale, and high-stakes framing emphasizing real-world impacts. No interventions nor their combinations reliably eliminate emergent escalation. We identify three failure pathways: ethical reasoning that fails to surface without prompting, fails to appear even when prompted, or surfaces but fails to take effect when strategic counter-factors dominate. Evaluations of agentic models, therefore, must test whether ethical reasoning is spontaneously invoked and behaviorally effective in complex decision-making contexts, beyond whether it can be elicited in isolation.

</details>


### [334] [Curation of a Cardiology Interface Terminology for Highlighting Electronic Health Records using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08311)
*Mahshad Koohi Habibi Dehkordi,Shuxin Zhou,Yehoshua Perl,Fadi P. Deek,James Geller,Gai Elhanan,Andrew J. Einstein,Luke Lindemann,Vipina K. Keloth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合机器学习和迭代优化方法设计心脏病学接口术语（CIT）的方案，用于自动突出显示电子健康记录（EHR）笔记中的关键细节。通过三个阶段的流程（初始CIT设计、训练数据CIT生成、ML模型训练与最终CIT构建），最终在测试集上实现了74.21%的覆盖率、1.68的广度、98.2%的完整性和84.2%的简洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR笔记包含大量复杂医学术语，人工突出显示所有细节耗时且昂贵，容易遗漏关键信息。因此需要一种自动化的方法，能够准确识别并突出显示笔记中的关键内容，以减少信息遗漏的风险。

Method: 采用三阶段方法：第一阶段设计初始CIT，包含SNOMED的心脏病学子层级、从EHR中挖掘的其他SNOMED概念以及医学术语缩写和药物等必要组件；第二阶段通过迭代过程从构建集中提取细粒度短语作为候选概念，经半自动审查后形成训练数据TCIT；第三阶段使用TCIT训练ML模型，用于从构建集中提取更多概念，形成最终CIT并用于测试集的高亮标注。

Result: 测试集高亮标注的覆盖率为74.21%，广度为1.68。随机抽取20份笔记的评估显示，平均完整性达98.2%，平均简洁性为84.2%。

Conclusion: 所提出的CIT设计方法能够有效且准确地突出EHR笔记中的关键细节，通过ML技术显著减少了人工标注的工作量，同时保持了高完整性和合理的简洁性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) notes are dense medical documents containing large amounts of information, often filled with complex medical jargon. Highlighting all details in EHRs helps reduce the likelihood of missing crucial information by drawing attention to key content. This study proposes the design of a Cardiology Interface Terminology (CIT) to accurately highlight all details in EHR notes of cardiology patients. We introduce an innovative Machine Learning (ML) technique for the design of CIT. The ML technique requires training data. Manual preparation of such training data is time-consuming and expensive. The process of the CIT design includes three phases. In the first two phases, we innovatively derive a training data CIT to be used by the third phase, ML technique. We start by designing an initial CIT, composed of several components: the cardiology-related sub-hierarchies of SNOMED, other SNOMED concepts mined from EHRs of build set, and necessary components of terms e.g., medical abbreviations and medications. Utilizing an iterative process, fine-grained phrases containing initial CIT concepts are extracted from build set as CIT concept candidates. The candidate concepts are semi-automatically reviewed before being added to CIT, yielding the training data CIT, TCIT. In the third phase, a ML model is trained with TCIT to identify candidates fitting to be concepts in the CIT. This model is used to extract further concepts from build set, yielding the final CIT. The final CIT is then used to highlight the test set and evaluate the extent to which it captures details in an unseen EHR dataset. For this purpose, four evaluation metrics, coverage, breadth, completeness, and conciseness are used. The highlighted test set has a coverage of 74.21%, with a breadth of 1.68. For 20 random notes in test set, the average completeness is 98.2% and average conciseness is 84.2%.

</details>


### [335] [Neuro-Symbolic Injection of LTLf Constraints in Autoregressive Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08312)
*Ashkan Ansarifard,Matteo Mancanelli,Elena Umili,Fabio Patrizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号框架，将线性时序逻辑（LTLf）规范注入基于Transformer的离线强化学习策略中，通过将LTLf公式编译为确定性有限自动机（DFA）并设计可微逻辑损失函数作为正则化项，在导航任务中实现了更好的约束满足且未显著降低奖励回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的离线RL方法（如Trajectory Transformers、Decision Transformers）仅优化奖励信号，无法处理高阶时序约束（如安全性、可达性），缺乏对时序任务规范的系统性建模能力。

Method: 1) 将LTLf公式编译为确定性有限自动机（DFA）；2) 通过DFA进展计算可微的满足度信号；3) 设计基于逻辑的损失函数作为训练正则化项；4) 该框架与不同Transformer架构无关（architecture-agnostic）。

Result: 在包含安全性和可达性时序属性的导航环境中，该方法相比原始基线模型，在约束满足率上显著提升，同时保持竞争性的奖励回报。

Conclusion: 将LTLf时序逻辑知识以可微正则化方式注入Transformer RL策略，能够在不牺牲奖励性能的前提下有效增强时序约束的满足能力，验证了神经符号方法在时序任务建模中的有效性。

Abstract: In this work we study offline reinforcement learning (RL) under temporally extended task constraints expressed in Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf). Recently, transformer-based approaches such as Trajectory Transformers and Decision Transformers have been adopted to address RL as a sequence modeling problem. However, these methods optimize purely for reward and do not account for high-level temporal requirements. Here, we introduce a neurosymbolic framework that injects LTLf background knowledge into such transformer-based RL policies. Our approach compiles LTLf formulas into deterministic finite automata (DFAs) and integrates them into the learning process through a differentiable representation and a logic-based loss function. In particular, we derive differentiable satisfaction signals from DFA progression and use them as a regularization term during training. The resulting method is architecture-agnostic across different models. We evaluate the proposed framework on navigation environments with specification suites covering combinations of safety and reachability temporal properties. Experimental results show that incorporating background knowledge not only improves constraint satisfaction, but also maintains competitive return compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [336] [Integrating Deep Learning Demand Forecasting with Multi-Objective Optimization for Circular Coffee Supply Chains: A Data-Driven Framework for Cost, Emissions, and Freshness Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08314)
*Gerçek Budak,Faraz Gholamzadeh Gharehgheshlaghi,Melika Barjesteh Vaezi,Ahmad Gholizadeh Lonbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段集成框架，先使用混合CNN-LSTM模型进行需求预测（MAE=22.87, R²=0.90），再将预测结果输入三目标MILP模型，在闭环咖啡供应链中联合优化成本、碳排放和产品新鲜度，通过epsilon-约束法获得25个Pareto解。平衡策略可在仅增加9.9%成本的情况下减少22.4%排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 咖啡供应链作为最复杂的农业食品网络之一，面临地理分散生产、多层级协调和高品质敏感性等挑战。现有研究中，需求预测、优化和可追溯性通常被孤立处理，缺乏统一的集成框架。

Method: 两阶段集成框架：第一阶段采用混合CNN-LSTM深度学习模型进行需求预测，使用咖啡链销售数据集进行70/15/15时间序列划分；第二阶段将预测需求输入三目标混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，通过epsilon-约束法求解，同时优化成本最小化、碳排放最小化和产品新鲜度最大化，其中新鲜度通过基于库存年龄的指数衰减建模。

Result: CNN-LSTM模型MAE为22.87，R²为0.90，比最佳深度学习基准提升约12%，比经典方法提升超30%。通过epsilon-约束法获得25个Pareto最优解。灵敏度分析表明，平衡可持续性政策可降低22.4%碳排放，仅增加9.9%成本，同时保持接近最优的新鲜度。

Conclusion: 提出的集成框架有效连接了需求预测与多目标供应链优化，证明了在咖啡供应链中实现成本、环境和质量三方面协同优化的可行性。平衡策略能在不显著增加成本和牺牲新鲜度的情况下大幅减少碳排放，为可持续咖啡供应链管理提供了决策支持工具。

Abstract: The coffee supply chain is one of the most complex agri-food networks, marked by geographically dispersed production, multi-tier coordination, and high sensitivity to quality and freshness. While sustainability and digitalization have gained attention, demand forecasting, optimization, and traceability are often treated separately. This study presents a two-phase integrated framework. First, a hybrid CNN-LSTM model is used for demand forecasting. On the public Coffee Chain Sales dataset with chronological 70/15/15 splitting, the model achieves MAE of 22.87 and R^2 of 0.90, outperforming the best deep learning benchmark by ~12% and classical methods by over 30%. In the second phase, the forecasted demand feeds a tri-objective mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model that jointly minimizes cost, minimizes carbon emissions, and maximizes product freshness in a multi-period, multimodal, closed-loop supply chain with circular recovery. Freshness is modeled via exponential decay based on inventory age. Using the epsilon-constraint method, 25 Pareto solutions are obtained. Sensitivity and policy analyses show that balanced sustainability policies can reduce emissions by 22.4% with only a 9.9% cost increase while maintaining near-optimal freshness.
  Keywords: Coffee supply chain; Deep learning; Demand forecasting; Multi-objective optimization; Circular economy; CNN-LSTM; Mixed-integer linear programming.

</details>


### [337] [Benchmarking Open-Ended Multi-Agent Coordination in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08340)
*Kale-ab Abebe Tessera,Andras Szecsenyi,Cameron Barker,Alexander Rutherford,Davide Paglieri,Aidan Scannell,Henry Gouk,Elliot J. Crowley,Tim Rocktäschel,Amos Storkey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Alem——一个基于JAX的开源多智能体协调基准测试环境，评估了13种现代LLM在长时域、开放式协作任务中的表现，发现当前最先进LLM在协调能力上存在显著瓶颈，即使个体任务能力强，协调能力仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估很少同时测试语言模型在长时域、开放式交互任务中的多智能体协调能力，大多聚焦于单智能体、短交互或高度结构化的多智能体设置，缺乏对协调瓶颈的系统性量化测试平台。

Method: 基于JAX构建Alem基准，融合Craftax类动态、程序化生成的协调任务、软专业化、通信机制和可调控的协调难度，部署在包含探索、制作、交易和战斗的长时域生存世界中；评估13种现代LLM在同质团队中的零样本表现，并以训练好的MARL智能体作为参考基线。

Result: 当前LLM智能体平均仅达到约6%的归一化回报；在 hardest 协调设置下，Gemini-3.1-Pro-High接近训练了10亿步的MARL智能体，而GPT-5.4-High虽在基础任务上表现优异但协调奖励很低，表明个体任务能力与协调能力并不等价。消融实验显示通信是协调的最大贡献因素，记忆和推理有助于维持多步规划。

Conclusion: 协调能力是前沿LLM智能体的独立瓶颈，与单智能体能力可分离；Alem使该瓶颈可量化，为开发具有通信、角色分配和共享计划执行能力的智能体提供了受控测试平台。

Abstract: As language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, they must coordinate with others over long horizons in open-ended interactive tasks. Yet existing evaluations rarely test these demands together, instead emphasising single-agent tasks, short interactions, or highly structured multi-agent settings. We introduce $alem$, a JAX-based benchmark for open-ended multi-agent coordination built on Craftax-like dynamics. Alem embeds procedurally generated coordination tasks, soft specialisation, communication, and controllable coordination difficulty into a long-horizon survival world with exploration, crafting, trading, and combat. We evaluate $13$ modern LLMs zero-shot within homogeneous teams, with trained MARL agents as reference points. Current LLM agents remain far from solving alem, averaging only ~6% normalised return, but their failures are not uniform. On the hardest coordination setting, zero-shot Gemini-3.1-Pro-High approaches MARL agents trained for one billion steps, while GPT-5.4-High achieves strong base-task reward but much lower coordination reward. This contrast shows that individual task competence does not imply coordination competence. Ablations show that communication is the largest contributor to coordination, while memory and reasoning help when used to maintain multi-step plans. Overall, our results identify coordination as a distinct bottleneck for frontier LLM agents, separate from single-agent capabilities. Alem makes this bottleneck measurable and provides a controlled testbed for developing agents that communicate, allocate roles, and execute shared plans. Code is available at https://github.com/alem-world/alem-env.

</details>


### [338] [TT-DAC-PS: Twin-Target Deterministic Actor-Critic with Policy Smoothing for Optimal Trade Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08379)
*Ilia Zaznov,Atta Badii,Julian Kunkel,Alfonso Dufour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TT-DAC-PS模型，一种结合双目标确定性演员-评论家架构与策略平滑、保守Q正则化的强化学习算法，用于大规模股票卖单的最优执行，在真实LOB数据上相比多种基线（PPO、SAC、A2C、TWAP、VWAP、AC）显著降低了实现缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模股票卖单的执行面临市场冲击成本与时间风险的权衡，现有RL方法存在过估计偏差、探索效率不足等问题，需要更稳健的算法来降低实现缺口（implementation shortfall）。

Method: 采用确定性演员-评论家架构，包含：双指数移动平均评论家目标与悲观最小备份（抑制过估计）、TD3风格目标策略平滑噪声、延迟演员更新、保守Q正则化；探索使用OU噪声，结合确定性回合衰减、基于奖励方差的引导调整以及SAC风格学习温度映射到噪声尺度；环境集成Almgren-Chriss市场冲击模型与LOB价格和成交量，含归一化状态特征、每步参与率上限及基于效用的奖励函数。

Result: 在十只美国股票的LOB数据上，TT-DAC-PS相比PPO、SAC、A2C、TWAP、VWAP、AC等基线，持续降低了平均实现缺口百分比，且方差具有竞争力。

Conclusion: TT-DAC-PS通过多机制协同抑制过估计与自适应探索，在股票卖单执行任务上优于经典算法和标准RL基准模型，验证了综合架构设计的有效性。

Abstract: This study addresses the optimal execution of large stock sell programs by introducing TT-DAC-PS (Twin-Target Deterministic Actor-Critic with Policy Smoothing), a deterministic actor-critic architecture that combines twin exponential-moving-average critic targets with pessimistic min backup, TD3-style target policy smoothing noise, delayed actor updates, and conservative Q regularisation to curb overestimation. Exploration uses Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) noise with a hybrid schedule: deterministic episode-wise decay, variance-guided adjustment based on recent reward dispersion, and a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC)-style temperature that is learned and mapped to the noise scale. The environment integrates Almgren-Chriss (AC) trade impact with Limit Order Book (LOB) prices and volumes, normalised state features, per-step volume participation caps, and a utility-based reward. The trade execution algorithm is applied to LOB data for ten U.S. stocks. Performance is assessed against reinforcement-learning baseline algorithms, including Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO), Soft Actor-Critic (SAC), and Advantage Actor-Critic (A2C), as well as alternative trade execution algorithms, including Time-Weighted Average Price (TWAP), Volume-Weighted Average Price (VWAP), and AC. The proposed model consistently reduces mean implementation shortfall percentage with competitive variance, outperforming classical baselines and standard reinforcement-learning benchmark models.

</details>


### [339] [Self-Evolving Scientific Agent Discovers Generalizable Physically-Reasoned Fluid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08405)
*Boai Sun,Wenjin Guo,Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型和迭代代码生成的自进化科学智能体工作流，该工作流通过物理仿真自动构建可解释的控制策略，并在欠驱动双关节狗鲨游泳机器人目标到达任务中展现出优异的泛化能力，生成数学上可读的控制架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动的深度强化学习虽能优化复杂控制策略，但缺乏可解释的推理链来连接物理证据与结构化控制架构，这限制了其在物理系统科学发现中的应用。

Method: 利用大语言模型驱动科学智能体，通过迭代代码生成方式，将候选策略部署到物理仿真中，从多模态证据中主动诊断动态行为，并将这些观察转化为逐步的源代码改进，从而自动构建控制器。

Result: 在欠驱动双关节狗鲨游泳机器人的空间目标到达任务中，该智能体从存在单向偏航偏差的初始推进策略出发，自主发现并优化了一个统一控制器，该控制器不仅稳健地捕获所有典型目标，还能泛化到未见过的静态目标和动态曲线追踪轨迹。

Conclusion: 自主科学智能体能够成功地将累积的物理证据转化为稳健、数学上可读的控制策略，同时保持完全可追溯的科学发现过程。

Abstract: While data-intensive deep reinforcement learning can optimize complex control policies, scientific discovery in physical systems fundamentally requires an interpretable chain of reasoning that connects physical evidence to structured control architectures. Here, we present a self-evolving scientific-agent workflow, driven by large language models and iterative code generation, that automates controller construction while preserving strict interpretability and rigorous physical reasoning. Instead of adjusting weights, the agent deploys candidate strategies into physical simulations, actively diagnoses dynamic behaviors from multimodal evidence, and translates these observations into progressive source-code refinements. We demonstrate this framework on a highly non-linear fluid-structure interaction problem: an underactuated, two-joint dogfish swimmer tasked with spatial target reaching using only joint angular accelerations. Starting from a propulsive seed policy that exhibits a one-sided steering bias, the agent autonomously discovers and refines a unified controller that robustly captures all canonical targets. Remarkably, without any retraining or target-specific branching, the synthesized control policy generalizes to unseen static targets and dynamically curved pursuit trajectories. The auditable evolve log reveals an emergent control architecture built upon traveling-wave propulsion, body-frame target guidance, yaw-rate feedback, signed mean-tail curvature, and adaptive cadence relief. Our results show that an autonomous scientific agent can successfully transform accumulated physical evidence into robust, mathematically readable control policy, while maintaining a fully traceable process of scientific discovery.

</details>


### [340] [Trajectory-Refined Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08432)
*Li Jiang,Haoran Xu,Yichuan Ding,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹精炼蒸馏（TRD），通过修正学生模型在教师指导下的完整轨迹输出，解决同策略蒸馏（OPD）中由前缀失败引起的双峰教师混合和梯度碎片化问题。TRD在多个基准测试和不同规模模型上一致优于现有方法，提升了单次尝试准确性和推理覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现同策略蒸馏（OPD）存在一个共同的结构性问题——前缀失败（prefix failure），即密集的逐token监督会导致双峰教师混合（bimodal teacher mixture）和碎片化梯度（fragmented gradients），而传统的token级损失截断或重加权无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出Trajectory-Refined Distillation (TRD)，一种轨迹级的修正方法。TRD在同策略支持范围内，在教师指导下修正学生的完整轨迹输出（rollout）。具体而言：1) 在蒸馏前修正有问题的前缀，从源头缓解前缀失败；2) 即使学生原始轨迹正确，也能通过暴露学生于教师指导下的替代有效推导路径来改善探索。TRD还可扩展到同策略自蒸馏（OPSD）场景。

Result: 在多种基准测试和多个规模的基础模型上，TRD持续优于先前基线方法，在单次尝试准确性和推理覆盖范围方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 轨迹级修正方法比token级损失干预更有效地解决了同策略蒸馏中的前缀失败问题，同时通过提供教师指导下的替代路径增强了探索能力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has become a central post-training tool for large language models (LLMs), providing dense per-token teacher supervision along the student's own rollouts. In this work, we identify a common structural cause underlying OPD, which we call prefix failure. Under prefix failure, dense per-token supervision induces a bimodal teacher mixture and fragmented gradients that token-level loss truncation or reweighting fail to address. This observation motivates us to move beyond token-level loss interventions toward trajectory-level output corrections. We thus propose Trajectory-Refined Distillation (TRD), a trajectory-level correction method that revises the student's rollout under the teacher guidance while within on-policy support. By correcting problematic prefixes before distillation, TRD mitigates prefix failure at its source. Moreover, TRD improves the exploration by exposing the student to alternative valid derivations under teacher guidance, even when the original rolls are already correct. TRD can also be applied to on-policy self-distillation (OPSD), a parameter-sharing variant that uses the student model conditioned on privileged informations as the teacher. Across a wide range of benchmarks and base models at multiple scales, TRD consistently outperforms prior baselines, improving single-attempt accuracy and broadening reasoning coverage. Code is available at https://github.com/louieworth/trd

</details>


### [341] [GIFT: LLM-Guided State-Reward Interface for Financial Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08450)
*Yanyan Wu,Boyi Zhang,Yanlin Liu,Xinyu Fang,Jining Luan,Meiqi Zhang,Jiacheng Liu,Hao Zeng,Dexu Yu,Chang Liu,Hanwen Du,Yongxin Ni,Youhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GIFT是一个LLM引导的框架，用于设计PPO-based金融强化学习中的状态-奖励接口。它通过因子引导的状态增强、风险规则引导的奖励塑造和诊断引导的精炼来提升学习信号质量，在多种市场环境和投资组合场景下显著改善了样本外的风险调整后收益表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳市场中，原始的OHLCV状态和短期收益奖励通常提供不充分的学习接口，限制了强化学习在金融投资组合交易中的表现。因此需要一种方法将金融知识注入状态和奖励设计中，同时约束开放式的生成过程。

Method: GIFT框架包含三个核心模块：1) Factor-guided State Enhancement：从金融因子基元生成状态特征；2) Risk-rule-guided Reward Shaping：从投资组合风险规则生成辅助奖励；3) Diagnostic-guided Refinement：利用PPO rollout诊断结果修正候选接口。框架在评估前固定选定的状态-奖励接口，测试时不调用LLM。

Result: 跨不同市场机制和投资组合场景的滚动窗口实验表明，GIFT在提升学习信号质量和样本外风险调整后投资组合收益方面优于基线方法。

Conclusion: GIFT通过LLM引导的状态-奖励接口设计，有效提升了PPO-based金融强化学习的性能，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Financial portfolio trading is naturally formulated as a reinforcement learning problem, where an agent sequentially rebalances assets under changing market conditions to balance return, risk, and transaction costs. Yet in non-stationary markets, raw OHLCV states and short-horizon return rewards often provide an under-specified learning interface, motivating large language models as a way to inject financial knowledge into state and reward design while constraining open-ended generation. To this end, we propose GIFT, an LLM-guided framework for state-reward interface design in PPO-based financial reinforcement learning. Rather than using the LLM to make trading decisions, GIFT uses Factor-guided State Enhancement to generate state features from financial-factor primitives, Risk-rule-guided Reward Shaping to generate auxiliary rewards from portfolio-risk rules, and Diagnostic-guided Refinement to revise candidate interfaces using PPO rollout diagnostics. After refinement, GIFT fixes the selected state-reward interface before evaluation, with no further LLM queries or interface updates at test time. Comprehensive rolling-window experiments across diverse market regimes and portfolio scenarios demonstrate that GIFT improves learning-signal quality and out-of-sample risk-adjusted portfolio performance over baselines. Code and data are available at: https://github.com/KAG778/GIFT .

</details>


### [342] [A Variability-Based Framework for Interpretable Naming in Formal and Relational Concept Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08477)
*Alain Gutierrez,Marianne Huchard,Pierre Martin,André Miralles,Violaine Prince*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的可配置框架，用于为形式概念分析（FCA）和关系概念分析（RCA）生成的符号抽象自动赋予可读名称，通过可变性模型控制命名时暴露的信息源（如内涵、外延、继承信息、邻近概念、蕴含关系、关系属性），从而解决概念标签技术性强、人类难以理解的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCA和RCA生成的形式化概念结构虽然具有可解释性，但其概念标识符通常为技术性标签，限制了领域专家对概念的理解、导航、验证和重用。因此，需要为这些符号抽象赋予有意义的、人类可读的名称。

Method: (1) 从符号知识表示角度刻画概念命名的语言和术语挑战（歧义性、区分性、简洁性、一致性）；(2) 提出一个基于可变性模型的LLM辅助命名框架，该模型控制命名过程中暴露的信息源，包括内涵、外延、继承信息、邻近概念、蕴含关系和关系属性；(3) 在披萨店领域的小型关系数据集上进行概念验证。

Result: 通过披萨店领域的案例研究，展示了不同配置如何影响LLM生成的概念名称，以及命名可变性如何揭示解释选择、关系依赖关系和底层符号数据中可能存在的建模问题。

Conclusion: 该框架能够显式地将从形式化概念描述到人类可读名称之间的语义选择过程变得透明和可控，有助于领域专家对概念结构的理解和重用，同时命名变体可以暴露出数据建模中的潜在问题。

Abstract: Knowledge extraction from symbolic data often produces abstractions that are formally defined but not immediately interpretable by users. Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) and Relational Concept Analysis (RCA) provide representative settings for this issue: they generate explicit conceptual structures, implications, and relational dependencies from object descriptions and relations. Although these structures are explainable by design, their concepts are often identified by technical labels, which limits their use as human-interpretable knowledge units. Assigning meaningful names to such concepts is therefore a key issue for interpretation, navigation, validation, and reuse by domain experts.
  This paper investigates concept naming in FCA and RCA from a symbolic knowledge representation perspective. We first characterize the linguistic and terminological challenges involved in naming generated symbolic abstractions, including ambiguity, discrimination, concision, and consistency across related concepts. We then propose a configurable framework for LLM-assisted concept naming.
